{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"a11cbfc5-5b1b-4a8a-90ff-e4cf512e7689","agentId":"af0fe4f4b572e87a8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"You are advising on search design for a Kaggle competition (\"Kaggriculture\", $50k prize, deadline 2026-09-30). I need your judgement on a specific design question, not a literature review. Be concrete and be willing to say a proposed approach is wrong.\n\nRepo: /Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture\nGround truth for all rules: reference/kaggriculture.py (read it, do not trust summaries).\nOur routine library (just written, 8 tests green): kagg/search/routines.py, tests/test_routines.py\nAn external model's proposal + our assessment: docs/kimi_take_chain_optimisation_2026-08-04.md\n\n=== ESTABLISHED FACTS (all measured or read from the reference source, not assumed) ===\n\nGAME SHAPE\n- 2 players, 30 days x 24 turns = 720 turns. Board 10x10. Start $3,000.\n- Each turn a player submits: one action per unit owned, plus up to 10 market orders.\n- Our JAX simulator reproduces the reference exactly (300/300 full-length episodes) and is\n  deterministic at weed_chance=0, so a perturbation's effect is an exact finite difference.\n- We can already build a verified, dependency-free submission from a raw 720-turn action array;\n  a replay round-trips to the dollar (ratio 1.0000).\n\nTHE DAILY RESET (this is the key fact and it fires unconditionally every day, reference ~line 847)\n    _drop_inventories_to_shed(private, shed_cap)\n    farm[\"farmer\"] = list(_default_spawn(board_size))   # teleport back to (4,4)\n    farm[\"hands\"] = []                                  # ALL hands dismissed\n    farm[\"hires_today\"] = 0                             # hire cost ladder resets\n    private[\"inventories\"] = [{}]\nConsequences: hands must be RE-HIRED every day; the farmer's position resynchronises to (4,4) every\ndawn; carried inventory is auto-deposited nightly; hire cost is Fibonacci * mult and resets daily\n(so ~$20/day buys a 6-hand crew, ~$600 for the whole season - crew size is nearly free).\n\nGEOMETRY / LAND\n- shed-access tiles (the ONLY tiles where PICKUP / DROP / shed-PLACE are legal) are exactly\n  (4,4), (5,4), (4,5), (5,5).\n- Only quadrant \"NW\" starts unlocked, so only (4,4) is initially usable. The others start LOCKED.\n- LAND_ORDER = [\"NE\",\"SW\",\"SE\"], LAND_PRICES = [1000,2000,4000], bought STRICTLY IN SEQUENCE.\n  _do_buy_land silently no-ops if money < cost.\n- So our agents have played every game on 25 tiles of 100. This is probably the single biggest lever.\n\nTHE ANIMAL CHAIN (why sub-action search provably fails)\n- ANIMALS: GOOSE $300 COOP first_yield 4 interval 1 max_held 4 -> EGG $50\n           COW   $400 PASTURE first_yield 8 interval 2 max_held 6 -> MILK $160\n           SHEEP $500 PASTURE first_yield 6 interval 3 max_held 6 -> WOOL $200\n- consecutive_unfed >= 2 => the animal ESCAPES (deterministic loss of $300-500).\n- FEED requires: standing on the animal's tile, not fed today, and consuming 1 WHEAT ($25) from the\n  acting unit's inventory.\n- pending_care_bonus accumulates +1 per fed+cared day and DUMPS ENTIRELY on the next production day,\n  capped at max_held. So daily HARVEST is near-optimal and \"sell at maturity\" wastes most yield.\n- BUILD_PASTURE / BUILD_COOP cost $0 but require an empty tile.\n- Therefore every partial prefix of the chain is money-negative, and a hill-climber selecting on\n  money rejects step one forever. Our PPO attempt failed the mirror-image way: FEED is masked until\n  an animal exists, so the policy learned to stop buying animals, which re-masks FEED. Same wall.\n\nWHAT WE BUILT AND MEASURED TODAY\nA \"ranch cell\": put the pen ON a shed-access tile, so one unit can PICKUP feed, FEED, CARE, HARVEST\nand DROP without ever moving (5 of the day's 24 turns). Measured in our sim vs a do-nothing opponent:\n    do-nothing baseline   $3,000\n    1x GOOSE on (4,4)     $4,604    net +$1,604\n    1x COW   on (4,4)      $11,119   net +$8,119\n    1x SHEEP on (4,4)      $9,510    net +$6,510\nCaveat we already know: that is an UPPER BOUND. The reason it is so large is that _town_consume\ndrains market inventory while a do-nothing opponent refills nothing, so MILK climbed $212 -> $333\nover the season. A real opponent selling milk removes most of that.\nAlso: the ranch caps at 4 animals, because there are only 4 shed-access tiles. Leaders bank\n$106k-$145k; we bank $23.8k. So livestock is necessary but is NOT where most of the gap is.\n\nCURRENT SEARCH (what I want to replace)\nA (1+lambda) hill-climb: 192 candidates/iter, each 8 random single-slot edits to a recorded seed\ntrajectory, accept on >= (wins across 4 fixed opponents, money as tie-break). 7.0s/iter on an M-series\nMac. It reached 4/4 wins and +7.6% money in ~100 iterations and then flattened. It provably cannot\nfind the animal chain, for the reason above.\n\n=== THE QUESTIONS I ACTUALLY WANT ANSWERED ===\n\n1. GENOME DESIGN. Given the daily reset, days are far more independent than I first assumed: a day's\n   state is fully summarised by (money, shed contents, tile map incl. animals and their fed/care/yield\n   counters, unlocked quadrants, market inventory/prices, town shops). Position and crew do NOT carry\n   across days. Should the genome therefore be a per-day routine assignment (30 slots, each naming a\n   set of routines + parameters), or the flat \"chain tuple\" genome Kimi proposes\n   [(chain_id, start_day, params), ...]? Which decodes more cleanly, and which is easier to search?\n\n2. IS THERE AN EXACT METHOD? This is the question I care most about. If days are nearly independent\n   given that state vector, is this a dynamic-programming / beam-search problem rather than a\n   stochastic-search problem? Concretely: beam search over days, where each node is a full simulator\n   state and each edge is a day-plan drawn from a routine library, keeping the top-K states per day\n   by some value estimate. 30 days x K beam width x |day-plans| exact rollouts of 24 turns each is\n   very cheap in our vmapped JAX sim. What breaks this? My worry is that the value of a state is not\n   monotone in money (a state with less cash but 4 pastures and unlocked land is worth more), so\n   pruning on money is wrong and I do not have an admissible heuristic. How would you rank beam\n   nodes? Is there a defensible surrogate, or does this degenerate?\n\n3. THE LAND FUNDING PROBLEM. Land is $1,000 / $2,000 / $4,000 bought strictly in sequence, and it is\n   probably the biggest single lever (4x the board). But SAVING for a $4,000 purchase looks exactly\n   like doing nothing to any greedy or prefix-locked search, and _do_buy_land silently no-ops when\n   short. Kimi proposes a Pareto frontier on (early money, late money). I suspect land timing must be\n   a GLOBAL genome parameter rather than something discovered locally. What is the principled fix?\n\n4. WHAT IS THE PRIOR RISK? Our own CLAUDE.md draws a line: \"a prior that is a shape does not cap the\n   ceiling; a prior that is a policy does.\" A hand-written routine library is uncomfortably close to\n   the second. How do I keep the strategy in the search and only the mechanics in the decoder? Is\n   there a test that would tell me the library has capped the ceiling?\n\n5. Anything in the established facts above that you think I have MIS-READ, or a lever I have not\n   noticed. Read reference/kaggriculture.py yourself. Be adversarial about this - I have already been\n   wrong twice today about this game's mechanics.\n\nDeliverable: a direct, opinionated answer to 1-5. Prefer concrete schemas, pseudocode and named\nfailure modes over general advice. Where you disagree with me or with Kimi, say so plainly and say\nwhy. Do not write any files."},"uuid":"ff35ac81-76df-4c9c-949b-c4242bf1f489","timestamp":"2026-08-04T09:25:38.352Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture","sessionId":"6e91a562-2c58-4e1d-8c6f-dd43634ac84a","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"humming-hopping-marshmallow"}

Explores user intent, requirements and design before implementation.\n- superpowers:dispatching-parallel-agents: Use when facing 2+ independent tasks that can be worked on without shared state or sequential dependencies\n- superpowers:executing-plans: Use when you have a written implementation plan to execute in a separate session with review checkpoints\n- superpowers:finishing-a-development-branch: Use when implementation is complete, all tests pass, and you need to decide how to integrate the work - guides completion of development work by presenting structured options for merge, PR, or cleanup\n- superpowers:receiving-code-review: Use when receiving code review feedback, before implementing suggestions, especially if feedback seems unclear or technically questionable - requires technical rigor and verification, not performative agreement or blind implementation\n- superpowers:requesting-code-review: Use when completing tasks, implementing major features, or before merging to verify work meets requirements\n- superpowers:subagent-driven-development: Use when executing implementation plans with independent tasks in the current session\n- superpowers:systematic-debugging: Use when encountering any bug, test failure, or unexpected behavior, before proposing fixes\n- superpowers:test-driven-development: Use when implementing any feature or bugfix, before writing implementation code\n- superpowers:using-git-worktrees: Use when starting feature work that needs isolation from current workspace or before executing implementation plans - creates isolated git worktrees with smart directory selection and safety verification\n- superpowers:using-superpowers: Use when starting any conversation - establishes how to find and use skills, requiring Skill tool invocation before ANY response including clarifying questions\n- superpowers:verification-before-completion: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always\n- superpowers:writing-plans: Use when you have a spec or requirements for a multi-step task, before touching code\n- superpowers:writing-skills: Use when creating new skills, editing existing skills, or verifying skills work before deployment\n- andrej-karpathy-skills:karpathy-guidelines: Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Use when writing, reviewing, or refactoring code to avoid overcomplication, make surgical changes, surface assumptions, and define verifiable success criteria.\n- fabric-skills:activator-authoring-cli: Create alerts, notifications, and automated actions on Fabric data and events via Fabric REST API and `az rest` CLI. Use when the user wants to: (1) create, update, or delete an alert or notification flow, (2) send a Teams message, send an email, or run a Fabric item when something happens, (3) connect alert logic to Eventhouse, Eventstream, Real-time Hub, or Digital Twin Builder / Ontology data, (4) adjust thresholds, filters, event triggers, or actions, (5) troubleshoot or change an existing Activator/Reflex definition. Triggers: \"create an alert\", \"notify me when\", \"let me know when\", \"take action when\", \"send me an email when\", \"send a teams message when\", \"run a pipeline when\", \"update an alert\", \"delete an alert\", \"activator rule\"\n- fabric-skills:activator-consumption-cli: Inspect existing alerts, notifications, and automated actions in Fabric via read-only REST API calls using `az rest` CLI. Use when the user wants to: (1) list existing alerts in a workspace, (2) inspect how an alert or notification is configured, (3) read and decode an Activator/Reflex definition (ReflexEntities.json), (4) list rules, sources, and actions behind an alert, (5) understand why an alert fires or what action it takes. Triggers: \"show my alerts\", \"what alerts do I have\", \"inspect this alert\", \"show me the rule\", \"show me the action\", \"show me the source\", \"get reflex definition\", \"list activators\", \"activator details\"\n- fabric-skills:check-updates: Check for skills-for-fabric marketplace updates at session start. Compares local version  against GitHub releases and shows changelog if updates are available. Use when the  user wants to: (1) check for skill updates, (2) see what's new in skills-for-fabric,  (3) verify current version. Triggers: \"check for updates\", \"am I up to date\",  \"what version\", \"update skills\", \"show changelog\".\n- fabric-skills:databricks-migration: Port Databricks notebooks and jobs to Microsoft Fabric. Provides an exhaustive dbutils to notebookutils substitution table: fs operations (mount removal via OneLake Shortcuts), secret scope to Key Vault URL conversion, notebook run and exit, widget replacement with parameter-tagged cells, and library install replacement with Fabric Environments. Covers Unity Catalog three-level namespace reduction to Lakehouse two-level schemas, DBFS path conversion to OneLake, Databricks Jobs to Spark Job Definitions, MLflow tracking URI removal, and Photon to Native Execution Engine substitution. Use when the user wants to: (1) replace dbutils with notebookutils, (2) collapse Unity Catalog namespaces to Lakehouse schemas, (3) convert Databricks Jobs or Delta Live Tables. Triggers: \"migrate from databricks\", \"databricks to fabric\", \"dbutils to notebookutils\", \"dbutils fabric\", \"unity catalog migration\", \"dbfs to onelake\", \"databricks notebook migration\", \"delta live tables fabric\", \"photon native execution\".\n- fabric-skills:dataflows-authoring-cli: Create, update, delete, and manage Fabric Dataflows Gen2 artifacts with Power Query M mashup definitions via CLI (az rest / curl). Uses az rest and curl against the Fabric REST API to author definitions containing base64-encoded mashup.pq, queryMetadata.json, and .platform parts. Supports creating dataflows with inline definitions, modifying mashup queries, binding connections, triggering Execute refresh jobs with typed parameter overrides, and exporting definitions for CI/CD. Use when the user wants to: (1) create a new Dataflow Gen2 with Power Query M queries, (2) update a dataflow mashup definition, (3) trigger a dataflow refresh job, (4) bind or manage dataflow connections, (5) set up CI/CD via definition export and import, (6) delete a dataflow, (7) configure staging destinations. Triggers: \"create dataflow\", \"author dataflow\", \"Power Query M\", \"mashup document\", \"update dataflow definition\", \"refresh dataflow\", \"dataflow connection\", \"ETL dataflow\", \"dataflow CI/CD\".\n- fabric-skills:dataflows-consumption-cli: Monitor, inspect, and discover Fabric Dataflows Gen2 via read-only CLI operations (az rest / curl). List dataflows across workspaces, decode base64 definitions to inspect Power Query M queries and queryMetadata.json, discover typed parameters with defaults, poll refresh operations for status, retrieve job history with timing and error details, and classify queries by staging settings. Use when the user wants to: (1) list dataflows, (2) inspect a dataflow definition and decode its mashup, (3) discover parameters, (4) check refresh status, (5) retrieve job history, (6) analyze staging settings, (7) examine connections and data source bindings. Triggers: \"dataflow status\", \"refresh history\", \"dataflow monitor\", \"list dataflows\", \"dataflow parameters\", \"explore dataflow\", \"inspect dataflow\", \"dataflow run status\".\n- fabric-skills:dataflows-save-as-authoring-cli: Assess, plan, and execute dataflow Gen1 → Gen2.1 CI/CD save-as operations via CLI (az rest / curl) against both Power BI REST and Fabric REST APIs. Scan workspaces or entire tenants for Gen1 dataflows, evaluate save-as readiness with seven risk signals (incremental refresh, BYOSA storage, Power Automate triggers, pipeline dependencies, linked entities, DirectQuery, caller-not-owner), produce a Save-As Readiness Snapshot (markdown + JSON), and invoke the SaveAsNativeArtifact API to create upgraded Gen2.1 copies of Gen1 dataflows. Use when the user wants to: (1) discover Gen1 dataflows in a workspace or tenant, (2) assess save-as readiness and risk signals, (3) upgrade or migrate Gen1 into a Gen2.1 copy, (4) validate post-save-as data integrity, (5) detect residual Gen1 references. Triggers: \"save Gen1 dataflow\", \"convert dataflow Gen1\", \"upgrade dataflow\", \"migrate dataflow\", \"dataflow readiness\", \"Gen1 to Gen2\", \"dataflow save-as assessment\", \"saveAsNativeArtifact\", \"dataflow save-as scan\".\n- fabric-skills:e2e-medallion-architecture: Implement end-to-end Medallion Architecture (Bronze/Silver/Gold) lakehouse patterns in Microsoft Fabric using PySpark, Delta Lake, and Fabric Pipelines. Use when the user wants to: (1) design a Bronze/Silver/Gold data lakehouse, (2) set up multi-layer workspace with lakehouses for each tier, (3) build ingestion-to-analytics pipelines with data quality enforcement, (4) optimize Spark configurations per medallion layer, (5) orchestrate Bronze-to-Silver-to-Gold flows via notebooks. Triggers: \"medallion architecture\", \"bronze silver gold\", \"lakehouse layers\", \"e2e data pipeline\", \"end-to-end lakehouse\", \"data lakehouse pattern\", \"multi-layer lakehouse\", \"build medallion\", \"setup medallion\".\n- fabric-skills:eventhouse-authoring-cli: Execute KQL management commands (table management, ingestion, policies, functions, materialized views) against Fabric Eventhouse and KQL Databases via CLI. Use when the user wants to:\n  1. Create or alter KQL tables, columns, or functions\n  2. Ingest data into an Eventhouse (inline, from storage, streaming)\n  3. Configure retention, caching, or partitioning policies\n  4. Create or manage materialized views and update policies\n  5. Manage data mappings for ingestion pipelines\n  6. Deploy KQL schema via scripts\nTriggers: \"create kql table\", \"kql ingestion\", \"ingest into eventhouse\", \"kql function\", \"materialized view\", \"kql retention policy\", \"eventhouse schema\", \"kql authoring\", \"create eventhouse table\", \"kql mapping\"\n- fabric-skills:eventhouse-consumption-cli: Run KQL queries against Fabric Eventhouse for real-time intelligence and time-series analytics using `az rest` against the Kusto REST API. Covers KQL operators (where, summarize, join, render), Eventhouse schema discovery (.show tables), time-series patterns with bin(), and ingestion monitoring. Use when the user wants to:\n  1. Run read-only KQL queries against an Eventhouse or KQL Database\n  2. Discover Eventhouse table schema and metadata\n  3. Analyse real-time or time-series data with KQL operators\n  4. Monitor ingestion health and active KQL queries\n  5. Export KQL results to JSON\nTriggers: \"kql query\", \"kusto query\", \"eventhouse query\", \"kql database\", \"real-time intelligence\", \"time-series kql\", \"query eventhouse\", \"explore eventhouse\", \"show tables kql\"\n- fabric-skills:eventstream-authoring-cli\n- fabric-skills:eventstream-consumption-cli\n- fabric-skills:hdinsight-migration\n- fabric-skills:powerbi-authoring-cli\n- fabric-skills:powerbi-consumption-cli: The ONLY supported path for read-only Microsoft Fabric Power BI semantic model (formerly \"Power BI dataset\") query interactions. Execute DAX queries via the MCP server ExecuteQuery tool to: (1) discover semantic model metadata (tables, columns, measures, relationships, hierarchies, etc.) and their properties, (2) retrieve data from a semantic model. Triggers: \"DAX query\", \"semantic model metadata\", \"list semantic model tables\", \"run EVALUATE\", \"get measure expression\".\n- fabric-skills:search-consumption-cli\n- fabric-skills:spark-authoring-cli\n- fabric-skills:spark-consumption-cli\n- fabric-skills:spark-operations-cli\n- fabric-skills:sqldw-authoring-cli\n- fabric-skills:sqldw-consumption-cli\n- fabric-skills:sqldw-operations-cli\n- fabric-skills:synapse-migration\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review\n- {\"cost\": 300, \"structure\": \"COOP\",    \"first_yield_day\": 4, \"interval\": 1, \"max_held\": 4, \"product\": \"EGG\"},\n21\t    \"COW\":   {\"cost\": 400, \"structure\": \"PASTURE\", 6, \"product\": \"WOOL\"},\n23\t}\n24\t\n25\tPRODUCTS = [\"WHEAT\", \"CARROT\", \"TOMATO\", \"STRAWBERRY\", \"MELON\", \"EGG\", \"MILK\", \"WOOL\", \"FERTILIZER\"]\n26\t\n27\t# Pricing model:\n28\t#     price(inv) = base + sign * amp * f(|inv - I0|)\n29\t#     sign = +1 below I0 (scarcity), -1 above I0 (glut)\n30\t#     amp  = target * base / f(T)            (derived; selling T units moves\n31\t#                                             price by `target` * base)\n32\t#     T    = production capacity of one 5x5 field over a 24-day game at\n33\t#            optimal watering, no fertilizer (animal T pre-discounted 30% for\n34\t#            wheat-feed overhead)\n35\t#     f    in {linear, sq, sqrt, log, log10}; log uses ln(1+x) so f(0)=0\n36\t# Floored at PRICE_FLOOR.\n37\tMARKET_I0 = 10000\n38\tPRICE_FLOOR = 1\n39\t\n40\tMARKET_PARAMS = {\n41\t    \"WHEAT\":      {\"base\":  25, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 400, \"below_func\": \"sqrt\",   \"below_target\": 0.80, \"above_func\": \"log\",    \"above_target\": 0.20},\n42\t    \"CARROT\":     {\"base\":  35, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 450, \"below_func\": \"log\",    \"below_target\": 0.20, \"above_func\": \"sqrt\",   \"above_target\": 0.70},\n43\t    \"TOMATO\":     {\"base\":  60, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 200, \"below_func\": \"linear\", \"below_target\": 0.40, \"above_func\": \"sqrt\",   \"above_target\": 0.60},\n44\t    \"STRAWBERRY\": {\"base\": 120, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 100, \"below_func\": \"sqrt\",   \"below_target\": 0.70, \"above_func\": \"linear\", \"above_target\": 1.60},\n45\t    \"MELON\":      {\"base\": 250, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 300, \"below_func\": \"log\",    \"below_target\": 0.20, \"above_func\": \"sq\",     \"above_target\": 3.60},\n46\t    \"EGG\":        {\"base\":  50, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 332, \"below_func\": \"linear\", \"below_target\": 0.40, \"above_func\": \"log\",    \"above_target\": 0.20},\n47\t    \"MILK\":       {\"base\": 160, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 122, \"below_func\": \"sqrt\",   \"below_target\": 0.60, \"above_func\": \"linear\", \"above_target\": 1.60},\n48\t    \"WOOL\":       {\"base\": 200, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 105, \"below_func\": \"log\",    \"below_target\": 0.20, \"above_func\": \"sq\",     \"above_target\": 3.20},\n49\t    \"FERTILIZER\": {\"base\": 100, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 200, \"below_func\": \"linear\", \"below_target\": 0.40, \"above_func\": \"linear\", \"above_target\": 0.40},\n50\t}\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef _shape(func, x):\n54\t    x = max(0.0, x)\n55\t    if func == \"linear\": return x\n56\t    if func == \"sq\":     return x * x\n57\t    if func == \"sqrt\":   return math.sqrt(x)\n58\t    if func == \"log\":    return math.log(1.0 + x)\n59\t    if func == \"log10\":  return math.log10(1.0 + x)\n60\t    return x\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tdef _resolve_market_params(overrides):\n64\t    \"\"\"Merge per-resource overrides onto MARKET_PARAMS defaults (sparse).\"\"\"\n65\t    resolved = {item: dict(p) for item, p in MARKET_PARAMS.items()}\n66\t    if not overrides:\n67\t        return resolved\n68\t    for item, patch in overrides.items():\n69\t        if item in resolved and isinstance(patch, dict):\n70\t            resolved[item].update(patch)\n71\t    return resolved\n72\t\n73\t# (dx, dy); y grows downward.\n74\tFARMER_MOVES = {\n75\t    \"NORTH\": (0, -1),\n76\t    \"SOUTH\": (0, 1),\n77\t    \"EAST\":  (1, 0),\n78\t    \"WEST\":  (-1, 0),\n79\t}\n80\t\n81\t# NW is always unlocked; players unlock the rest in this order.\n82\tLAND_ORDER = [\"NE\", \"SW\", \"SE\"]\n83\tLAND_PRICES = [1000, 2000, 4000]\n84\t\n85\t# n-th hire of the day -> cost = FARM_HAND_COST_MULT * fib(n), where\n86\t# fib starts 1, 1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, ... Configurable via `farmHandCostMult`.\n87\tFARM_HAND_COST_MULT = 1\n88\t\n89\tSHOPS = {\n90\t    \"BAKERY\":         [\"EGG\", \"WHEAT\"],\n91\t    \"PIZZA_SHOP\":     [\"MILK\", \"TOMATO\", \"WHEAT\"],\n92\t    \"BRUNCH_SPOT\":    [\"EGG\", \"WHEAT\", \"STRAWBERRY\"],\n93\t    \"YARN_STORE\":     [\"WOOL\"],\n94\t    \"ICE_CREAM_SHOP\": [\"STRAWBERRY\", \"MILK\", \"WHEAT\"],\n95\t    \"PET_CAFE\":       [\"CARROT\"],\n96\t    \"SMOOTHIE_SHOP\":  [\"STRAWBERRY\", \"MILK\"],\n97\t    \"FARMERS_MARKET\": [\"WHEAT\", \"CARROT\", \"TOMATO\", \"STRAWBERRY\"],\n98\t}\n99\t\n100\tTOWN_CENTER_PRODUCTS = [p for p in PRODUCTS if p != \"FERTILIZER\"]\n101\t\n102\t# Town center demand schedule: (day_threshold, multiplier), highest threshold first.\n103\tTOWN_CENTER_DEMAND_SCHEDULE = [(20, 4), (10, 2), (0, 1)]\n104\t\n105\t\n106\tdef get(d, key, default):\n107\t    if isinstance(d, dict):\n108\t        return d.get(key, default)\n109\t    return getattr(d, key, default)\n110\t\n111\t\n112\tdef _quadrant_of(x, y, board_size):\n113\t    half = board_size // 2\n114\t    return (\"N\" if y < half else \"S\") + (\"W\" if x < half else \"E\")\n115\t\n116\t\n117\tdef _shed_access_tiles(board_size):\n118\t    \"\"\"Four inner-corner tiles around the shed, in NWSE order.\"\"\"\n119\t    half = board_size // 2\n120\t    return [(half - 1, half - 1), (half, half - 1), (half - 1, half), (half, half)]\n121\t\n122\t\n123\tdef _is_shed_adjacent(pos, board_size):\n124\t    return tuple(pos) in {(x, y) for (x, y) in _shed_access_tiles(board_size)}\n125\t\n126\t\n127\tdef _new_farm(board_size, starting_money):\n128\t    return {\n129\t        \"money\": float(starting_money),\n130\t        # tiles[y][x] = None (empty unlocked) | \"LOCKED\" | dict structure\n131\t        \"tiles\": [\n132\t            [_initial_tile(x, y, board_size) for x in range(board_size)]\n133\t            for y in range(board_size)\n134\t        ],\n135\t        \"farmer\": list(_default_spawn(board_size)),\n136\t        \"hands\": [],\n137\t        \"unlocked_quadrants\": [\"NW\"],\n138\t        \"hires_today\": 0,\n139\t    }\n140\t\n141\t\n142\tdef _initial_tile(x, y, board_size):\n143\t    return None if _quadrant_of(x, y, board_size) == \"NW\" else \"LOCKED\"\n144\t\n145\t\n146\tdef _default_spawn(board_size):\n147\t    \"\"\"First free shed-access tile, NWSE preference.\"\"\"\n148\t    for tile in _shed_access_tiles(board_size):\n149\t        if _quadrant_of(tile[0], tile[1], board_size) == \"NW\":\n150\t            return tile\n151\t    return (0, 0)\n152\t\n153\t\n154\tdef _new_private():\n155\t    return {\n156\t        \"shed\": {item: 0 for item in PRODUCTS + list(ANIMALS)},\n157\t        \"seeds\": {crop: 0 for crop in CROPS},\n158\t        # inventories[0] = main farmer; hands appended/removed each day.\n159\t        \"inventories\": [{}],\n160\t    }\n161\t\n162\t\n163\tdef _new_market(params=None):\n164\t    params = params or MARKET_PARAMS\n165\t    inv = {item: params[item][\"I0\"] for item in PRODUCTS}\n166\t    prices = {item: params[item][\"base\"] for item in PRODUCTS}\n167\t    market = {\"inventory\": inv, \"prices\": prices}\n168\t    if params is not MARKET_PARAMS:\n169\t        market[\"params\"] = params\n170\t    return market\n171\t\n172\t\n173\tdef _new_town():\n174\t    return {\"unlocked_shops\": []}\n175\t\n176\t\n177\tdef market_price(item, inventory, params=None):\n178\t    \"\"\"Floor at PRICE_FLOOR.\"\"\"\n179\t    p = (params or MARKET_PARAMS)[item]\n180\t    base = p[\"base\"]\n181\t    I0 = p[\"I0\"]\n182\t    T = p[\"T\"]\n183\t    if inventory < I0:\n184\t        f = p[\"below_func\"]\n185\t        amp = p[\"below_target\"] * base / _shape(f, T)\n186\t        price = base + amp * _shape(f, I0 - inventory)\n187\t    else:\n188\t        f = p[\"above_func\"]\n189\t        amp = p[\"above_target\"] * base / _shape(f, T)\n190\t        price = base - amp * _shape(f, inventory - I0)\n191\t    return max(PRICE_FLOOR, int(round(price)))\n192\t\n193\t\n194\tdef _refresh_prices(market):\n195\t    params = market.get(\"params\")\n196\t    for item in PRODUCTS:\n197\t        market[\"prices\"][item] = market_price(item, market[\"inventory\"][item], params)\n198\t\n199\t\n200\tdef _new_plant(crop, day, turns_per_day):\n201\t    cd = CROPS[crop]\n202\t    return {\n203\t        \"kind\": \"PLANT\",\n204\t        \"crop\": crop,\n205\t        \"planted_day\": day,\n206\t        \"watered_today\": False,\n207\t        \"consecutive_unwatered\": 1,  # planting day counts as unwatered\n208\t        \"yield_units\": 0 if cd[\"ongoing\"] else 1,\n209\t        \"max_lifespan_step\": (-1 if cd[\"ongoing\"] else (day + cd[\"max_yield_day\"] + 1) * turns_per_day),\n210\t        \"fertilized_until_day\": -1,\n211\t    }\n212\t\n213\t\n214\tdef _new_animal(animal, day):\n215\t    a = ANIMALS[animal]\n216\t    return {\n217\t        \"kind\": a[\"structure\"],\n218\t        \"animal\": animal,\n219\t        \"placed_day\": day,\n220\t        \"yield_units\": 0,\n221\t        \"consecutive_unfed\": 0,\n222\t        \"fed_today\": False,\n223\t        \"cared_today\": False,\n224\t        \"fertilizer_available\": False,\n225\t        \"pending_care_bonus\": 0,\n226\t    }\n227\t\n228\t\n229\tdef _initialize(state, env):\n230\t    configuration = env.configuration\n231\t    num_agents = len(state)\n232\t    obs0 = state[0].observation\n233\t\n234\t    seed = resolve_episode_seed(env)\n235\t\n236\t    board_size = int(get(configuration, \"boardSize\", 10))\n237\t    starting_money = int(get(configuration, \"startingMoney\", 3000))\n238\t\n239\t    farms = [_new_farm(board_size, starting_money) for _ in range(num_agents)]\n240\t    privates = [_new_private() for _ in range(num_agents)]\n241\t    market_overrides = get(configuration, \"marketParams\", None)\n242\t    resolved_params = _resolve_market_params(market_overrides) if market_overrides else None\n243\t    market = _new_market(resolved_params)\n244\t    town = _new_town()\n245\t\n246\t    obs0.farms = farms\n247\t    obs0.market = market\n248\t    obs0.town = town\n249\t    obs0.day = 0\n250\t    obs0.hour = 0\n251\t\n252\t    for i in range(num_agents):\n253\t        state[i].observation.player = i\n254\t        state[i].observation.private = privates[i]\n255\t        if i > 0:\n256\t            state[i].observation.farms = farms\n257\t            state[i].observation.market = market\n258\t            state[i].observation.town = town\n259\t            state[i].observation.day = 0\n260\t            state[i].observation.hour = 0\n261\t\n262\t\n263\tdef _farmer_position(farm, idx):\n264\t    \"\"\"idx 0 = main farmer, 1+ = hand index.\"\"\"\n265\t    if idx == 0:\n266\t        return farm[\"farmer\"]\n267\t    return farm[\"hands\"][idx - 1] if idx - 1 < len(farm[\"hands\"]) else None\n268\t\n269\t\n270\tdef _set_farmer_position(farm, idx, pos):\n271\t    if idx == 0:\n272\t        farm[\"farmer\"] = list(pos)\n273\t    else:\n274\t        farm[\"hands\"][idx - 1] = list(pos)\n275\t\n276\t\n277\tdef _farmer_inventory(private, idx):\n278\t    \"\"\"Inventories list is [main_farmer, *hands]; grow it if idx is past the end.\"\"\"\n279\t    while len(private[\"inventories\"]) <= idx:\n280\t        private[\"inventories\"].append({})\n281\t    return private[\"inventories\"][idx]\n282\t\n283\t\n284\tdef _inv_add(inv, item, n=1):\n285\t    inv[item] = inv.get(item, 0) + n\n286\t\n287\t\n288\tdef _inv_take(inv, item, n=1):\n289\t    if inv.get(item, 0) < n:\n290\t        return False\n291\t    inv[item] -= n\n292\t    if inv[item] == 0:\n293\t        del inv[item]\n294\t    return True\n295\t\n296\t\n297\tdef _apply_unit_action(farm, private, idx, action, board_size, day, turns_per_day, shed_capacity=100):\n298\t    \"\"\"Process one farmer/hand's action. Invalid / illegal actions are silent no-ops.\"\"\"\n299\t    if not isinstance(action, list) or not action:\n300\t        return\n301\t    op = action[0]\n302\t    pos = _farmer_position(farm, idx)\n303\t    if pos is None:\n304\t        return\n305\t    fx, fy = pos[0], pos[1]\n306\t    inv = _farmer_inventory(private, idx)\n307\t\n308\t    if op in FARMER_MOVES:\n309\t        dx, dy = FARMER_MOVES[op]\n310\t        nx, ny = fx + dx, fy + dy\n311\t        if not (0 <= nx < board_size and 0 <= ny < board_size):\n312\t            return\n313\t        if farm[\"tiles\"][ny][nx] == \"LOCKED\":\n314\t            return\n315\t        _set_farmer_position(farm, idx, (nx, ny))\n316\t        return\n317\t\n318\t    if op == \"PASS\":\n319\t        return\n320\t\n321\t    tile = farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx]\n322\t    if tile == \"LOCKED\":\n323\t        return\n324\t\n325\t    if op == \"DROP\":\n326\t        if not _is_shed_adjacent((fx, fy), board_size):\n327\t            return\n328\t        shed = private[\"shed\"]\n329\t        for item, n in list(inv.items()):\n330\t            if n <= 0:\n331\t                del inv[item]\n332\t                continue\n333\t            room = max(0, shed_capacity - sum(shed.values()))\n334\t            take = min(n, room)\n335\t            if take > 0:\n336\t                shed[item] = shed.get(item, 0) + take\n337\t            del inv[item]\n338\t        return\n339\t\n340\t    if op == \"PICKUP\":\n341\t        if not _is_shed_adjacent((fx, fy), board_size):\n342\t            return\n343\t        if len(action) < 2:\n344\t            return\n345\t        item = action[1]\n346\t        n = int(action[2]) if len(action) >= 3 else 1\n347\t        if n <= 0:\n348\t            return\n349\t        # Seeds live in private[\"seeds\"] and are consumed directly by PLANT;\n350\t        # they never pass through farmer inventory or the shed.\n351\t        available = private[\"shed\"].get(item, 0)\n352\t        n = min(n, available)\n353\t        if n <= 0:\n354\t            return\n355\t        private[\"shed\"][item] -= n\n356\t        _inv_add(inv, item, n)\n357\t        return\n358\t\n359\t    if op == \"PLANT\":\n360\t        if len(action) < 2:\n361\t            return\n362\t        crop = action[1]\n363\t        if crop not in CROPS:\n364\t            return\n365\t        if tile is not None:\n366\t            return\n367\t        if private[\"seeds\"].get(crop, 0) <= 0:\n368\t            return\n369\t        private[\"seeds\"][crop] -= 1\n370\t        farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx] = _new_plant(crop, day, turns_per_day)\n371\t        return\n372\t\n373\t    if op == \"WATER\":\n374\t        if not (isinstance(tile, dict) and tile.get(\"kind\") == \"PLANT\"):\n375\t            return\n376\t        if tile[\"watered_today\"]:\n377\t            return\n378\t        tile[\"watered_today\"] = True\n379\t        crop_data = CROPS[tile[\"crop\"]]\n380\t        if not crop_data[\"ongoing\"]:\n381\t            age_days = day - tile[\"planted_day\"]\n382\t            window_start = (crop_data[\"max_yield_day\"] + 1) // 2\n383\t            if window_start <= age_days <= crop_data[\"max_yield_day\"]:\n384\t                bonus = 2 if tile[\"fertilized_until_day\"] >= day else 1\n385\t                tile[\"yield_units\"] = min(crop_data[\"max_yield\"], tile[\"yield_units\"] + bonus)\n386\t        return\n387\t\n388\t    if op == \"HARVEST\":\n389\t        if not isinstance(tile, dict):\n390\t            return\n391\t        if tile.get(\"yield_units\", 0) <= 0:\n392\t            return\n393\t        if tile.get(\"kind\") == \"PLANT\":\n394\t            crop_data = CROPS[tile[\"crop\"]]\n395\t            if day - tile[\"planted_day\"] < crop_data[\"first_yield_day\"]:\n396\t                # Ongoing crops only accumulate yield_units after first_yield_day,\n397\t                # so reaching here with yield_units > 0 indicates a bug.\n398\t                if crop_data[\"ongoing\"]:\n399\t                    print(\n400\t                        f\"WARNING: HARVEST on immature ongoing {tile['crop']} \"\n401\t                        f\"(planted day {tile['planted_day']}, current day {day}, \"\n402\t                        f\"first_yield_day {crop_data['first_yield_day']}, \"\n403\t                        f\"yield_units {tile['yield_units']}); should never happen\"\n404\t                    )\n405\t                return\n406\t            units = tile[\"yield_units\"]\n407\t            tile[\"yield_units\"] = 0\n408\t            _inv_add(inv, tile[\"crop\"], units)\n409\t            if not crop_data[\"ongoing\"]:\n410\t                farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx] = None\n411\t        elif \"animal\" in tile:\n412\t            units = tile[\"yield_units\"]\n413\t            tile[\"yield_units\"] = 0\n414\t            _inv_add(inv, ANIMALS[tile[\"animal\"]][\"product\"], units)\n415\t        return\n416\t\n417\t    if op == \"FERTILIZE\":\n418\t        if not (isinstance(tile, dict) and tile.get(\"kind\") == \"PLANT\"):\n419\t            return\n420\t        if not _inv_take(inv, \"FERTILIZER\", 1):\n421\t            return\n422\t        # Active for `day`, `day+1`, `day+2` (3 days inclusive).\n423\t        tile[\"fertilized_until_day\"] = max(tile.get(\"fertilized_until_day\", -1), day + 2)\n424\t        return\n425\t\n426\t    if op == \"DIG\":\n427\t        if tile is None:\n428\t            return\n429\t        # Removes plants, weeds, empty coop/pasture. Does NOT remove a placed animal.\n430\t        if isinstance(tile, dict) and \"animal\" in tile:\n431\t            return\n432\t        farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx] = None\n433\t        return\n434\t\n435\t    if op == \"BUILD_COOP\":\n436\t        if tile is not None:\n437\t            return\n438\t        farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx] = {\"kind\": \"COOP\"}\n439\t        return\n440\t\n441\t    if op == \"BUILD_PASTURE\":\n442\t        if tile is not None:\n443\t            return\n444\t        farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx] = {\"kind\": \"PASTURE\"}\n445\t        return\n446\t\n447\t    if op == \"PLACE\":\n448\t        if len(action) < 2:\n449\t            return\n450\t        item = action[1]\n451\t        # Animal placement: standing on a matching unoccupied structure.\n452\t        if (\n453\t            item in ANIMALS\n454\t            and isinstance(tile, dict)\n455\t            and tile.get(\"kind\") == ANIMALS[item][\"structure\"]\n456\t            and \"animal\" not in tile\n457\t        ):\n458\t            if _inv_take(inv, item, 1):\n459\t                farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx] = _new_animal(item, day)\n460\t            return\n461\t        # Shed drop: orthogonally adjacent to the shed; obeys shedCapacity.\n462\t        if _is_shed_adjacent((fx, fy), board_size):\n463\t            n = int(action[2]) if len(action) >= 3 else 1\n464\t            if n <= 0:\n465\t                return\n466\t            n = min(n, inv.get(item, 0))\n467\t            if n <= 0:\n468\t                return\n469\t            current = sum(private[\"shed\"].values())\n470\t            room = max(0, shed_capacity - current)\n471\t            n = min(n, room)\n472\t            if n <= 0:\n473\t                return\n474\t            inv[item] -= n\n475\t            if inv[item] == 0:\n476\t                del inv[item]\n477\t            private[\"shed\"][item] = private[\"shed\"].get(item, 0) + n\n478\t        return\n479\t\n480\t    if op == \"FEED\":\n481\t        if not (isinstance(tile, dict) and \"animal\" in tile):\n482\t            return\n483\t        if tile[\"fed_today\"]:\n484\t            return\n485\t        if not _inv_take(inv, \"WHEAT\", 1):\n486\t            return\n487\t        tile[\"fed_today\"] = True\n488\t        return\n489\t\n490\t    if op == \"COLLECT_FERTILIZER\":\n491\t        if not (isinstance(tile, dict) and \"animal\" in tile):\n492\t            return\n493\t        if not tile[\"fertilizer_available\"]:\n494\t            return\n495\t        tile[\"fertilizer_available\"] = False\n496\t        _inv_add(inv, \"FERTILIZER\", 1)\n497\t        return\n498\t\n499\t    if op == \"CARE\":\n500\t        if not (isinstance(tile, dict) and \"animal\" in tile):\n501\t            return\n502\t        if tile[\"cared_today\"]:\n503\t            return\n504\t        tile[\"cared_today\"] = True\n505\t        return\n506\t\n507\t\n508\tdef _spawn_hand(farm, board_size):\n509\t    \"\"\"First free shed-access tile (NWSE order); ties broken by min occupancy.\"\"\"\n510\t    occupants = {tile: 0 for tile in _shed_access_tiles(board_size)}\n511\t    all_pos = [tuple(farm[\"farmer\"])] + [tuple(p) for p in farm[\"hands\"]]\n512\t    for pos in all_pos:\n513\t        if pos in occupants:\n514\t            occupants[pos] += 1\n515\t    best = sorted(occupants.items(), key=lambda kv: (kv[1], _shed_access_tiles(board_size).index(kv[0])))\n516\t    return list(best[0][0])\n517\t\n518\t\n519\tdef _process_market(state, env):\n520\t    \"\"\"Per-unit lockstep: at each step, quote both players' current-unit prices, then commit both.\"\"\"\n521\t    obs0 = state[0].observation\n522\t    market = obs0.market\n523\t    farms = obs0.farms\n524\t    privates = [s.observation.private for s in state]\n525\t    board_size = int(get(env.configuration, \"boardSize\", 10))\n526\t    max_orders = max(1, int(get(env.configuration, \"maxMarketOrdersPerTurn\", 10)))\n527\t    hire_mult = int(get(env.configuration, \"farmHandCostMult\", FARM_HAND_COST_MULT))\n528\t\n529\t    queues = []\n530\t    for s in state:\n531\t        action = s.action if isinstance(s.action, dict) else {}\n532\t        m = action.get(\"market\", []) if isinstance(action, dict) else []\n533\t        q = list(m) if isinstance(m, list) else []\n534\t        queues.append(q[:max_orders])\n535\t\n536\t    max_len = max((len(q) for q in queues), default=0)\n537\t    for i in range(max_len):\n538\t        order_states = []\n539\t        for player_id, q in enumerate(queues):\n540\t            ostate = None\n541\t            if i < len(q):\n542\t                ostate = _parse_order(q[i])\n543\t            order_states.append(ostate)\n544\t\n545\t        # Atomic orders (HIRE, BUY_LAND): handle once, in player order.\n546\t        for player_id, ostate in enumerate(order_states):\n547\t            if ostate is None:\n548\t                continue\n549\t            op = ostate[\"type\"]\n550\t            if op == \"HIRE\":\n551\t                _do_hire(farms[player_id], privates[player_id], board_size, hire_mult)\n552\t                order_states[player_id] = None\n553\t            elif op == \"BUY_LAND\":\n554\t                _do_buy_land(farms[player_id], board_size)\n555\t                order_states[player_id] = None\n556\t\n557\t        # Per-unit lockstep loop for SELL / BUY_*.\n558\t        idx_esc = 0\n559\t        while True:\n560\t            idx_esc += 1\n561\t            if idx_esc >= 100_000:\n562\t                print(\"WARNING: kaggriculture market loop exceeded 100k iterations; aborting\")\n563\t                break\n564\t            quoted = [None, None]\n565\t            for player_id, ostate in enumerate(order_states):\n566\t                if ostate is None or ostate[\"remaining\"] <= 0:\n567\t                    continue\n568\t                op = ostate[\"type\"]\n569\t                item = ostate[\"item\"]\n570\t                if op == \"SELL\" and item in PRODUCTS:\n571\t                    quoted[player_id] = (\"SELL\", item, market_price(item, market[\"inventory\"][item], market.get(\"params\")), ostate)\n572\t                elif op == \"BUY_PRODUCT\" and item in (\"WHEAT\", \"FERTILIZER\"):\n573\t                    # Quote at post-buy inventory so a buy/sell round-trip\n574\t                    # against an unchanged market nets zero.\n575\t                    quoted[player_id] = (\"BUY_PRODUCT\", item, market_price(item, market[\"inventory\"][item] - 1, market.get(\"params\")), ostate)\n576\t                elif op == \"BUY_SEED\" and item in CROPS:\n577\t                    quoted[player_id] = (\"BUY_SEED\", item, CROPS[item][\"seed\"], ostate)\n578\t                elif op == \"BUY_ANIMAL\" and item in ANIMALS:\n579\t                    quoted[player_id] = (\"BUY_ANIMAL\", item, ANIMALS[item][\"cost\"], ostate)\n580\t                else:\n581\t                    order_states[player_id] = None  # malformed sub-op; abort\n582\t\n583\t            if all(q is None for q in quoted):\n584\t                break\n585\t\n586\t            # Both players see the same pre-commit inventory for this unit.\n587\t            committed_any = False\n588\t            for player_id, q in enumerate(quoted):\n589\t                if q is None:\n590\t                    continue\n591\t                op, item, price, ostate = q\n592\t                ok = _commit_unit(op, item, price, farms[player_id], privates[player_id], market)\n593\t                if ok:\n594\t                    ostate[\"remaining\"] -= 1\n595\t                    committed_any = True\n596\t                else:\n597\t                    order_states[player_id] = None  # can't continue this order\n598\t\n599\t            if not committed_any:\n600\t                break\n601\t\n602\t        _refresh_prices(market)\n603\t\n604\t\n605\tdef _parse_order(order):\n606\t    if not isinstance(order, list) or not order:\n607\t        return None\n608\t    op = order[0]\n609\t    if op == \"HIRE\":\n610\t        return {\"type\": \"HIRE\"}\n611\t    if op == \"BUY_LAND\":\n612\t        return {\"type\": \"BUY_LAND\"}\n613\t    if op in (\"BUY_SEED\", \"BUY_PRODUCT\", \"BUY_ANIMAL\", \"SELL\"):\n614\t        if len(order) < 3:\n615\t            return None\n616\t        try:\n617\t            n = int(order[2])\n618\t        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n619\t            return None\n620\t        if n <= 0:\n621\t            return None\n622\t        return {\"type\": op, \"item\": order[1], \"remaining\": n}\n623\t    return None\n624\t\n625\t\n626\tdef _commit_unit(op, item, price, farm, private, market):\n627\t    if op == \"SELL\":\n628\t        if private[\"shed\"].get(item, 0) <= 0:\n629\t            return False\n630\t        private[\"shed\"][item] -= 1\n631\t        farm[\"money\"] += price\n632\t        # Sales at $1 do not increase market supply.\n633\t        if price > 1:\n634\t            market[\"inventory\"][item] += 1\n635\t        return True\n636\t    if op == \"BUY_PRODUCT\":\n637\t        if farm[\"money\"] < price:\n638\t            return False\n639\t        farm[\"money\"] -= price\n640\t        private[\"shed\"][item] = private[\"shed\"].get(item, 0) + 1\n641\t        market[\"inventory\"][item] -= 1\n642\t        return True\n643\t    if op == \"BUY_SEED\":\n644\t        if farm[\"money\"] < price:\n645\t            return False\n646\t        farm[\"money\"] -= price\n647\t        private[\"seeds\"][item] = private[\"seeds\"].get(item, 0) + 1\n648\t        return True\n649\t    if op == \"BUY_ANIMAL\":\n650\t        if farm[\"money\"] < price:\n651\t            return False\n652\t        farm[\"money\"] -= price\n653\t        private[\"shed\"][item] = private[\"shed\"].get(item, 0) + 1\n654\t        return True\n655\t    return False\n656\t\n657\t\n658\tdef _fib(n):\n659\t    \"\"\"Indexed so _fib(0)=1, _fib(1)=1, _fib(2)=2, _fib(3)=3, _fib(4)=5...\"\"\"\n660\t    a, b = 1, 1\n661\t    for _ in range(n):\n662\t        a, b = b, a + b\n663\t    return a\n664\t\n665\t\n666\tdef _hire_cost(n_already_today, mult=FARM_HAND_COST_MULT):\n667\t    return mult * _fib(n_already_today)\n668\t\n669\t\n670\tdef _do_hire(farm, private, board_size, mult=FARM_HAND_COST_MULT):\n671\t    cost = _hire_cost(farm[\"hires_today\"], mult)\n672\t    if farm[\"money\"] < cost:\n673\t        return\n674\t    farm[\"money\"] -= cost\n675\t    farm[\"hires_today\"] += 1\n676\t    farm[\"hands\"].append(_spawn_hand(farm, board_size))\n677\t    private[\"inventories\"].append({})\n678\t\n679\t\n680\tdef _do_buy_land(farm, board_size):\n681\t    n_unlocked_extra = len(farm[\"unlocked_quadrants\"]) - 1  # NW is always there\n682\t    if n_unlocked_extra >= len(LAND_ORDER):\n683\t        return\n684\t    cost = LAND_PRICES[n_unlocked_extra]\n685\t    if farm[\"money\"] < cost:\n686\t        return\n687\t    farm[\"money\"] -= cost\n688\t    quadrant = LAND_ORDER[n_unlocked_extra]\n689\t    farm[\"unlocked_quadrants\"].append(quadrant)\n690\t    for y in range(board_size):\n691\t        for x in range(board_size):\n692\t            if _quadrant_of(x, y, board_size) == quadrant and farm[\"tiles\"][y][x] == \"LOCKED\":\n693\t                farm[\"tiles\"][y][x] = None\n694\t\n695\t\n696\tdef _town_consume(env, state, step):\n697\t    obs0 = state[0].observation\n698\t    market = obs0.market\n699\t    town = obs0.town\n700\t    cfg = env.configuration\n701\t    shop_interval = max(1, int(get(cfg, \"townShopSellInterval\", 4)))\n702\t    center_interval = max(1, int(get(cfg, \"townCenterSellInterval\", 12)))\n703\t    turns_per_day = max(1, int(get(cfg, \"turnsPerDay\", 24)))\n704\t    day = step // turns_per_day\n705\t\n706\t    if step % shop_interval == 0:\n707\t        for shop_name in town.get(\"unlocked_shops\", []):\n708\t            products = SHOPS[shop_name]\n709\t            multiplier = 2 if len(products) == 1 else 1\n710\t            for item in products:\n711\t                market[\"inventory\"][item] -= multiplier\n712\t\n713\t    if step % center_interval == 0:\n714\t        center_mult = next(m for threshold, m in TOWN_CENTER_DEMAND_SCHEDULE if day >= threshold)\n715\t        for item in TOWN_CENTER_PRODUCTS:\n716\t            market[\"inventory\"][item] -= center_mult\n717\t\n718\t    _refresh_prices(market)\n719\t\n720\t\n721\tdef _decay_plants(farm, step):\n722\t    board_size = len(farm[\"tiles\"])\n723\t    for y in range(board_size):\n724\t        for x in range(board_size):\n725\t            tile = farm[\"tiles\"][y][x]\n726\t            if not isinstance(tile, dict) or tile.get(\"kind\") != \"PLANT\":\n727\t                continue\n728\t            mls = tile[\"max_lifespan_step\"]\n729\t            if mls < 0 or step < mls:\n730\t                continue\n731\t            if (step - mls) % 2 != 0:\n732\t                continue\n733\t            tile[\"yield_units\"] -= 1\n734\t            if tile[\"yield_units\"] <= 0:\n735\t                farm[\"tiles\"][y][x] = {\"kind\": \"WEED\"}\n736\t\n737\t\n738\tdef _daily_refresh_plants(farm, current_day, turns_per_day):\n739\t    board_size = len(farm[\"tiles\"])\n740\t    next_day = current_day + 1\n741\t    for y in range(board_size):\n742\t        for x in range(board_size):\n743\t            tile = farm[\"tiles\"][y][x]\n744\t            if not isinstance(tile, dict) or tile.get(\"kind\") != \"PLANT\":\n745\t                continue\n746\t            was_watered = tile[\"watered_today\"]\n747\t            if was_watered:\n748\t                tile[\"consecutive_unwatered\"] = 0\n749\t            else:\n750\t                tile[\"consecutive_unwatered\"] += 1\n751\t            tile[\"watered_today\"] = False\n752\t            if tile[\"consecutive_unwatered\"] >= 2:\n753\t                farm[\"tiles\"][y][x] = {\"kind\": \"WEED\"}\n754\t                continue\n755\t            cd = CROPS[tile[\"crop\"]]\n756\t            if not cd[\"ongoing\"]:\n757\t                continue\n758\t            days_since_first = next_day - tile[\"planted_day\"] - cd[\"first_yield_day\"]\n759\t            if days_since_first < 0:\n760\t                continue\n761\t            interval = cd[\"interval\"]\n762\t            if days_since_first % interval != 0:\n763\t                continue\n764\t            production_count = days_since_first // interval + 1\n765\t            if production_count > cd[\"max_yield\"]:\n766\t                continue\n767\t            # Fertilizer bonus only applies on watered days (basic needs first).\n768\t            fertilized = was_watered and tile.get(\"fertilized_until_day\", -1) >= current_day\n769\t            tile[\"yield_units\"] = min(cd[\"max_yield\"], tile[\"yield_units\"] + (2 if fertilized else 1))\n770\t            if production_count == cd[\"max_yield\"]:\n771\t                tile[\"max_lifespan_step\"] = (next_day + 1) * turns_per_day\n772\t\n773\t\n774\tdef _daily_refresh_animals(farm, day):\n775\t    board_size = len(farm[\"tiles\"])\n776\t    next_day = day + 1\n777\t    for y in range(board_size):\n778\t        for x in range(board_size):\n779\t            tile = farm[\"tiles\"][y][x]\n780\t            if not (isinstance(tile, dict) and \"animal\" in tile):\n781\t                continue\n782\t            if tile[\"fed_today\"]:\n783\t                tile[\"consecutive_unfed\"] = 0\n784\t            else:\n785\t                tile[\"consecutive_unfed\"] += 1\n786\t            if tile[\"consecutive_unfed\"] >= 2:\n787\t                # Animal escapes; structure remains.\n788\t                farm[\"tiles\"][y][x] = {\"kind\": ANIMALS[tile[\"animal\"]][\"structure\"]}\n789\t                continue\n790\t            a = ANIMALS[tile[\"animal\"]]\n791\t            days_since_first = next_day - tile[\"placed_day\"] - a[\"first_yield_day\"]\n792\t            if days_since_first >= 0 and days_since_first % a[\"interval\"] == 0:\n793\t                base = 1\n794\t                # Care bonus only consumed on a fed production day.\n795\t                bonus = tile.pop(\"pending_care_bonus\", 0) if tile[\"fed_today\"] else 0\n796\t                tile[\"yield_units\"] =
