json\nimport math\nimport random\nfrom os import path\n\nfrom kaggle_environments.utils import resolve_episode_seed\n\ndirpath = path.dirname(__file__)\n\n\nCROPS = {\n    \"WHEAT\":      {\"seed\": 10, \"first_yield_day\": 2, \"max_yield_day\": 4, \"interval\": 0, \"max_yield\": 6, \"ongoing\": False},\n    \"CARROT\":     {\"seed\": 20, \"first_yield_day\": 2, \"max_yield_day\": 3, \"interval\": 0, \"max_yield\": 4, \"ongoing\": False},\n    \"TOMATO\":     {\"seed\": 50, \"first_yield_day\": 8, \"max_yield_day\": 8, \"interval\": 1, \"max_yield\": 4, \"ongoing\": True},\n    \"STRAWBERRY\": {\"seed\": 100, \"first_yield_day\": 10, \"max_yield_day\": 10, \"interval\": 2, \"max_yield\": 4, \"ongoing\": True},\n    \"MELON\":      {\"seed\": 80, \"first_yield_day\": 10, \"max_yield_day\": 12, \"interval\": 0, \"max_yield\": 6, \"ongoing\": False},\n}\n\nANIMALS = {\n    \"GOOSE\": {\"cost\": 300, \"structure\": \"COOP\",    \"first_yield_day\": 4, \"interval\": 1, \"max_held\": 4, \"product\": \"EGG\"},\n    \"COW\":   {\"cost\": 400, \"structure\": \"PASTURE\", \"first_yield_day\": 8, \"interval\": 2, \"max_held\": 6, \"product\": \"MILK\"},\n    \"SHEEP\": {\"cost\": 500, \"structure\": \"PASTURE\", \"first_yield_day\": 6, \"interval\": 3, \"max_held\": 6, \"product\": \"WOOL\"},\n}\n\nPRODUCTS = [\"WHEAT\", \"CARROT\", \"TOMATO\", \"STRAWBERRY\", \"MELON\", \"EGG\", \"MILK\", \"WOOL\", \"FERTILIZER\"]\n\n# Pricing model:\n#     price(inv) = base + sign * amp * f(|inv - I0|)\n#     sign = +1 below I0 (scarcity), -1 above I0 (glut)\n#     amp  = target * base / f(T)            (derived; selling T units moves\n#                                             price by `target` * base)\n#     T    = production capacity of one 5x5 field over a 24-day game at\n#            optimal watering, no fertilizer (animal T pre-discounted 30% for\n#            wheat-feed overhead)\n#     f    in {linear, sq, sqrt, log, log10}; log uses ln(1+x) so f(0)=0\n# Floored at PRICE_FLOOR.\nMARKET_I0 = 10000\nPRICE_FLOOR = 1\n\nMARKET_PARAMS = {\n    \"WHEAT\":      {\"base\":  25, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 400, \"below_func\": \"sqrt\",   \"below_target\": 0.80, \"above_func\": \"log\",    \"above_target\": 0.20},\n    \"CARROT\":     {\"base\":  35, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 450, \"below_func\": \"log\",    \"below_target\": 0.20, \"above_func\": \"sqrt\",   \"above_target\": 0.70},\n    \"TOMATO\":     {\"base\":  60, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 200, \"below_func\": \"linear\", \"below_target\": 0.40, \"above_func\": \"sqrt\",   \"above_target\": 0.60},\n    \"STRAWBERRY\": {\"base\": 120, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 100, \"below_func\": \"sqrt\",   \"below_target\": 0.70, \"above_func\": \"linear\", \"above_target\": 1.60},\n    \"MELON\":      {\"base\": 250, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 300, \"below_func\": \"log\",    \"below_target\": 0.20, \"above_func\": \"sq\",     \"above_target\": 3.60},\n    \"EGG\":        {\"base\":  50, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 332, \"below_func\": \"linear\", \"below_target\": 0.40, \"above_func\": \"log\",    \"above_target\": 0.20},\n    \"MILK\":       {\"base\": 160, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 122, \"below_func\": \"sqrt\",   \"below_target\": 0.60, \"above_func\": \"linear\", \"above_target\": 1.60},\n    \"WOOL\":       {\"base\": 200, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 105, \"below_func\": \"log\",    \"below_target\": 0.20, \"above_func\": \"sq\",     \"above_target\": 3.20},\n    \"FERTILIZER\": {\"base\": 100, \"I0\": MARKET_I0, \"T\": 200, \"below_func\": \"linear\", \"below_target\": 0.40, \"above_func\": \"linear\", \"above_target\": 0.40},\n}\n\n\ndef _shape(func, x):\n    x = max(0.0, x)\n    if func == \"linear\": return x\n    if func == \"sq\":     return x * x\n    if func == \"sqrt\":   return math.sqrt(x)\n    if func == \"log\":    return math.log(1.0 + x)\n    if func == \"log10\":  return math.log10(1.0 + x)\n    return x\n\n\ndef _resolve_market_params(overrides):\n    \"\"\"Merge per-resource overrides onto MARKET_PARAMS defaults (sparse).\"\"\"\n    resolved = {item: dict(p) for item, p in MARKET_PARAMS.items()}\n    if not overrides:\n        return resolved\n    for item, patch in overrides.items():\n        if item in resolved and isinstance(patch, dict):\n            resolved[item].update(patch)\n    return resolved\n\n# (dx, dy); y grows downward.\nFARMER_MOVES = {\n    \"NORTH\": (0, -1),\n    \"SOUTH\": (0, 1),\n    \"EAST\":  (1, 0),\n    \"WEST\":  (-1, 0),\n}\n\n# NW is always unlocked; players unlock the rest in this order.\nLAND_ORDER = [\"NE\", \"SW\", \"SE\"]\nLAND_PRICES = [1000, 2000, 4000]\n\n# n-th hire of the day -> cost = FARM_HAND_COST_MULT * fib(n), where\n# fib starts 1, 1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, ... Configurable via `farmHandCostMult`.\nFARM_HAND_COST_MULT = 1\n\nSHOPS = {\n    \"BAKERY\":         [\"EGG\", \"WHEAT\"],\n    \"PIZZA_SHOP\":     [\"MILK\", \"TOMATO\", \"WHEAT\"],\n    \"BRUNCH_SPOT\":    [\"EGG\", \"WHEAT\", \"STRAWBERRY\"],\n    \"YARN_STORE\":     [\"WOOL\"],\n    \"ICE_CREAM_SHOP\": [\"STRAWBERRY\", \"MILK\", \"WHEAT\"],\n    \"PET_CAFE\":       [\"CARROT\"],\n    \"SMOOTHIE_SHOP\":  [\"STRAWBERRY\", \"MILK\"],\n    \"FARMERS_MARKET\": [\"WHEAT\", \"CARROT\", \"TOMATO\", \"STRAWBERRY\"],\n}\n\nTOWN_CENTER_PRODUCTS = [p for p in PRODUCTS if p != \"FERTILIZER\"]\n\n# Town center demand schedule: (day_threshold, multiplier), highest threshold first.\nTOWN_CENTER_DEMAND_SCHEDULE = [(20, 4), (10, 2), (0, 1)]\n\n\ndef get(d, key, default):\n    if isinstance(d, dict):\n        return d.get(key, default)\n    return getattr(d, key, default)\n\n\ndef _quadrant_of(x, y, board_size):\n    half = board_size // 2\n    return (\"N\" if y < half else \"S\") + (\"W\" if x < half else \"E\")\n\n\ndef _shed_access_tiles(board_size):\n    \"\"\"Four inner-corner tiles around the shed, in NWSE order.\"\"\"\n    half = board_size // 2\n    return [(half - 1, half - 1), (half, half - 1), (half - 1, half), (half, half)]\n\n\ndef _is_shed_adjacent(pos, board_size):\n    return tuple(pos) in {(x, y) for (x, y) in _shed_access_tiles(board_size)}\n\n\ndef _new_farm(board_size, starting_money):\n    return {\n        \"money\": float(starting_money),\n        # tiles[y][x] = None (empty unlocked) | \"LOCKED\" | dict structure\n        \"tiles\": [\n            [_initial_tile(x, y, board_size) for x in range(board_size)]\n            for y in range(board_size)\n        ],\n        \"farmer\": list(_default_spawn(board_size)),\n        \"hands\": [],\n        \"unlocked_quadrants\": [\"NW\"],\n        \"hires_today\": 0,\n    }\n\n\ndef _initial_tile(x, y, board_size):\n    return None if _quadrant_of(x, y, board_size) == \"NW\" else \"LOCKED\"\n\n\ndef _default_spawn(board_size):\n    \"\"\"First free shed-access tile, NWSE preference.\"\"\"\n    for tile in _shed_access_tiles(board_size):\n        if _quadrant_of(tile[0], tile[1], board_size) == \"NW\":\n            return tile\n    return (0, 0)\n\n\ndef _new_private():\n    return {\n        \"shed\": {item: 0 for item in PRODUCTS + list(ANIMALS)},\n        \"seeds\": {crop: 0 for crop in CROPS},\n        # inventories[0] = main farmer; hands appended/removed each day.\n        \"inventories\": [{}],\n    }\n\n\ndef _new_market(params=None):\n    params = params or MARKET_PARAMS\n    inv = {item: params[item][\"I0\"] for item in PRODUCTS}\n    prices = {item: params[item][\"base\"] for item in PRODUCTS}\n    market = {\"inventory\": inv, \"prices\": prices}\n    if params is not MARKET_PARAMS:\n        market[\"params\"] = params\n    return market\n\n\ndef _new_town():\n    return {\"unlocked_shops\": []}\n\n\ndef market_price(item, inventory, params=None):\n    \"\"\"Floor at PRICE_FLOOR.\"\"\"\n    p = (params or MARKET_PARAMS)[item]\n    base = p[\"base\"]\n    I0 = p[\"I0\"]\n    T = p[\"T\"]\n    if inventory < I0:\n        f = p[\"below_func\"]\n        amp = p[\"below_target\"] * base / _shape(f, T)\n        price = base + amp * _shape(f, I0 - inventory)\n    else:\n        f = p[\"above_func\"]\n        amp = p[\"above_target\"] * base / _shape(f, T)\n        price = base - amp * _shape(f, inventory - I0)\n    return max(PRICE_FLOOR, int(round(price)))\n\n\ndef _refresh_prices(market):\n    params = market.get(\"params\")\n    for item in PRODUCTS:\n        market[\"prices\"][item] = market_price(item, market[\"inventory\"][item], params)\n\n\ndef _new_plant(crop, day, turns_per_day):\n    cd = CROPS[crop]\n    return {\n        \"kind\": \"PLANT\",\n        \"crop\": crop,\n        \"planted_day\": day,\n        \"watered_today\": False,\n        \"consecutive_unwatered\": 1,  # planting day counts as unwatered\n        \"yield_units\": 0 if cd[\"ongoing\"] else 1,\n        \"max_lifespan_step\": (-1 if cd[\"ongoing\"] else (day + cd[\"max_yield_day\"] + 1) * turns_per_day),\n        \"fertilized_until_day\": -1,\n    }\n\n\ndef _new_animal(animal, day):\n    a = ANIMALS[animal]\n    return {\n        \"kind\": a[\"structure\"],\n        \"animal\": animal,\n        \"placed_day\": day,\n        \"yield_units\": 0,\n        \"consecutive_unfed\": 0,\n        \"fed_today\": False,\n        \"cared_today\": False,\n        \"fertilizer_available\": False,\n        \"pending_care_bonus\": 0,\n    }\n\n\ndef _initialize(state, env):\n    configuration = env.configuration\n    num_agents = len(state)\n    obs0 = state[0].observation\n\n    seed = resolve_episode_seed(env)\n\n    board_size = int(get(configuration, \"boardSize\", 10))\n    starting_money = int(get(configuration, \"startingMoney\", 3000))\n\n    farms = [_new_farm(board_size, starting_money) for _ in range(num_agents)]\n    privates = [_new_private() for _ in range(num_agents)]\n    market_overrides = get(configuration, \"marketParams\", None)\n    resolved_params = _resolve_market_params(market_overrides) if market_overrides else None\n    market = _new_market(resolved_params)\n    town = _new_town()\n\n    obs0.farms = farms\n    obs0.market = market\n    obs0.town = town\n    obs0.day = 0\n    obs0.hour = 0\n\n    for i in range(num_agents):\n        state[i].observation.player = i\n        state[i].observation.private = privates[i]\n        if i > 0:\n            state[i].observation.farms = farms\n            state[i].observation.market = market\n            state[i].observation.town = town\n            state[i].observation.day = 0\n            state[i].observation.hour = 0\n\n\ndef _farmer_position(farm, idx):\n    \"\"\"idx 0 = main farmer, 1+ = hand index.\"\"\"\n    if idx == 0:\n        return farm[\"farmer\"]\n    return farm[\"hands\"][idx - 1] if idx - 1 < len(farm[\"hands\"]) else None\n\n\ndef _set_farmer_position(farm, idx, pos):\n    if idx == 0:\n        farm[\"farmer\"] = list(pos)\n    else:\n        farm[\"hands\"][idx - 1] = list(pos)\n\n\ndef _farmer_inventory(private, idx):\n    \"\"\"Inventories list is [main_farmer, *hands]; grow it if idx is past the end.\"\"\"\n    while len(private[\"inventories\"]) <= idx:\n        private[\"inventories\"].append({})\n    return private[\"inventories\"][idx]\n\n\ndef _inv_add(inv, item, n=1):\n    inv[item] = inv.get(item, 0) + n\n\n\ndef _inv_take(inv, item, n=1):\n    if inv.get(item, 0) < n:\n        return False\n    inv[item] -= n\n    if inv[item] == 0:\n        del inv[item]\n    return True\n\n\ndef _apply_unit_action(farm, private, idx, action, board_size, day, turns_per_day, shed_capacity=100):\n    \"\"\"Process one farmer/hand's action. Invalid / illegal actions are silent no-ops.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(action, list) or not action:\n        return\n    op = action[0]\n    pos = _farmer_position(farm, idx)\n    if pos is None:\n        return\n    fx, fy = pos[0], pos[1]\n    inv = _farmer_inventory(private, idx)\n\n    if op in FARMER_MOVES:\n        dx, dy = FARMER_MOVES[op]\n        nx, ny = fx + dx, fy + dy\n        if not (0 <= nx < board_size and 0 <= ny < board_size):\n            return\n        if farm[\"tiles\"][ny][nx] == \"LOCKED\":\n            return\n        _set_farmer_position(farm, idx, (nx, ny))\n        return\n\n    if op == \"PASS\":\n        return\n\n    tile = farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx]\n    if tile == \"LOCKED\":\n        return\n\n    if op == \"DROP\":\n        if not _is_shed_adjacent((fx, fy), board_size):\n            return\n        shed = private[\"shed\"]\n        for item, n in list(inv.items()):\n            if n <= 0:\n                del inv[item]\n                continue\n            room = max(0, shed_capacity - sum(shed.values()))\n            take = min(n, room)\n            if take > 0:\n                shed[item] = shed.get(item, 0) + take\n            del inv[item]\n        return\n\n    if op == \"PICKUP\":\n        if not _is_shed_adjacent((fx, fy), board_size):\n            return\n        if len(action) < 2:\n            return\n        item = action[1]\n        n = int(action[2]) if len(action) >= 3 else 1\n        if n <= 0:\n            return\n        # Seeds live in private[\"seeds\"] and are consumed directly by PLANT;\n        # they never pass through farmer inventory or the shed.\n        available = private[\"shed\"].get(item, 0)\n        n = min(n, available)\n        if n <= 0:\n            return\n        private[\"shed\"][item] -= n\n        _inv_add(inv, item, n)\n        return\n\n    if op == \"PLANT\":\n        if len(action) < 2:\n            return\n        crop = action[1]\n        if crop not in CROPS:\n            return\n        if tile is not None:\n            return\n        if private[\"seeds\"].get(crop, 0) <= 0:\n            return\n        private[\"seeds\"][crop] -= 1\n        farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx] = _new_plant(crop, day, turns_per_day)\n        return\n\n    if op == \"WATER\":\n        if not (isinstance(tile, dict) and tile.get(\"kind\") == \"PLANT\"):\n            return\n        if tile[\"watered_today\"]:\n            return\n        tile[\"watered_today\"] = True\n        crop_data = CROPS[tile[\"crop\"]]\n        if not crop_data[\"ongoing\"]:\n            age_days = day - tile[\"planted_day\"]\n            window_start = (crop_data[\"max_yield_day\"] + 1) // 2\n            if window_start <= age_days <= crop_data[\"max_yield_day\"]:\n                bonus = 2 if tile[\"fertilized_until_day\"] >= day else 1\n                tile[\"yield_units\"] = min(crop_data[\"max_yield\"], tile[\"yield_units\"] + bonus)\n        return\n\n    if op == \"HARVEST\":\n        if not isinstance(tile, dict):\n            return\n        if tile.get(\"yield_units\", 0) <= 0:\n            return\n        if tile.get(\"kind\") == \"PLANT\":\n            crop_data = CROPS[tile[\"crop\"]]\n            if day - tile[\"planted_day\"] < crop_data[\"first_yield_day\"]:\n                # Ongoing crops only accumulate yield_units after first_yield_day,\n                # so reaching here with yield_units > 0 indicates a bug.\n                if crop_data[\"ongoing\"]:\n                    print(\n                        f\"WARNING: HARVEST on immature ongoing {tile['crop']} \"\n                        f\"(planted day {tile['planted_day']}, current day {day}, \"\n                        f\"first_yield_day {crop_data['first_yield_day']}, \"\n                        f\"yield_units {tile['yield_units']}); should never happen\"\n                    )\n                return\n            units = tile[\"yield_units\"]\n            tile[\"yield_units\"] = 0\n            _inv_add(inv, tile[\"crop\"], units)\n            if not crop_data[\"ongoing\"]:\n                farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx] = None\n        elif \"animal\" in tile:\n            units = tile[\"yield_units\"]\n            tile[\"yield_units\"] = 0\n            _inv_add(inv, ANIMALS[tile[\"animal\"]][\"product\"], units)\n        return\n\n    if op == \"FERTILIZE\":\n        if not (isinstance(tile, dict) and tile.get(\"kind\") == \"PLANT\"):\n            return\n        if not _inv_take(inv, \"FERTILIZER\", 1):\n            return\n        # Active for `day`, `day+1`, `day+2` (3 days inclusive).\n        tile[\"fertilized_until_day\"] = max(tile.get(\"fertilized_until_day\", -1), day + 2)\n        return\n\n    if op == \"DIG\":\n        if tile is None:\n            return\n        # Removes plants, weeds, empty coop/pasture. Does NOT remove a placed animal.\n        if isinstance(tile, dict) and \"animal\" in tile:\n            return\n        farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx] = None\n        return\n\n    if op == \"BUILD_COOP\":\n        if tile is not None:\n            return\n        farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx] = {\"kind\": \"COOP\"}\n        return\n\n    if op == \"BUILD_PASTURE\":\n        if tile is not None:\n            return\n        farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx] = {\"kind\": \"PASTURE\"}\n        return\n\n    if op == \"PLACE\":\n        if len(action) < 2:\n            return\n        item = action[1]\n        # Animal placement: standing on a matching unoccupied structure.\n        if (\n            item in ANIMALS\n            and isinstance(tile, dict)\n            and tile.get(\"kind\") == ANIMALS[item][\"structure\"]\n            and \"animal\" not in tile\n        ):\n            if _inv_take(inv, item, 1):\n                farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx] = _new_animal(item, day)\n            return\n        # Shed drop: orthogonally adjacent to the shed; obeys shedCapacity.\n        if _is_shed_adjacent((fx, fy), board_size):\n            n = int(action[2]) if len(action) >= 3 else 1\n            if n <= 0:\n                return\n            n = min(n, inv.get(item, 0))\n            if n <= 0:\n                return\n            current = sum(private[\"shed\"].values())\n            room = max(0, shed_capacity - current)\n            n = min(n, room)\n            if n <= 0:\n                return\n            inv[item] -= n\n            if inv[item] == 0:\n                del inv[item]\n            private[\"shed\"][item] = private[\"shed\"].get(item, 0) + n\n        return\n\n    if op == \"FEED\":\n        if not (isinstance(tile, dict) and \"animal\" in tile):\n            return\n        if tile[\"fed_today\"]:\n            return\n        if not _inv_take(inv, \"WHEAT\", 1):\n            return\n        tile[\"fed_today\"] = True\n        return\n\n    if op == \"COLLECT_FERTILIZER\":\n        if not (isinstance(tile, dict) and \"animal\" in tile):\n            return\n        if not tile[\"fertilizer_available\"]:\n            return\n        tile[\"fertilizer_available\"] = False\n        _inv_add(inv, \"FERTILIZER\", 1)\n        return\n\n    if op == \"CARE\":\n        if not (isinstance(tile, dict) and \"animal\" in tile):\n            return\n        if tile[\"cared_today\"]:\n            return\n        tile[\"cared_today\"] = True\n        return\n\n\ndef _spawn_hand(farm, board_size):\n    \"\"\"First free shed-access tile (NWSE order); ties broken by min occupancy.\"\"\"\n    occupants = {tile: 0 for tile in _shed_access_tiles(board_size)}\n    all_pos = [tuple(farm[\"farmer\"])] + [tuple(p) for p in farm[\"hands\"]]\n    for pos in all_pos:\n        if pos in occupants:\n            occupants[pos] += 1\n    best = sorted(occupants.items(), key=lambda kv: (kv[1], _shed_access_tiles(board_size).index(kv[0])))\n    return list(best[0][0])\n\n\ndef _process_market(state, env):\n    \"\"\"Per-unit lockstep: at each step, quote both players' current-unit prices, then commit both.\"\"\"\n    obs0 = state[0].observation\n    market = obs0.market\n    farms = obs0.farms\n    privates = [s.observation.private for s in state]\n    board_size = int(get(env.configuration, \"boardSize\", 10))\n    max_orders = max(1, int(get(env.configuration, \"maxMarketOrdersPerTurn\", 10)))\n    hire_mult = int(get(env.configuration, \"farmHandCostMult\", FARM_HAND_COST_MULT))\n\n    queues = []\n    for s in state:\n        action = s.action if isinstance(s.action, dict) else {}\n        m = action.get(\"market\", []) if isinstance(action, dict) else []\n        q = list(m) if isinstance(m, list) else []\n        queues.append(q[:max_orders])\n\n    max_len = max((len(q) for q in queues), default=0)\n    for i in range(max_len):\n        order_states = []\n        for player_id, q in enumerate(queues):\n            ostate = None\n            if i < len(q):\n                ostate = _parse_order(q[i])\n            order_states.append(ostate)\n\n        # Atomic orders (HIRE, BUY_LAND): handle once, in player order.\n        for player_id, ostate in enumerate(order_states):\n            if ostate is None:\n                continue\n            op = ostate[\"type\"]\n            if op == \"HIRE\":\n                _do_hire(farms[player_id], privates[player_id], board_size, hire_mult)\n                order_states[player_id] = None\n            elif op == \"BUY_LAND\":\n                _do_buy_land(farms[player_id], board_size)\n                order_states[player_id] = None\n\n        # Per-unit lockstep loop for SELL / BUY_*.\n        idx_esc = 0\n        while True:\n            idx_esc += 1\n            if idx_esc >= 100_000:\n                print(\"WARNING: kaggriculture market loop exceeded 100k iterations; aborting\")\n                break\n            quoted = [None, None]\n            for player_id, ostate in enumerate(order_states):\n                if ostate is None or ostate[\"remaining\"] <= 0:\n                    continue\n                op = ostate[\"type\"]\n                item = ostate[\"item\"]\n                if op == \"SELL\" and item in PRODUCTS:\n                    quoted[player_id] = (\"SELL\", item, market_price(item, market[\"inventory\"][item], market.get(\"params\")), ostate)\n                elif op == \"BUY_PRODUCT\" and item in (\"WHEAT\", \"FERTILIZER\"):\n                    # Quote at post-buy inventory so a buy/sell round-trip\n                    # against an unchanged market nets zero.\n                    quoted[player_id] = (\"BUY_PRODUCT\", item, market_price(item, market[\"inventory\"][item] - 1, market.get(\"params\")), ostate)\n                elif op == \"BUY_SEED\" and item in CROPS:\n                    quoted[player_id] = (\"BUY_SEED\", item, CROPS[item][\"seed\"], ostate)\n                elif op == \"BUY_ANIMAL\" and item in ANIMALS:\n                    quoted[player_id] = (\"BUY_ANIMAL\", item, ANIMALS[item][\"cost\"], ostate)\n                else:\n                    order_states[player_id] = None  # malformed sub-op; abort\n\n            if all(q is None for q in quoted):\n                break\n\n            # Both players see the same pre-commit inventory for this unit.\n            committed_any = False\n            for player_id, q in enumerate(quoted):\n                if q is None:\n                    continue\n                op, item, price, ostate = q\n                ok = _commit_unit(op, item, price, farms[player_id], privates[player_id], market)\n                if ok:\n                    ostate[\"remaining\"] -= 1\n                    committed_any = True\n                else:\n                    order_states[player_id] = None  # can't continue this order\n\n            if not committed_any:\n                break\n\n        _refresh_prices(market)\n\n\ndef _parse_order(order):\n    if not isinstance(order, list) or not order:\n        return None\n    op = order[0]\n    if op == \"HIRE\":\n        return {\"type\": \"HIRE\"}\n    if op == \"BUY_LAND\":\n        return {\"type\": \"BUY_LAND\"}\n    if op in (\"BUY_SEED\", \"BUY_PRODUCT\", \"BUY_ANIMAL\", \"SELL\"):\n        if len(order) < 3:\n            return None\n        try:\n            n = int(order[2])\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n            return None\n        if n <= 0:\n            return None\n        return {\"type\": op, \"item\": order[1], \"remaining\": n}\n    return None\n\n\ndef _commit_unit(op, item, price, farm, private, market):\n    if op == \"SELL\":\n        if private[\"shed\"].get(item, 0) <= 0:\n            return False\n        private[\"shed\"][item] -= 1\n        farm[\"money\"] += price\n        # Sales at $1 do not increase market supply.\n        if price > 1:\n            market[\"inventory\"][item] += 1\n        return True\n    if op == \"BUY_PRODUCT\":\n        if farm[\"money\"] < price:\n            return False\n        farm[\"money\"] -= price\n        private[\"shed\"][item] = private[\"shed\"].get(item, 0) + 1\n        market[\"inventory\"][item] -= 1\n        return True\n    if op == \"BUY_SEED\":\n        if farm[\"money\"] < price:\n            return False\n        farm[\"money\"] -= price\n        private[\"seeds\"][item] = private[\"seeds\"].get(item, 0) + 1\n        return True\n    if op == \"BUY_ANIMAL\":\n        if farm[\"money\"] < price:\n            return False\n        farm[\"money\"] -= price\n        private[\"shed\"][item] = private[\"shed\"].get(item, 0) + 1\n        return True\n    return False\n\n\ndef _fib(n):\n    \"\"\"Indexed so _fib(0)=1, _fib(1)=1, _fib(2)=2, _fib(3)=3, _fib(4)=5...\"\"\"\n    a, b = 1, 1\n    for _ in range(n):\n        a, b = b, a + b\n    return a\n\n\ndef _hire_cost(n_already_today, mult=FARM_HAND_COST_MULT):\n    return mult * _fib(n_already_today)\n\n\ndef _do_hire(farm, private, board_size, mult=FARM_HAND_COST_MULT):\n    cost = _hire_cost(farm[\"hires_today\"], mult)\n    if farm[\"money\"] < cost:\n        return\n    farm[\"money\"] -= cost\n    farm[\"hires_today\"] += 1\n    farm[\"hands\"].append(_spawn_hand(farm, board_size))\n    private[\"inventories\"].append({})\n\n\ndef _do_buy_land(farm, board_size):\n    n_unlocked_extra = len(farm[\"unlocked_quadrants\"]) - 1  # NW is always there\n    if n_unlocked_extra >= len(LAND_ORDER):\n        return\n    cost = LAND_PRICES[n_unlocked_extra]\n    if farm[\"money\"] < cost:\n        return\n    farm[\"money\"] -= cost\n    quadrant = LAND_ORDER[n_unlocked_extra]\n    farm[\"unlocked_quadrants\"].append(quadrant)\n    for y in range(board_size):\n        for x in range(board_size):\n            if _quadrant_of(x, y, board_size) == quadrant and farm[\"tiles\"][y][x] == \"LOCKED\":\n                farm[\"tiles\"][y][x] = None\n\n\ndef _town_consume(env, state, step):\n    obs0 = state[0].observation\n    market = obs0.market\n    town = obs0.town\n    cfg = env.configuration\n    shop_interval = max(1, int(get(cfg, \"townShopSellInterval\", 4)))\n    center_interval = max(1, int(get(cfg, \"townCenterSellInterval\", 12)))\n    turns_per_day = max(1, int(get(cfg, \"turnsPerDay\", 24)))\n    day = step // turns_per_day\n\n    if step % shop_interval == 0:\n        for shop_name in town.get(\"unlocked_shops\", []):\n            products = SHOPS[shop_name]\n            multiplier = 2 if len(products) == 1 else 1\n            for item in products:\n                market[\"inventory\"][item] -= multiplier\n\n    if step % center_interval == 0:\n        center_mult = next(m for threshold, m in TOWN_CENTER_DEMAND_SCHEDULE if day >= threshold)\n        for item in TOWN_CENTER_PRODUCTS:\n            market[\"inventory\"][item] -= center_mult\n\n    _refresh_prices(market)\n\n\ndef _decay_plants(farm, step):\n    board_size = len(farm[\"tiles\"])\n    for y in range(board_size):\n        for x in range(board_size):\n            tile = farm[\"tiles\"][y][x]\n            if not isinstance(tile, dict) or tile.get(\"kind\") != \"PLANT\":\n                continue\n            mls = tile[\"max_lifespan_step\"]\n            if mls < 0 or step < mls:\n                continue\n            if (step - mls) % 2 != 0:\n                continue\n            tile[\"yield_units\"] -= 1\n            if tile[\"yield_units\"] <= 0:\n                farm[\"tiles\"][y][x] = {\"kind\": \"WEED\"}\n\n\ndef _daily_refresh_plants(farm, current_day, turns_per_day):\n    board_size = len(farm[\"tiles\"])\n    next_day = current_day + 1\n    for y in range(board_size):\n        for x in range(board_size):\n            tile = farm[\"tiles\"][y][x]\n            if not isinstance(tile, dict) or tile.get(\"kind\") != \"PLANT\":\n                continue\n            was_watered = tile[\"watered_today\"]\n            if was_watered:\n                tile[\"consecutive_unwatered\"] = 0\n            else:\n                tile[\"consecutive_unwatered\"] += 1\n            tile[\"watered_today\"] = False\n            if tile[\"consecutive_unwatered\"] >= 2:\n                farm[\"tiles\"][y][x] = {\"kind\": \"WEED\"}\n                continue\n            cd = CROPS[tile[\"crop\"]]\n            if not cd[\"ongoing\"]:\n                continue\n            days_since_first = next_day - tile[\"planted_day\"] - cd[\"first_yield_day\"]\n            if days_since_first < 0:\n                continue\n            interval = cd[\"interval\"]\n            if days_since_first % interval != 0:\n                continue\n            production_count = days_since_first // interval + 1\n            if production_count > cd[\"max_yield\"]:\n                continue\n            # Fertilizer bonus only applies on watered days (basic needs first).\n            fertilized = was_watered and tile.get(\"fertilized_until_day\", -1) >= current_day\n            tile[\"yield_units\"] = min(cd[\"max_yield\"], tile[\"yield_units\"] + (2 if fertilized else 1))\n            if production_count == cd[\"max_yield\"]:\n                tile[\"max_lifespan_step\"] = (next_day + 1) * turns_per_day\n\n\ndef _daily_refresh_animals(farm, day):\n    board_size = len(farm[\"tiles\"])\n    next_day = day + 1\n    for y in range(board_size):\n        for x in range(board_size):\n            tile = farm[\"tiles\"][y][x]\n            if not (isinstance(tile, dict) and \"animal\" in tile):\n                continue\n            if tile[\"fed_today\"]:\n                tile[\"consecutive_unfed\"] = 0\n            else:\n                tile[\"consecutive_unfed\"] += 1\n            if tile[\"consecutive_unfed\"] >= 2:\n                # Animal escapes; structure remains.\n                farm[\"tiles\"][y][x] = {\"kind\": ANIMALS[tile[\"animal\"]][\"structure\"]}\n                continue\n            a = ANIMALS[tile[\"animal\"]]\n            days_since_first = next_day - tile[\"placed_day\"] - a[\"first_yield_day\"]\n            if days_since_first >= 0 and days_since_first % a[\"interval\"] == 0:\n                base = 1\n                # Care bonus only consumed on a fed production day.\n                bonus = tile.pop(\"pending_care_bonus\", 0) if tile[\"fed_today\"] else 0\n                tile[\"yield_units\"] = min(a[\"max_held\"], tile[\"yield_units\"] + base + bonus)\n                tile[\"pending_care_bonus\"] = 0\n            if tile[\"cared_today\"] and tile[\"fed_today\"]:\n                tile[\"pending_care_bonus\"] = tile.get(\"pending_care_bonus\", 0) + 1\n            tile[\"fertilizer_available\"] = True\n            tile[\"fed_today\"] = False\n            tile[\"cared_today\"] = False\n\n\ndef _spawn_weeds(farm, board_size, weed_chance, rng):\n    for y in range(board_size):\n        for x in range(board_size):\n            if farm[\"tiles\"][y][x] is None and rng.random() < weed_chance:\n                farm[\"tiles\"][y][x] = {\"kind\": \"WEED\"}\n\n\ndef _drop_inventories_to_shed(private, capacity):\n    \"\"\"Drop every per-farmer inventory into the shed up to `capacity`; overflow is discarded.\n    Seeds are tracked separately in private[\"seeds\"] and don't pass through the shed.\"\"\"\n    shed = private[\"shed\"]\n    for inv in private[\"inventories\"]:\n        for item, n in list(inv.items()):\n            if n <= 0:\n                del inv[item]\n                continue\n            current = sum(v for k, v in shed.items())\n            room = max(0, capacity - current)\n            take = min(n, room)\n            if take > 0:\n                shed[item] = shed.get(item, 0) + take\n            del inv[item]\n\n\ndef _end_of_day(state, env, day):\n    obs0 = state[0].observation\n    cfg = env.configuration\n    board_size = int(get(cfg, \"boardSize\", 10))\n    turns_per_day = max(1, int(get(cfg, \"turnsPerDay\", 24)))\n    weed_chance = float(get(cfg, \"weedSpawnChance\", 0.005))\n    shed_cap = int(get(cfg, \"shedCapacity\", 100))\n    shop_interval = max(1, int(get(cfg, \"townShopUnlockInterval\", 3)))\n\n    # Stable RNG keyed off env.info[\"seed\"] + day so replays reproduce.\n    seed = env.info.get(\"seed\", 0)\n    rng = random.Random((seed * 1_000_003) ^ day)\n\n    for player_id, farm in enumerate(obs0.farms):\n        private = state[player_id].observation.private\n        _daily_refresh_plants(farm, day, turns_per_day)\n        _daily_refresh_animals(farm, day)\n        _spawn_weeds(farm, board_size, weed_chance, rng)\n        _drop_inventories_to_shed(private, shed_cap)\n        farm[\"farmer\"] = list(_default_spawn(board_size))\n        farm[\"hands\"] = []\n        farm[\"hires_today\"] = 0\n        private[\"inventories\"] = [{}]\n\n    next_day = day + 1\n    town = obs0.town\n    if next_day > 0 and next_day % shop_interval == 0:\n        remaining = [s for s in SHOPS if s not in town[\"unlocked_shops\"]]\n        if remaining:\n            choice = rng.choice(sorted(remaining))\n            town[\"unlocked_shops\"].append(choice)\n\n\ndef interpreter(state, env):\n    num_agents = len(state)\n    obs0 = state[0].observation\n\n    if not hasattr(obs0, \"farms\") or not obs0.farms:\n        _initialize(state, env)\n        return state\n\n    if env.done:\n        return state\n\n    cfg = env.configuration\n    turns_per_day = max(1, int(get(cfg, \"turnsPerDay\", 24)))\n    board_size = int(get(cfg, \"boardSize\", 10))\n    shed_capacity = int(get(cfg, \"shedCapacity\", 100))\n\n    step = get(obs0, \"step\", 0)\n    day = step // turns_per_day\n\n    for i, s in enumerate(state):\n        action = s.action if isinstance(s.action, dict) else {}\n        farmer_action = action.get(\"farmer\", [\"PASS\"]) if isinstance(action, dict) else [\"PASS\"]\n        hands_actions = action.get(\"hands\", []) if isinstance(action, dict) else []\n        if not isinstance(hands_actions, list):\n            hands_actions = []\n\n        # Atomic PLANT validation: if total PLANT requests for a crop this turn\n        # exceed available seeds, drop ALL PLANT requests for that crop.\n        unit_actions = [farmer_action, *hands_actions]\n        plant_demand = {}\n        for a in unit_actions:\n            if isinstance(a, list) and len(a) >= 2 and a[0] == \"PLANT\":\n                plant_demand[a[1]] = plant_demand.get(a[1], 0) + 1\n        seeds = s.observation.private.get(\"seeds\", {}) if hasattr(s.observation.private, \"get\") else {}\n        blocked = {crop for crop, n in plant_demand.items() if n > seeds.get(crop, 0)}\n\n        def _allowed(a):\n            if isinstance(a, list) and len(a) >= 2 and a[0] == \"PLANT\" and a[1] in blocked:\n                return [\"PASS\"]\n            return a\n\n        _apply_unit_action(obs0.farms[i], s.observation.private, 0, _allowed(farmer_action),\n                           board_size, day, turns_per_day, shed_capacity)\n        for h_idx, hand_action in enumerate(hands_actions):\n            _apply_unit_action(obs0.farms[i], s.observation.private, h_idx + 1,\n                               _allowed(hand_action), board_size, day, turns_per_day, shed_capacity)\n\n    _process_market(state, env)\n    _town_consume(env, state, step)\n    for farm in obs0.farms:\n        _decay_plants(farm, step)\n    if (step + 1) % turns_per_day == 0:\n        _end_of_day(state, env, day)\n\n    next_step = step + 1\n    obs0.day = next_step // turns_per_day\n    obs0.hour = next_step % turns_per_day\n    for i in range(1, num_agents):\n        state[i].observation.farms = obs0.farms\n        state[i].observation.market = obs0.market\n        state[i].observation.town = obs0.town\n        state[i].observation.day = obs0.day\n        state[i].observation.hour = obs0.hour\n\n    # `step` here is the previous step counter; framework records the post-interpreter\n    # state at the next index. -2 fires DONE on the final recorded step.\n    if step >= cfg.episodeSteps - 2:\n        for s in state:\n            s.status = \"DONE\"\n            s.reward = float(obs0.farms[s.observation.player][\"money\"])\n\n    return state\n\n\ndef _render_tile(tile):\n    if tile is None:\n        return \".\"\n    if tile == \"LOCKED\":\n        return \"#\"\n    if isinstance(tile, dict):\n        kind = tile.get(\"kind\")\n        if kind == \"WEED\":\n            return \"x\"\n        if kind == \"PLANT\":\n            return tile[\"crop\"][0].lower()\n        if \"animal\" in tile:\n            return tile[\"animal\"][0]\n        if kind == \"COOP\":\n            return \"C\"\n        if kind == \"PASTURE\":\n            return \"P\"\n    return \"?\"\n\n\ndef renderer(state, env):\n    obs = state[0].observation\n    out = f\"Step {get(obs, 'step', 0)}  Day {get(obs, 'day', 0)}  Hour {get(obs, 'hour', 0)}\\n\"\n    market = get(obs, \"market\", {}) or {}\n    town = get(obs, \"town\", {}) or {}\n    out += f\"Town shops: {town.get('unlocked_shops', [])}\\n\"\n    out += \"Prices: \" + \", \".join(f\"{k}=${v}\" for k, v in (market.get(\"prices\", {}) or {}).items()) + \"\\n\"\n    for i, s in enumerate(state):\n        farm = obs.farms[i] if i < len(obs.farms) else None\n        if farm is None:\n            continue\n        priv = get(s.observation, \"private\", {}) or {}\n        out += (\n            f\"Player {i}: ${farm['money']:.0f}  farmer={farm['farmer']}  \"\n            f\"hands={len(farm['hands'])}  unlocked={farm['unlocked_quadrants']}  \"\n            f\"shed={priv.get('shed')}  seeds={priv.get('seeds')}\\n\"\n        )\n        for row in farm[\"tiles\"]:\n            out += \"  \" + \" \".join(_render_tile(t) for t in row) + \"\\n\"\n    return out\n\n\njson_path = path.abspath(path.join(dirpath, \"kaggriculture.json\"))\nwith open(json_path) as json_file:\n    specification = json.load(json_file)\n\n\ndef html_renderer(env, mode):\n    jspath = path.join(dirpath, \"visualizer\", \"default\", \"dist\", \"index.html\")\n    if path.exists(jspath):\n        with open(jspath, encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            return f.read()\n    return \"\"\n\n\ndef pass_agent(obs):\n    return {\"farmer\": [\"PASS\"], \"hands\": [], \"market\": []}\n\n\ndef random_agent(obs):\n    rng = random.Random()\n    farms = obs.get(\"farms\", [])\n    player = obs.get(\"player\", 0)\n    private = obs.get(\"private\", {}) or {}\n    farm = farms[player] if farms and player < len(farms) else None\n    if farm is None:\n        return {\"farmer\": [\"PASS\"], \"hands\": [], \"market\": []}\n\n    farmer_ops = [\"NORTH\", \"SOUTH\", \"EAST\", \"WEST\", \"WATER\", \"HARVEST\", \"PASS\"]\n    market = []\n    seeds = private.get(\"seeds\", {})\n\n    affordable = [c for c in CROPS if CROPS[c][\"seed\"] <= farm[\"money\"]]\n    if affordable and rng.random() < 0.1:\n        market.append([\"BUY_SEED\", rng.choice(affordable), 1])\n\n    available_seeds = [c for c, n in seeds.items() if n > 0]\n    if available_seeds and rng.random() < 0.3:\n        farmer = [\"PLANT\", rng.choice(available_seeds)]\n    else:\n        farmer = [rng.choice(farmer_ops)]\n\n    hands_actions = [[rng.choice(farmer_ops)] for _ in farm.get(\"hands\", [])]\n    return {\"farmer\": farmer, \"hands\": hands_actions, \"market\": market}\n\n\ndef starter_agent(obs):\n    \"\"\"Carrot loop: buy seed, plant on the current tile, water, harvest at max_yield_day.\"\"\"\n    farms = obs.get(\"farms\", [])\n    player = obs.get(\"player\", 0)\n    private = obs.get(\"private\", {}) or {}\n    if not farms or player >= len(farms):\n        return {\"farmer\": [\"PASS\"], \"hands\": [], \"market\": []}\n    farm = farms[player]\n    fx, fy = farm[\"farmer\"]\n    tile = farm[\"tiles\"][fy][fx]\n    day = obs.get(\"day\", 0)\n    seeds = private.get(\"seeds\", {})\n    shed = private.get(\"shed\", {})\n\n    market = []\n    if shed.get(\"CARROT\", 0) > 0:\n        market.append([\"SELL\", \"CARROT\", shed[\"CARROT\"]])\n    if seeds.get(\"CARROT\", 0) == 0 and farm[\"money\"] >= CROPS[\"CARROT\"][\"seed\"]:\n        market.append([\"BUY_SEED\", \"CARROT\", 1])\n\n    farmer = [\"PASS\"]\n    if tile is None and seeds.get(\"CARROT\", 0) > 0:\n        farmer = [\"PLANT\", \"CARROT\"]\n    elif isinstance(tile, dict) and tile.get(\"kind\") == \"PLANT\" and tile[\"crop\"] == \"CARROT\":\n        age = day - tile[\"planted_day\"]\n        if age >= CROPS[\"CARROT\"][\"max_yield_day\"]:\n            farmer = [\"HARVEST\"]\n        elif not tile[\"watered_today\"]:\n            farmer = [\"WATER\"]\n    return {\"farmer\": farmer, \"hands\": [], \"market\": market}\n\n\nagents = {\"pass\": pass_agent, \"random\": random_agent, \"starter\": {"parentUuid":"009a60a6-a864-4dbf-995f-59ff59ba2947","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"a11cbfc5-5b1b-4a8a-90ff-e4cf512e7689","agentId":"af0fe4f4b572e87a8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TqdknPgeDN9FXw1FzcvxwD","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Macro-routines: the chain is the unit of search, not the sub-action.\n2\t\n3\t**Why this module exists.** A sub-action search cannot find the animal economy, and that is a\n4\tstructural fact rather than a tuning problem. Two mechanics close the door:\n5\t\n6\t- `consecutive_unfed >= 2` escapes the animal (`kaggriculture.py:786`), so buying one you will not\n7\t  feed every day is a *deterministic* loss of $300-$500, not a bad expectation.\n8\t- FEED is illegal until an animal is on the board, unfed, with a unit standing on it carrying wheat\n9\t  (`jax_masks.py:93`), so a policy samples FEED from a distribution where it has zero mass.\n10\t\n11\tEvery prefix of the chain is therefore money-negative and every complete chain needs ~15 coordinated\n12\tedits at specific tiles on specific days. A hill-climber selecting on money rejects step one forever;\n13\tPPO learns to stop buying animals, which re-masks FEED and closes the loop. Both failures are the\n14\tsame wall.\n15\t\n16\tThe fix is not a better optimiser. It is a representation in which a partial chain **cannot be\n17\texpressed**. A routine emits the whole chain or nothing, so the valley is not crossed -- it is not in\n18\tthe space. What the search then owns is *which* routine, *where*, *when* and *how many*, which is\n19\tstrategy, while correctness lives in the decoder.\n20\t\n21\t**The ranch cell.** The four shed-access tiles are `(4,4) (5,4) (4,5) (5,5)` -- the inner corners\n22\taround the shed. PICKUP, DROP and shed-PLACE test only shed-adjacency, and FEED/CARE/HARVEST test\n23\tonly that the unit stands on the animal's tile. Put the pen *on* a shed-access tile and one unit can\n24\trun the entire loop -- draw feed, feed, care, harvest, deposit -- **without ever moving**, in 5 of\n25\tthe day's 24 turns. That matters beyond convenience: a unit's tile is the cumulative sum of its\n26\tmovement ops, so in an open-loop script one edited step silently invalidates every later op. A\n27\tzero-movement routine has no such coupling, which is what makes it a safe unit of search.\n28\t\n29\t**Land is the gate.** Only `(4,4)` is in NW, the sole quadrant unlocked at the start; the other three\n30\tcells begin LOCKED. `LAND_ORDER` is `['NE','SW','SE']` at `[1000, 2000, 4000]`, bought in sequence,\n31\tso cell `(5,5)` costs $7,000 of land to reach. This is also why an agent that never buys land plays\n32\tthe whole season on 25 tiles of 100.\n33\t\"\"\"\n34\t\n35\timport numpy as np\n36\t\n37\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n38\t# The reference's own tables, mirrored. Kept as literals rather than imported so this module has no\n39\t# dependency on `reference/`, and pinned by `tests/test_routines.py` against the geometry helpers.\n40\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n41\t\n42\tOP_NAMES = [\n43\t    \"PASS\", \"NORTH\", \"SOUTH\", \"EAST\", \"WEST\",\n44\t    \"WATER\", \"HARVEST\", \"PLANT\", \"DIG\",\n45\t    \"PICKUP\", \"DROP\", \"PLACE\", \"FEED\", \"CARE\", \"COLLECT_FERTILIZER\", \"FERTILIZE\",\n46\t    \"BUILD_COOP\", \"BUILD_PASTURE\",\n47\t]\n48\tOP = {n: i for i, n in enumerate(OP_NAMES)}\n49\t\n50\tPRODUCTS = [\"WHEAT\", \"CARROT\", \"TOMATO\", \"STRAWBERRY\", \"MELON\", \"EGG\", \"MILK\", \"WOOL\", \"FERTILIZER\"]\n51\tCROPS = [\"WHEAT\", \"CARROT\", \"TOMATO\", \"STRAWBERRY\", \"MELON\"]\n52\tANIMAL_NAMES = [\"GOOSE\", \"COW\", \"SHEEP\"]\n53\tITEMS = PRODUCTS + ANIMAL_NAMES          # the `unit_item` domain, 12 wide\n54\t\n55\tANIMALS = {\n56\t    \"GOOSE\": {\"cost\": 300, \"structure\": \"COOP\",    \"first_yield_day\": 4, \"interval\": 1, \"max_held\": 4, \"product\": \"EGG\"},\n57\t    \"COW\":   {\"cost\": 400, \"structure\": \"PASTURE\", \"first_yield_day\": 8, \"interval\": 2, \"max_held\": 6, \"product\": \"MILK\"},\n58\t    \"SHEEP\": {\"cost\": 500, \"structure\": \"PASTURE\", \"first_yield_day\": 6, \"interval\": 3, \"max_held\": 6, \"product\": \"WOOL\"},\n59\t}\n60\tSTRUCTURE_OP = {\"COOP\": OP[\"BUILD_COOP\"], \"PASTURE\": OP[\"BUILD_PASTURE\"]}\n61\t\n62\tLAND_ORDER = [\"NE\", \"SW\", \"SE\"]\n63\tLAND_PRICES = [1000, 2000, 4000]\n64\t\n65\tBOARD_SIZE = 10\n66\tTURNS_PER_DAY = 24\n67\tSEASON_DAYS = 30\n68\tT_DEFAULT = 719          # the recorder's length: 30 days of 24 turns, minus the unplayed last turn\n69\tU_DEFAULT = 24\n70\t\n71\t\n72\tdef quadrant_of(x, y, board_size=BOARD_SIZE):\n73\t    half = board_size // 2\n74\t    return (\"N\" if y < half else \"S\") + (\"W\" if x < half else \"E\")\n75\t\n76\t\n77\tdef shed_access_tiles(board_size=BOARD_SIZE):\n78\t    \"\"\"The four inner-corner tiles around the shed, in the reference's NWSE order.\"\"\"\n79\t    half = board_size // 2\n80\t    return [(half - 1, half - 1), (half, half - 1), (half - 1, half), (half, half)]\n81\t\n82\t\n83\tdef land_purchases_for(tile, board_size=BOARD_SIZE):\n84\t    \"\"\"How many sequential BUY_LAND orders are needed before `tile` is unlocked.\n85\t\n86\t    `_do_buy_land` ignores its caller's wishes and unlocks `LAND_ORDER[n_already_bought]`, so\n87\t    reaching SE costs all three purchases whether or not NE and SW are wanted.\n88\t    \"\"\"\n89\t    q = quadrant_of(*tile, board_size=board_size)\n90\t    if q == \"NW\":\n91\t        return 0\n92\t    return LAND_ORDER.index(q) + 1\n93\t\n94\t\n95\tclass Script:\n96\t    \"\"\"A mutable action tape: `[T, U]` unit ops plus the per-turn market vector.\n97\t\n98\t    Deliberately plain arrays rather than a builder DSL. The optimiser mutates *plans*, expands them\n99\t    through the routines below into one of these, and hands the arrays straight to the simulator --\n100\t    the same arrays `scripts/build_script_submission.py` already turns into a verified submission.\n101\t    \"\"\"\n102\t\n103\t    def __init__(self, T=T_DEFAULT, U=U_DEFAULT):\n104\t        self.T, self.U = T, U\n105\t        self.unit_op = np.zeros((T, U), np.int64)      # 0 == PASS\n106\t        self.unit_crop = np.zeros((T, U), np.int64)\n107\t        self.unit_item = np.zeros((T, U), np.int64)\n108\t        self.unit_qty = np.zeros((T, U), np.int64)\n109\t        self.sell = np.zeros((T, len(PRODUCTS)), np.int64)\n110\t        self.buy_seed = np.zeros((T, len(CROPS)), np.int64)\n111\t        self.buy_product = np.zeros((T, len(PRODUCTS)), np.int64)\n112\t        self.buy_animal = np.zeros((T, len(ANIMAL_NAMES)), np.int64)\n113\t        self.hire = np.zeros(T, np.int64)\n114\t        self.buy_land = np.zeros(T, np.int64)\n115\t\n116\t    def arrays(self):\n117\t        return {f: getattr(self, f) for f in\n118\t                (\"unit_op\", \"unit_crop\", \"unit_item\", \"unit_qty\", \"sell\", \"buy_seed\",\n119\t                 \"buy_product\", \"buy_animal\", \"hire\", \"buy_land\")}\n120\t\n121\t    def op(self, t, u, name, item=None, qty=0, crop=None):\n122\t        \"\"\"Write one unit op. Out-of-range turns are dropped so a routine may run past the season.\"\"\"\n123\t        if not (0 <= t < self.T):\n124\t            return\n125\t        self.unit_op[t, u] = OP[name]\n126\t        if item is not None:\n127\t            self.unit_item[t, u] = ITEMS.index(item)\n128\t            self.unit_qty[t, u] = qty\n129\t        if crop is not None:\n130\t            self.unit_crop[t, u] = CROPS.index(crop)\n131\t\n132\t\n133\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n134\t# Routines\n135\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n136\t\n137\tdef ranch(script, unit, animal, tile, start_day, start_pos=(4, 4), sell=True):\n138\t    \"\"\"Build a pen on `tile`, stock it, and feed it every remaining day of the season.\n139\t\n140\t    Emits the complete chain and nothing partial. There is deliberately no primitive that buys an\n141\t    animal without also committing the feed loop that keeps it alive -- that combination is the\n142\t    $300-$500 deterministic loss the search must never be able to propose.\n143\t\n144\t    `tile` must be a shed-access tile so the loop needs no movement. `start_pos` is the unit's spawn,\n145\t    which is deterministic only for the farmer at `(4,4)`; a hand's spawn depends on which quadrants\n146\t    were unlocked when it was hired, so a caller placing hands must supply the truth.\n147\t    \"\"\"\n148\t    if tuple(tile) not in shed_access_tiles():\n149\t        raise ValueError(f\"{tile} is not a shed-access tile; the loop would need movement\")\n150\t    if animal not in ANIMALS:\n151\t        raise ValueError(f\"unknown animal {animal}\")\n152\t\n153\t    info = ANIMALS[animal]\n154\t    product = info[\"product\"]\n155\t    a_idx = ANIMAL_NAMES.index(animal)\n156\t    wheat = PRODUCTS.index(\"WHEAT\")\n157\t    prod_idx = PRODUCTS.index(product)\n158\t\n159\t    # --- land, before anything is built on a locked tile -------------------\n160\t    n_land = land_purchases_for(tile)\n161\t    for k in range(n_land):\n162\t        # One per turn on day 0. HIRE and BUY_LAND are atomic per order *slot*, so stacking them on\n163\t        # one turn is legal, but spreading them keeps the ten-slot budget free for feed and sells.\n164\t        script.buy_land[k] = 1\n165\t\n166\t    base = start_day * TURNS_PER_DAY\n167\t\n168\t    # --- walk to the cell, if it is not the spawn -------------------------\n169\t    t = base\n170\t    dx = tile[0] - start_pos[0]\n171\t    dy = tile[1] - start_pos[1]\n172\t    for _ in range(abs(dx)):\n173\t        script.op(t, unit, \"EAST\" if dx > 0 else \"WEST\")\n174\t        t += 1\n175\t    for _ in range(abs(dy)):\n176\t        script.op(t, unit, \"SOUTH\" if dy > 0 else \"NORTH\")\n177\t        t += 1\n178\t\n179\t    # --- build, stock, place ---------------------------------------------\n180\t    script.op(t, unit, \"BUILD_PASTURE\" if info[\"structure\"] == \"PASTURE\" else \"BUILD_COOP\")\n181\t    # Ordered on the build turn so the animal and the first feed are in the shed by the next turn,\n182\t    # whichever side of the unit phase the market resolves on.\n183\t    script.buy_animal[t, a_idx] += 1\n184\t    script.buy_product[t, wheat] += 1\n185\t    t += 1\n186\t    script.op(t, unit, \"PICKUP\", item=animal, qty=1); t += 1\n187\t    script.op(t, unit, \"PLACE\", item=animal, qty=1); t += 1\n188\t    # Feed on the placement day too. Missing it is survivable -- escape needs two consecutive unfed\n189\t    # days -- but it costs a production day and there is no reason to spend one.\n190\t    script.op(t, unit, \"PICKUP\", item=\"WHEAT\", qty=1); t += 1\n191\t    script.op(t, unit, \"FEED\"); t += 1\n192\t    script.op(t, unit, \"CARE\"); t += 1\n193\t\n194\t    # --- the daily loop ---------------------------------------------------\n195\t    for day in range(start_day + 1, SEASON_DAYS):\n196\t        d = day * TURNS_PER_DAY\n197\t        # Buy the feed a turn before it is drawn, so the order has resolved either way.\n198\t        script.buy_product[d, wheat] += 1\n199\t        script.op(d + 1, unit, \"PICKUP\", item=\"WHEAT\", qty=1)\n200\t        script.op(d + 2, unit, \"FEED\")\n201\t        # CARE grants +1 yield on the next fed production day, which roughly doubles output for one\n202\t        # turn a day. It is why CARE correlates +0.441 with money across real leaderboard seats.\n203\t        script.op(d + 3, unit, \"CARE\")\n204\t        script.op(d + 4, unit, \"HARVEST\")\n205\t        script.op(d + 5, unit, \"DROP\")\n206\t        if sell:\n207\t            # Over-requesting is safe: `_process_market` stops when the shed runs dry. Selling every\n208\t            # day is the naive cadence; timing is a plan parameter the search should own.\n209\t            script.sell[d + 6, prod_idx] += info[\"max_held\"]\n210\t\n211\t    return script\n212\t\n213\t\n214\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n215\t# Inspection helpers, used by the tests to check the tape rather than trust it\n216\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n217\t\n218\tdef _turns_with(script, unit, op_name):\n219\t    return np.flatnonzero(np.asarray(script.unit_op)[:, unit] == OP[op_name])\n220\t\n221\t\n222\tdef feed_days(script, unit):\n223\t    return {int(t) // TURNS_PER_DAY for t in _turns_with(script, unit, \"FEED\")}\n224\t\n225\t\n226\tdef place_day(script, unit):\n227\t    ts = _turns_with(script, unit, \"PLACE\")\n228\t    return int(ts[0]) // TURNS_PER_DAY if len(ts) else None\n229\t\n230\t\n231\tdef build_turn(script, unit):\n232\t    ops = np.asarray(script.unit_op)[:, unit]\n233\t    ts = np.flatnonzero((ops == OP[\"BUILD_PASTURE\"]) | (ops == OP[\"BUILD_COOP\"]))\n234\t    return int(ts[0]) if len(ts) else None\n235\t"}]},"uuid":"861fe73b-027d-49aa-9871-4552ae00f0bb","timestamp":"2026-08-04T09:25:54.457Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture/kagg/search/routines.py","content":"\"\"\"Macro-routines: the chain is the unit of search, not the sub-action.\n\n**Why this module exists.** A sub-action search cannot find the animal economy, and that is a\nstructural fact rather than a tuning problem. Two mechanics close the door:\n\n- `consecutive_unfed >= 2` escapes the animal (`kaggriculture.py:786`), so buying one you will not\n  feed every day is a *deterministic* loss of $300-$500, not a bad expectation.\n- FEED is illegal until an animal is on the board, unfed, with a unit standing on it carrying wheat\n  (`jax_masks.py:93`), so a policy samples FEED from a distribution where it has zero mass.\n\nEvery prefix of the chain is therefore money-negative and every complete chain needs ~15 coordinated\nedits at specific tiles on specific days. A hill-climber selecting on money rejects step one forever;\nPPO learns to stop buying animals, which re-masks FEED and closes the loop. Both failures are the\nsame wall.\n\nThe fix is not a better optimiser. It is a representation in which a partial chain **cannot be\nexpressed**. A routine emits the whole chain or nothing, so the valley is not crossed -- it is not in\nthe space. What the search then owns is *which* routine, *where*, *when* and *how many*, which is\nstrategy, while correctness lives in the decoder.\n\n**The ranch cell.** The four shed-access tiles are `(4,4) (5,4) (4,5) (5,5)` -- the inner corners\naround the shed. PICKUP, DROP and shed-PLACE test only shed-adjacency, and FEED/CARE/HARVEST test\nonly that the unit stands on the animal's tile. Put the pen *on* a shed-access tile and one unit can\nrun the entire loop -- draw feed, feed, care, harvest, deposit -- **without ever moving**, in 5 of\nthe day's 24 turns. That matters beyond convenience: a unit's tile is the cumulative sum of its\nmovement ops, so in an open-loop script one edited step silently invalidates every later op. A\nzero-movement routine has no such coupling, which is what makes it a safe unit of search.\n\n**Land is the gate.** Only `(4,4)` is in NW, the sole quadrant unlocked at the start; the other three\ncells begin LOCKED. `LAND_ORDER` is `['NE','SW','SE']` at `[1000, 2000, 4000]`, bought in sequence,\nso cell `(5,5)` costs $7,000 of land to reach. This is also why an agent that never buys land plays\nthe whole season on 25 tiles of 100.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# The reference's own tables, mirrored. Kept as literals rather than imported so this module has no\n# dependency on `reference/`, and pinned by `tests/test_routines.py` against the geometry helpers.\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nOP_NAMES = [\n    \"PASS\", \"NORTH\", \"SOUTH\", \"EAST\", \"WEST\",\n    \"WATER\", \"HARVEST\", \"PLANT\", \"DIG\",\n    \"PICKUP\", \"DROP\", \"PLACE\", \"FEED\", \"CARE\", \"COLLECT_FERTILIZER\", \"FERTILIZE\",\n    \"BUILD_COOP\", \"BUILD_PASTURE\",\n]\nOP = {n: i for i, n in enumerate(OP_NAMES)}\n\nPRODUCTS = [\"WHEAT\", \"CARROT\", \"TOMATO\", \"STRAWBERRY\", \"MELON\", \"EGG\", \"MILK\", \"WOOL\", \"FERTILIZER\"]\nCROPS = [\"WHEAT\", \"CARROT\", \"TOMATO\", \"STRAWBERRY\", \"MELON\"]\nANIMAL_NAMES = [\"GOOSE\", \"COW\", \"SHEEP\"]\nITEMS = PRODUCTS + ANIMAL_NAMES          # the `unit_item` domain, 12 wide\n\nANIMALS = {\n    \"GOOSE\": {\"cost\": 300, \"structure\": \"COOP\",    \"first_yield_day\": 4, \"interval\": 1, \"max_held\": 4, \"product\": \"EGG\"},\n    \"COW\":   {\"cost\": 400, \"structure\": \"PASTURE\", \"first_yield_day\": 8, \"interval\": 2, \"max_held\": 6, \"product\": \"MILK\"},\n    \"SHEEP\": {\"cost\": 500, \"structure\": \"PASTURE\", \"first_yield_day\": 6, \"interval\": 3, \"max_held\": 6, \"product\": \"WOOL\"},\n}\nSTRUCTURE_OP = {\"COOP\": OP[\"BUILD_COOP\"], \"PASTURE\": OP[\"BUILD_PASTURE\"]}\n\nLAND_ORDER = [\"NE\", \"SW\", \"SE\"]\nLAND_PRICES = [1000, 2000, 4000]\n\nBOARD_SIZE = 10\nTURNS_PER_DAY = 24\nSEASON_DAYS = 30\nT_DEFAULT = 719          # the recorder's length: 30 days of 24 turns, minus the unplayed last turn\nU_DEFAULT = 24\n\n\ndef quadrant_of(x, y, board_size=BOARD_SIZE):\n    half = board_size // 2\n    return (\"N\" if y < half else \"S\") + (\"W\" if x < half else \"E\")\n\n\ndef shed_access_tiles(board_size=BOARD_SIZE):\n    \"\"\"The four inner-corner tiles around the shed, in the reference's NWSE order.\"\"\"\n    half = board_size // 2\n    return [(half - 1, half - 1), (half, half - 1), (half - 1, half), (half, half)]\n\n\ndef land_purchases_for(tile, board_size=BOARD_SIZE):\n    \"\"\"How many sequential BUY_LAND orders are needed before `tile` is unlocked.\n\n    `_do_buy_land` ignores its caller's wishes and unlocks `LAND_ORDER[n_already_bought]`, so\n    reaching SE costs all three purchases whether or not NE and SW are wanted.\n    \"\"\"\n    q = quadrant_of(*tile, board_size=board_size)\n    if q == \"NW\":\n        return 0\n    return LAND_ORDER.index(q) + 1\n\n\nclass Script:\n    \"\"\"A mutable action tape: `[T, U]` unit ops plus the per-turn market vector.\n\n    Deliberately plain arrays rather than a builder DSL. The optimiser mutates *plans*, expands them\n    through the routines below into one of these, and hands the arrays straight to the simulator --\n    the same arrays `scripts/build_script_submission.py` already turns into a verified submission.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, T=T_DEFAULT, U=U_DEFAULT):\n        self.T, self.U = T, U\n        self.unit_op = np.zeros((T, U), np.int64)      # 0 == PASS\n        self.unit_crop = np.zeros((T, U), np.int64)\n        self.unit_item = np.zeros((T, U), np.int64)\n        self.unit_qty = np.zeros((T, U), np.int64)\n        self.sell = np.zeros((T, len(PRODUCTS)), np.int64)\n        self.buy_seed = np.zeros((T, len(CROPS)), np.int64)\n        self.buy_product = np.zeros((T, len(PRODUCTS)), np.int64)\n        self.buy_animal = np.zeros((T, len(ANIMAL_NAMES)), np.int64)\n        self.hire = np.zeros(T, np.int64)\n        self.buy_land = np.zeros(T, np.int64)\n\n    def arrays(self):\n        return {f: getattr(self, f) for f in\n                (\"unit_op\", \"unit_crop\", \"unit_item\", \"unit_qty\", \"sell\", \"buy_seed\",\n                 \"buy_product\", \"buy_animal\", \"hire\", \"buy_land\")}\n\n    def op(self, t, u, name, item=None, qty=0, crop=None):\n        \"\"\"Write one unit op. Out-of-range turns are dropped so a routine may run past the season.\"\"\"\n        if not (0 <= t < self.T):\n            return\n        self.unit_op[t, u] = OP[name]\n        if item is not None:\n            self.unit_item[t, u] = ITEMS.index(item)\n            self.unit_qty[t, u] = qty\n        if crop is not None:\n            self.unit_crop[t, u] = CROPS.index(crop)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Routines\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef ranch(script, unit, animal, tile, start_day, start_pos=(4, 4), sell=True):\n    \"\"\"Build a pen on `tile`, stock it, and feed it every remaining day of the season.\n\n    Emits the complete chain and nothing partial. There is deliberately no primitive that buys an\n    animal without also committing the feed loop that keeps it alive -- that combination is the\n    $300-$500 deterministic loss the search must never be able to propose.\n\n    `tile` must be a shed-access tile so the loop needs no movement. `start_pos` is the unit's spawn,\n    which is deterministic only for the farmer at `(4,4)`; a hand's spawn depends on which quadrants\n    were unlocked when it was hired, so a caller placing hands must supply the truth.\n    \"\"\"\n    if tuple(tile) not in shed_access_tiles():\n        raise ValueError(f\"{tile} is not a shed-access tile; the loop would need movement\")\n    if animal not in ANIMALS:\n        raise ValueError(f\"unknown animal {animal}\")\n\n    info = ANIMALS[animal]\n    product = info[\"product\"]\n    a_idx = ANIMAL_NAMES.index(animal)\n    wheat = PRODUCTS.index(\"WHEAT\")\n    prod_idx = PRODUCTS.index(product)\n\n    # --- land, before anything is built on a locked tile -------------------\n    n_land = land_purchases_for(tile)\n    for k in range(n_land):\n        # One per turn on day 0. HIRE and BUY_LAND are atomic per order *slot*, so stacking them on\n        # one turn is legal, but spreading them keeps the ten-slot budget free for feed and sells.\n        script.buy_land[k] = 1\n\n    base = start_day * TURNS_PER_DAY\n\n    # --- walk to the cell, if it is not the spawn -------------------------\n    t = base\n    dx = tile[0] - start_pos[0]\n    dy = tile[1] - start_pos[1]\n    for _ in range(abs(dx)):\n        script.op(t, unit, \"EAST\" if dx > 0 else \"WEST\")\n        t += 1\n    for _ in range(abs(dy)):\n        script.op(t, unit, \"SOUTH\" if dy > 0 else \"NORTH\")\n        t += 1\n\n    # --- build, stock, place ---------------------------------------------\n    script.op(t, unit, \"BUILD_PASTURE\" if info[\"structure\"] == \"PASTURE\" else \"BUILD_COOP\")\n    # Ordered on the build turn so the animal and the first feed are in the shed by the next turn,\n    # whichever side of the unit phase the market resolves on.\n    script.buy_animal[t, a_idx] += 1\n    script.buy_product[t, wheat] += 1\n    t += 1\n    script.op(t, unit, \"PICKUP\", item=animal, qty=1); t += 1\n    script.op(t, unit, \"PLACE\", item=animal, qty=1); t += 1\n    # Feed on the placement day too. Missing it is survivable -- escape needs two consecutive unfed\n    # days -- but it costs a production day and there is no reason to spend one.\n    script.op(t, unit, \"PICKUP\", item=\"WHEAT\", qty=1); t += 1\n    script.op(t, unit, \"FEED\"); t += 1\n    script.op(t, unit, \"CARE\"); t += 1\n\n    # --- the daily loop ---------------------------------------------------\n    for day in range(start_day + 1, SEASON_DAYS):\n        d = day * TURNS_PER_DAY\n        # Buy the feed a turn before it is drawn, so the order has resolved either way.\n        script.buy_product[d, wheat] += 1\n        script.op(d + 1, unit, \"PICKUP\", item=\"WHEAT\", qty=1)\n        script.op(d + 2, unit, \"FEED\")\n        # CARE grants +1 yield on the next fed production day, which roughly doubles output for one\n        # turn a day. It is why CARE correlates +0.441 with money across real leaderboard seats.\n        script.op(d + 3, unit, \"CARE\")\n        script.op(d + 4, unit, \"HARVEST\")\n        script.op(d + 5, unit, \"DROP\")\n        if sell:\n            # Over-requesting is safe: `_process_market` stops when the shed runs dry. Selling every\n            # day is the naive cadence; timing is a plan parameter the search should own.\n            script.sell[d + 6, prod_idx] += info[\"max_held\"]\n\n    return script\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Inspection helpers, used by the tests to check the tape rather than trust it\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef _turns_with(script, unit, op_name):\n    return np.flatnonzero(np.asarray(script.unit_op)[:, unit] == OP[op_name])\n\n\ndef feed_days(script, unit):\n    return {int(t) // TURNS_PER_DAY for t in _turns_with(script, unit, \"FEED\")}\n\n\ndef place_day(script, unit):\n    ts = _turns_with(script, unit, \"PLACE\")\n    return int(ts[0]) // TURNS_PER_DAY if len(ts) else None\n\n\ndef build_turn(script, unit):\n    ops = np.asarray(script.unit_op)[:, unit]\n    ts = np.flatnonzero((ops == OP[\"BUILD_PASTURE\"]) | (ops == OP[\"BUILD_COOP\"]))\n    return int(ts[0]) if len(ts) else None\n","numLines":235,"startLine":1,"totalLines":235}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"009a60a6-a864-4dbf-995f-59ff59ba2947","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture","sessionId":"6e91a562-2c58-4e1d-8c6f-dd43634ac84a","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"humming-hopping-marshmallow"}

FEED is\n4\tillegal until an animal is on the board unfed with a unit carrying wheat (`jax_masks.py:93`), and\n5\tbuying the animal first is a *deterministic* loss -- `consecutive_unfed >= 2` escapes it\n6\t(`kaggriculture.py:786`) against $300-$500 and a first yield 4-8 days out. So every partial prefix\n7\tof the chain is money-negative, and neither a hill-climber selecting on money nor a policy sampling\n8\tfrom a distribution where FEED has zero mass can assemble it.\n9\t\n10\tThe fix is not a better optimiser. It is a representation in which a partial chain **is not\n11\trepresentable**. That is what `kagg/search/routines.py` provides, and what these tests pin:\n12\t\n13\t- the chain completes and the animal survives the whole season (never two unfed days running)\n14\t- the routine pays for itself in the real simulator, not in an argument\n15\t- a bought-but-unfed animal cannot be expressed through the API at all\n16\t- cells outside NW emit the `buy_land` that unlocks them, because only `(4,4)` starts unlocked\n17\t\"\"\"\n18\t\n19\timport numpy as np\n20\timport pytest\n21\t\n22\tfrom kagg.search import routines as R\n23\tfrom kagg.sim import jax_sim as J\n24\t\n25\tFIELDS = (\"unit_op\", \"unit_crop\", \"unit_item\", \"unit_qty\",\n26\t          \"sell\", \"buy_seed\", \"buy_product\", \"buy_animal\", \"hire\", \"buy_land\")\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tdef run(script, seed=0):\n30\t    \"\"\"Final money for seat 0 running `script`, seat 1 passing. Weeds off, so this is exact.\"\"\"\n31\t    import jax.numpy as jnp\n32\t\n33\t    state = J.reset_with_shops(1, seed=seed)\n34\t    arrs = script.arrays()\n35\t    for t in range(script.T):\n36\t        acts = {}\n37\t        for f in FIELDS:\n38\t            mine = np.asarray(arrs[f][t])\n39\t            theirs = np.zeros_like(mine)\n40\t            acts[f] = jnp.asarray(np.stack([mine, theirs])[None])   # [1, 2, ...]\n41\t        state = J.step(state, J.Actions(**acts), weed_chance=0.0)\n42\t    return float(np.asarray(state.farm.money)[0, 0])\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tdef test_a_shed_access_cell_is_the_only_unlocked_one():\n46\t    \"\"\"Geometry check. Three of the four cells start LOCKED, so `buy_land` gates the ranch.\"\"\"\n47\t    tiles = R.shed_access_tiles()\n48\t    assert tiles == [(4, 4), (5, 4), (4, 5), (5, 5)]\n49\t    unlocked = [t for t in tiles if R.quadrant_of(*t) == \"NW\"]\n50\t    assert unlocked == [(4, 4)], \"only (4,4) starts unlocked; the rest need BUY_LAND\"\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef test_a_ranch_routine_keeps_its_animal_alive_all_season():\n54\t    \"\"\"No two consecutive unfed days, checked on the emitted tape rather than trusted.\n55\t\n56\t    The escape rule is the whole reason a partial chain is fatal, so the routine must guarantee a\n57\t    FEED every day the animal is on the board -- not merely usually.\n58\t    \"\"\"\n59\t    s = R.Script()\n60\t    R.ranch(s, unit=0, animal=\"COW\", tile=(4, 4), start_day=1)\n61\t    fed_days = R.feed_days(s, unit=0)\n62\t    placed = R.place_day(s, unit=0)\n63\t    for d in range(placed, R.SEASON_DAYS):\n64\t        assert d in fed_days, f\"day {d} has no FEED; the animal escapes after two\"\n65\t\n66\t\n67\tdef test_a_ranch_routine_pays_for_itself_in_the_real_simulator():\n68\t    \"\"\"The claim that matters, measured rather than argued.\"\"\"\n69\t    empty = R.Script()\n70\t    baseline = run(empty)\n71\t\n72\t    ranched = R.Script()\n73\t    R.ranch(ranched, unit=0, animal=\"COW\", tile=(4, 4), start_day=1)\n74\t    with_ranch = run(ranched)\n75\t\n76\t    assert with_ranch > baseline, (\n77\t        f\"ranch lost money: ${with_ranch:,.0f} vs baseline ${baseline:,.0f}\")\n78\t\n79\t\n80\tdef test_a_partial_chain_cannot_be_expressed():\n81\t    \"\"\"There is no API by which the search can buy an animal it will not feed.\n82\t\n83\t    This is the property that makes the valley unreachable instead of merely deep.\n84\t    \"\"\"\n85\t    assert not hasattr(R, \"buy_animal\"), \"no raw buy-animal primitive may be exposed\"\n86\t    s = R.Script()\n87\t    R.ranch(s, unit=0, animal=\"COW\", tile=(4, 4), start_day=1)\n88\t    bought = int(np.asarray(s.buy_animal).sum())\n89\t    feeds = len(R.feed_days(s, unit=0))\n90\t    assert bought == 1 and feeds >= R.SEASON_DAYS - 2, (\n91\t        f\"bought {bought} animals but only {feeds} feed days\")\n92\t\n93\t\n94\t@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"tile\", [(5, 4), (4, 5), (5, 5)])\n95\tdef test_a_locked_cell_buys_its_own_land(tile):\n96\t    \"\"\"A routine placed outside NW must unlock its quadrant before it builds.\"\"\"\n97\t    s = R.Script()\n98\t    R.ranch(s, unit=0, animal=\"COW\", tile=tile, start_day=1)\n99\t    assert int(np.asarray(s.buy_land).sum()) >= 1, f\"{tile} is LOCKED and no BUY_LAND was emitted\"\n100\t    build_t = R.build_turn(s, unit=0)\n101\t    land_t = int(np.flatnonzero(np.asarray(s.buy_land))[0])\n102\t    assert land_t <= build_t, \"BUY_LAND must precede BUILD on a locked tile\"\n103\t\n104\t\n105\tdef test_a_ranch_never_moves_off_its_cell():\n106\t    \"\"\"The cell is chosen so PICKUP, FEED, CARE, HARVEST and DROP all work from one tile.\n107\t\n108\t    Movement is what makes an open-loop op script brittle: a unit's tile is the cumulative sum of\n109\t    its movement ops, so one edited step invalidates every later op. A zero-movement routine has no\n110\t    such coupling.\n111\t    \"\"\"\n112\t    s = R.Script()\n113\t    R.ranch(s, unit=0, animal=\"COW\", tile=(4, 4), start_day=1)\n114\t    ops = np.asarray(s.unit_op)[:, 0]\n115\t    moves = np.isin(ops, [R.OP[\"NORTH\"], R.OP[\"SOUTH\"], R.OP[\"EAST\"], R.OP[\"WEST\"]]).sum()\n116\t    assert moves == 0, f\"{moves} movement ops emitted for a cell the unit already stands on\"\n117\t"}]},"uuid":"1cc10698-f32d-41ef-b051-f6eb2793e1a3","timestamp":"2026-08-04T09:25:54.816Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture/tests/test_routines.py","content":"\"\"\"The chain is the unit of search: a routine must be correct by construction.\n\nThese tests exist because a sub-action search provably cannot find the animal economy. FEED is\nillegal until an animal is on the board unfed with a unit carrying wheat (`jax_masks.py:93`), and\nbuying the animal first is a *deterministic* loss -- `consecutive_unfed >= 2` escapes it\n(`kaggriculture.py:786`) against $300-$500 and a first yield 4-8 days out. So every partial prefix\nof the chain is money-negative, and neither a hill-climber selecting on money nor a policy sampling\nfrom a distribution where FEED has zero mass can assemble it.\n\nThe fix is not a better optimiser. It is a representation in which a partial chain **is not\nrepresentable**. That is what `kagg/search/routines.py` provides, and what these tests pin:\n\n- the chain completes and the animal survives the whole season (never two unfed days running)\n- the routine pays for itself in the real simulator, not in an argument\n- a bought-but-unfed animal cannot be expressed through the API at all\n- cells outside NW emit the `buy_land` that unlocks them, because only `(4,4)` starts unlocked\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pytest\n\nfrom kagg.search import routines as R\nfrom kagg.sim import jax_sim as J\n\nFIELDS = (\"unit_op\", \"unit_crop\", \"unit_item\", \"unit_qty\",\n          \"sell\", \"buy_seed\", \"buy_product\", \"buy_animal\", \"hire\", \"buy_land\")\n\n\ndef run(script, seed=0):\n    \"\"\"Final money for seat 0 running `script`, seat 1 passing. Weeds off, so this is exact.\"\"\"\n    import jax.numpy as jnp\n\n    state = J.reset_with_shops(1, seed=seed)\n    arrs = script.arrays()\n    for t in range(script.T):\n        acts = {}\n        for f in FIELDS:\n            mine = np.asarray(arrs[f][t])\n            theirs = np.zeros_like(mine)\n            acts[f] = jnp.asarray(np.stack([mine, theirs])[None])   # [1, 2, ...]\n        state = J.step(state, J.Actions(**acts), weed_chance=0.0)\n    return float(np.asarray(state.farm.money)[0, 0])\n\n\ndef test_a_shed_access_cell_is_the_only_unlocked_one():\n    \"\"\"Geometry check. Three of the four cells start LOCKED, so `buy_land` gates the ranch.\"\"\"\n    tiles = R.shed_access_tiles()\n    assert tiles == [(4, 4), (5, 4), (4, 5), (5, 5)]\n    unlocked = [t for t in tiles if R.quadrant_of(*t) == \"NW\"]\n    assert unlocked == [(4, 4)], \"only (4,4) starts unlocked; the rest need BUY_LAND\"\n\n\ndef test_a_ranch_routine_keeps_its_animal_alive_all_season():\n    \"\"\"No two consecutive unfed days, checked on the emitted tape rather than trusted.\n\n    The escape rule is the whole reason a partial chain is fatal, so the routine must guarantee a\n    FEED every day the animal is on the board -- not merely usually.\n    \"\"\"\n    s = R.Script()\n    R.ranch(s, unit=0, animal=\"COW\", tile=(4, 4), start_day=1)\n    fed_days = R.feed_days(s, unit=0)\n    placed = R.place_day(s, unit=0)\n    for d in range(placed, R.SEASON_DAYS):\n        assert d in fed_days, f\"day {d} has no FEED; the animal escapes after two\"\n\n\ndef test_a_ranch_routine_pays_for_itself_in_the_real_simulator():\n    \"\"\"The claim that matters, measured rather than argued.\"\"\"\n    empty = R.Script()\n    baseline = run(empty)\n\n    ranched = R.Script()\n    R.ranch(ranched, unit=0, animal=\"COW\", tile=(4, 4), start_day=1)\n    with_ranch = run(ranched)\n\n    assert with_ranch > baseline, (\n        f\"ranch lost money: ${with_ranch:,.0f} vs baseline ${baseline:,.0f}\")\n\n\ndef test_a_partial_chain_cannot_be_expressed():\n    \"\"\"There is no API by which the search can buy an animal it will not feed.\n\n    This is the property that makes the valley unreachable instead of merely deep.\n    \"\"\"\n    assert not hasattr(R, \"buy_animal\"), \"no raw buy-animal primitive may be exposed\"\n    s = R.Script()\n    R.ranch(s, unit=0, animal=\"COW\", tile=(4, 4), start_day=1)\n    bought = int(np.asarray(s.buy_animal).sum())\n    feeds = len(R.feed_days(s, unit=0))\n    assert bought == 1 and feeds >= R.SEASON_DAYS - 2, (\n        f\"bought {bought} animals but only {feeds} feed days\")\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"tile\", [(5, 4), (4, 5), (5, 5)])\ndef test_a_locked_cell_buys_its_own_land(tile):\n    \"\"\"A routine placed outside NW must unlock its quadrant before it builds.\"\"\"\n    s = R.Script()\n    R.ranch(s, unit=0, animal=\"COW\", tile=tile, start_day=1)\n    assert int(np.asarray(s.buy_land).sum()) >= 1, f\"{tile} is LOCKED and no BUY_LAND was emitted\"\n    build_t = R.build_turn(s, unit=0)\n    land_t = int(np.flatnonzero(np.asarray(s.buy_land))[0])\n    assert land_t <= build_t, \"BUY_LAND must precede BUILD on a locked tile\"\n\n\ndef test_a_ranch_never_moves_off_its_cell():\n    \"\"\"The cell is chosen so PICKUP, FEED, CARE, HARVEST and DROP all work from one tile.\n\n    Movement is what makes an open-loop op script brittle: a unit's tile is the cumulative sum of\n    its movement ops, so one edited step invalidates every later op. A zero-movement routine has no\n    such coupling.\n    \"\"\"\n    s = R.Script()\n    R.ranch(s, unit=0, animal=\"COW\", tile=(4, 4), start_day=1)\n    ops = np.asarray(s.unit_op)[:, 0]\n    moves = np.isin(ops, [R.OP[\"NORTH\"], R.OP[\"SOUTH\"], R.OP[\"EAST\"], R.OP[\"WEST\"]]).sum()\n    assert moves == 0, f\"{moves} movement ops emitted for a cell the unit already stands on\"\n","numLines":117,"startLine":1,"totalLines":117}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d691f0be-9b38-4051-98b8-42801505e66d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture","sessionId":"6e91a562-2c58-4e1d-8c6f-dd43634ac84a","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"humming-hopping-marshmallow"}
