{"parentUuid":"de3ffd52-0657-4eb9-a151-42e80162c6c6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5bffb09b-9cfa-457a-b68e-4ca7752df84e","agentId":"af0fe4f4b572e87a8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XEgQ2fMyjydKwz3tsbUJMf","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The plan interpreter: turns a :class:`~kagg.search.plan.Plan` into one turn's actions.\n2\t\n3\tDeliberately mechanical. Everything here is *how to carry out* a plan, never *what plan to carry\n4\tout* -- no crop preferences, no sell cadence, no hiring curve, no priority between crops. Those all\n5\tlive in the genes so that search owns them.\n6\t\n7\tThe only judgements baked in are ones that are forced by the rules rather than by taste:\n8\t\n9\t- a unit works the **nearest** outstanding job inside its own block of tiles, because walking is\n10\t  the dominant cost and the alternative is to search over routes as well;\n11\t- within a tile, **DIG > HARVEST > WATER > PLANT**, because a weed blocks everything, an unharvested\n12\t  ongoing crop stops producing, and an unwatered plant dies after two days;\n13\t- a unit never requests a PLANT the farm has no seed for, because `interpreter` drops *every*\n14\t  PLANT for a crop when the requests exceed the seeds held.\n15\t\n16\tRunning the same code on both simulators\n17\t----------------------------------------\n18\t\n19\t`decide` reads a :class:`View` -- a handful of arrays -- and writes a :class:`Decision`. Both the\n20\tbatched simulator (`kagg.search.rollout`) and a live `kaggle_environments` observation\n21\t(`kagg.search.agent`) build the same View, so there is exactly one copy of the logic and no chance\n22\tof the searched behaviour and the submitted behaviour drifting apart.\n23\t\n24\tTwo divergences between the two simulators had to be designed around rather than discovered later:\n25\t\n26\t**Market order slots.** The simulator settles every product's sell orders against both players\n27\talternating unit by unit, then handles seeds, hiring and land. The reference instead pairs the\n28\tplayers' *i-th market order*. Those agree only if both players put the same product in the same\n29\tslot. So the emitted order list has a **fixed 8-slot layout** -- one slot per sellable crop, then\n30\tseed, hire, land -- with unwanted slots filled by ``[\"SELL\", crop, 0]``, which `_parse_order`\n31\trejects while the slot itself still lines up. That also keeps the list inside\n32\t`maxMarketOrdersPerTurn`, which the simulator does not model at all.\n33\t\n34\t**Atomic PLANT.** The reference drops all PLANT requests for a crop when they exceed seeds held;\n35\tthe simulator instead plants greedily unit by unit until the seeds run out. The parity gate never\n36\tcaught this because its random scripts only ever move one unit. Not requesting more plants than\n37\tthere are seeds makes the two agree, and `tests/test_search_executor.py` pins it.\n38\t\"\"\"\n39\t\n40\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n41\t\n42\timport numpy as np\n43\t\n44\tfrom kagg.sim import ops as O\n45\tfrom kagg.search.plan import (\n46\t    BASE_PRICE,\n47\t    BLOCK_DAYS,\n48\t    BOARD,\n49\t    COOP,\n50\t    FERT_APPLY,\n51\t    FERT_SELL,\n52\t    FIRST_YIELD_DAY,\n53\t    HARVEST_DELTA,\n54\t    MAX_YIELD_DAY,\n55\t    N_BLOCKS,\n56\t    N_CROPS,\n57\t    N_ANIMALS,\n58\t    NO_STRUCTURE,\n59\t    ONGOING,\n60\t    ONGOING_THRESHOLD,\n61\t    PASTURE as PLAN_PASTURE,\n62\t    SEED_COST,\n63\t    TILES,\n64\t)\n65\t\n66\tN_PRODUCTS = 9        # market.N_PRODUCTS; the sell/buy_product axis width\n67\t\n68\t# `market.ANIMAL_COST`, inlined for the same reason the crop tables above are: this module is pure\n69\t# numpy and imported by the submission path, so it must not pull in jax.\n70\tANIMAL_COST = np.array([300.0, 400.0, 500.0])\n71\t\n72\t# Ceiling used only to size a wheat order against cash. Wheat's base price is $25 and it rises as the\n73\t# town runs dry, so budgeting at the top of the range under-orders rather than overdrawing.\n74\tWHEAT_PRICE_CAP = 60.0\n75\t\n76\t# `plan.COOP`, renamed at the import site to avoid colliding with the tile-kind COOP below.\n77\tCOOP_GENE_KIND = 1\n78\t\n79\t# Tile kinds, matching `kagg.sim.tiles`.\n80\tEMPTY, LOCKED, WEED, PLANT, COOP, PASTURE = range(6)\n81\t\n82\tMAX_EXEC_UNITS = 16      # the hire gene tops out at 14 hands plus the farmer\n83\tN_MARKET_SLOTS = 8\n84\t\n85\t\n86\tdef _boustrophedon():\n87\t    \"\"\"Tile visiting order: serpentine rows, so consecutive plan genes are spatial neighbours.\"\"\"\n88\t    out = []\n89\t    for y in range(BOARD):\n90\t        xs = range(BOARD) if y % 2 == 0 else reversed(range(BOARD))\n91\t        for x in xs:\n92\t            out.append((x, y))\n93\t    return out\n94\t\n95\t\n96\tORDER = _boustrophedon()\n97\tOX = np.array([p[0] for p in ORDER], dtype=np.int16)\n98\tOY = np.array([p[1] for p in ORDER], dtype=np.int16)\n99\tTILE_OF_ORD = (OY.astype(np.int64) * BOARD + OX.astype(np.int64))\n100\tORD_OF_TILE = np.zeros(TILES, dtype=np.int16)\n101\tORD_OF_TILE[TILE_OF_ORD] = np.arange(TILES, dtype=np.int16)\n102\tDIST = (np.abs(OX[:, None] - OX[None, :]) + np.abs(OY[:, None] - OY[None, :])).astype(np.int16)\n103\t\n104\t# Neighbour order-position in each of the four move directions, -1 off the board.\n105\t_DIRS = [(0, -1), (0, 1), (1, 0), (-1, 0)]        # N, S, E, W -- matches ops.NORTH..WEST\n106\tNEIGH = np.full((TILES, 4), -1, dtype=np.int16)\n107\tfor _i, (_x, _y) in enumerate(ORDER):\n108\t    for _d, (_dx, _dy) in enumerate(_DIRS):\n109\t        _nx, _ny = _x + _dx, _y + _dy\n110\t        if 0 <= _nx < BOARD and 0 <= _ny < BOARD:\n111\t            NEIGH[_i, _d] = ORD_OF_TILE[_ny * BOARD + _nx]\n112\tMOVE_OPS = np.array([O.NORTH, O.SOUTH, O.EAST, O.WEST], dtype=np.int64)\n113\t\n114\t(JOB_NONE, JOB_DIG, JOB_HARVEST, JOB_WATER, JOB_PLANT,\n115\t JOB_FEED, JOB_CARE, JOB_COLLECT, JOB_PLACE, JOB_BUILD) = range(10)\n116\tJOB_TO_OP = np.array([O.NOOP, O.DIG, O.HARVEST, O.WATER, O.PLANT_OP,\n117\t                      O.FEED, O.CARE, O.COLLECT_FERT, O.PLACE, O.BUILD_PASTURE], dtype=np.int64)\n118\t\n119\t# **Jobs that only some units can serve**, because the rules gate them on what the unit is carrying:\n120\t# `jax_masks.py:96` needs `carrying_wheat` for FEED, and PLACE needs the animal in hand. `tile_jobs`\n121\t# is per-tile and cannot know, so `decide` masks these out for units that are not equipped.\n122\tCARRY_GATED = (JOB_FEED, JOB_PLACE)\n123\t\n124\t# Item indices into the shed/carried axis, which is `PRODUCTS + ANIMALS` (`market.py:42`).\n125\tWHEAT_ITEM = 0\n126\tANIMAL_ITEM_BASE = 9        # market.N_PRODUCTS; asserted in tests/test_executor_animals.py\n127\t\n128\t# Where a night leaves every unit. `ops.SHED_TILES == [44, 45, 54, 55]`, and the simulator respawns\n129\t# the whole crew there at dawn -- so hour 0 is a **free** PICKUP window that needs no routing at all.\n130\t# Sending a unit to fetch wheat mid-day is what turned `jax_frontier` into a 70.5% PICKUP loop.\n131\tSHED_ORDS = ORD_OF_TILE[np.asarray(O.SHED_TILES)].astype(np.int64)\n132\t\n133\t_BIG = np.int16(30000)\n134\t\n135\t\n136\t@dataclass\n137\tclass View:\n138\t    \"\"\"Everything the interpreter reads, batched over B games, in serpentine tile order.\"\"\"\n139\t\n140\t    day: int\n141\t    hour: int\n142\t    kind: np.ndarray            # [B, TILES] int\n143\t    crop: np.ndarray            # [B, TILES] int, -1 where no plant\n144\t    planted_day: np.ndarray     # [B, TILES] int\n145\t    watered: np.ndarray         # [B, TILES] bool\n146\t    yield_units: np.ndarray     # [B, TILES] int\n147\t    unit_ord: np.ndarray        # [B, MAX_EXEC_UNITS] int, serpentine position of each unit\n148\t    n_units: np.ndarray         # [B] int\n149\t    money: np.ndarray           # [B] float\n150\t    seeds: np.ndarray           # [B, N_CROPS] int\n151\t    shed: np.ndarray            # [B, N_CROPS] int\n152\t    prices: np.ndarray          # [B, N_CROPS] int\n153\t    n_land: np.ndarray          # [B] int\n154\t\n155\t    # **Livestock, appended with defaults on 2026-08-04.** Defaults so `agent.py` -- which builds a\n156\t    # View by hand from a live observation dict -- keeps working unchanged and simply runs crop-only.\n157\t    # A required field here would break the submission path on import.\n158\t    animal: np.ndarray = None               # [B, TILES] int, -1 where none\n159\t    fed_today: np.ndarray = None            # [B, TILES] bool\n160\t    cared_today: np.ndarray = None          # [B, TILES] bool\n161\t    consecutive_unfed: np.ndarray = None    # [B, TILES] int\n162\t    fertilizer_available: np.ndarray = None  # [B, TILES] bool\n163\t    shed_animals: np.ndarray = None         # [B, N_ANIMALS] int\n164\t    shed_wheat: np.ndarray = None           # [B] int\n165\t    carried_wheat: np.ndarray = None        # [B, MAX_EXEC_UNITS] int\n166\t    carried_animal: np.ndarray = None       # [B, MAX_EXEC_UNITS] int, -1 where none\n167\t\n168\t    def __post_init__(self):\n169\t        \"\"\"Fill the livestock fields with 'no animals anywhere' when a caller omits them.\"\"\"\n170\t        b, t = self.kind.shape\n171\t        u = self.unit_ord.shape[1]\n172\t        blanks = dict(\n173\t            animal=lambda: np.full((b, t), -1, dtype=np.int64),\n174\t            fed_today=lambda: np.zeros((b, t), dtype=bool),\n175\t            cared_today=lambda: np.zeros((b, t), dtype=bool),\n176\t            consecutive_unfed=lambda: np.zeros((b, t), dtype=np.int64),\n177\t            fertilizer_available=lambda: np.zeros((b, t), dtype=bool),\n178\t            shed_animals=lambda: np.zeros((b, N_ANIMALS), dtype=np.int64),\n179\t            shed_wheat=lambda: np.zeros(b, dtype=np.int64),\n180\t            carried_wheat=lambda: np.zeros((b, u), dtype=np.int64),\n181\t            carried_animal=lambda: np.full((b, u), -1, dtype=np.int64),\n182\t        )\n183\t        for name, make in blanks.items():\n184\t            if getattr(self, name) is None:\n185\t                object.__setattr__(self, name, make())\n186\t\n187\t\n188\t@dataclass\n189\tclass Decision:\n190\t    \"\"\"One turn of actions for B games, in the layout `kagg.sim.game.Actions` wants.\"\"\"\n191\t\n192\t    unit_op: np.ndarray         # [B, MAX_EXEC_UNITS]\n193\t    unit_crop: np.ndarray       # [B, MAX_EXEC_UNITS]\n194\t    sell: np.ndarray            # [B, N_CROPS]\n195\t    buy_seed: np.ndarray        # [B, N_CROPS]\n196\t    hire: np.ndarray            # [B] bool\n197\t    buy_land: np.ndarray        # [B] bool\n198\t    # Livestock. `game.Actions` has carried these slots all along; the executor never used them.\n199\t    unit_item: np.ndarray = None    # [B, MAX_EXEC_UNITS] item index for PICKUP / PLACE\n200\t    unit_qty: np.ndarray = None     # [B, MAX_EXEC_UNITS] quantity for PICKUP\n201\t    buy_animal: np.ndarray = None   # [B, N_ANIMALS] animals to order this turn\n202\t    buy_product: np.ndarray = None  # [B, N_PRODUCTS] products to buy -- wheat, as feed\n203\t\n204\t    def __post_init__(self):\n205\t        b, u = self.unit_op.shape\n206\t        if self.unit_item is None:\n207\t            object.__setattr__(self, \"unit_item\", np.zeros((b, u), dtype=np.int64))\n208\t        if self.unit_qty is None:\n209\t            object.__setattr__(self, \"unit_qty\", np.zeros((b, u), dtype=np.int64))\n210\t        if self.buy_animal is None:\n211\t            object.__setattr__(self, \"buy_animal\", np.zeros((b, N_ANIMALS), dtype=np.int64))\n212\t        if self.buy_product is None:\n213\t            object.__setattr__(self, \"buy_product\", np.zeros((b, N_PRODUCTS), dtype=np.int64))\n214\t\n215\t\n216\tdef tile_jobs(view, plan):\n217\t    \"\"\"[B, TILES] job code per tile, and [B, TILES] bool for tiles this plan actually farms.\"\"\"\n218\t    day = view.day\n219\t    crop_of = plan.crop_of.astype(np.int64)\n220\t    farmed = (crop_of >= 0) & (view.kind != LOCKED)\n221\t\n222\t    want = np.maximum(crop_of, 0)\n223\t    growing = np.maximum(view.crop, 0)\n224\t\n225\t    # PLANT: an empty farmed tile, on its phase day, inside the planting window, with a seed held.\n226\t    have_seed = np.take_along_axis(view.seeds, want, axis=1) > 0\n227\t    phase_ok = (day % 5) == plan.phase\n228\t    plant = (\n229\t        (view.kind == EMPTY)\n230\t        & phase_ok\n231\t        & (day <= plan.stop_plant[:, None])\n232\t        & have_seed\n233\t    )\n234\t\n235\t    # HARVEST: the plan says how ripe to let each crop get. For a crop that clears the tile when\n236\t    # cut, that is an age relative to `max_yield_day`; for an ongoing crop it is a unit count.\n237\t    par = np.take_along_axis(plan.harvest_par.astype(np.int64), growing, axis=1)\n238\t    age = day - view.planted_day\n239\t    ongoing = ONGOING[growing]\n240\t    ripe_age = np.maximum(MAX_YIELD_DAY[growing] + HARVEST_DELTA[par], FIRST_YIELD_DAY[growing])\n241\t    ready = np.where(\n242\t        ongoing,\n243\t        (view.yield_units >= ONGOING_THRESHOLD[par]) & (age >= FIRST_YIELD_DAY[growing]),\n244\t        (view.yield_units > 0) & (age >= ripe_age),\n245\t    )\n246\t    is_plant = view.kind == PLANT\n247\t    harvest = is_plant & ready\n248\t    water = is_plant & ~view.watered\n249\t\n250\t    # ---- livestock ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n251\t    #\n252\t    # **Ordering is the whole risk here.** `jax_frontier` bought four animals, never once built a pen\n253\t    # (`max empty pens: 0` over 240 turns), and banked $380 with the livestock sitting in the shed as\n254\t    # dead capital. So: a structure gets built first, an animal is only ordered against a *standing\n255\t    # empty* pen (in `decide`), and FEED outranks everything optional because an animal unfed two\n256\t    # nights escapes with the whole purchase price (`jax_sim.py:1159`).\n257\t    wants_structure = plan.structure_of.astype(np.int64)\n258\t    build = (view.kind == EMPTY) & (wants_structure != NO_STRUCTURE) & (view.kind != LOCKED)\n259\t    is_structure = (view.kind == COOP) | (view.kind == PASTURE)\n260\t    has_animal = view.animal >= 0\n261\t\n262\t    # An empty pen is where a carried animal goes.\n263\t    place = is_structure & ~has_animal\n264\t    # Hungry first, then the free upkeep. CARE and COLLECT_FERT cost nothing but the action.\n265\t    feed = has_animal & ~view.fed_today\n266\t    care = has_animal & ~view.cared_today\n267\t    collect = has_animal & view.fertilizer_available\n268\t    animal_harvest = has_animal & (view.yield_units > 0)\n269\t\n270\t    # A structure tile is `FALLOW` in `crop_of` (`plan.decode`), so `farmed` excludes it -- livestock\n271\t    # jobs are therefore scored on their own mask rather than being multiplied away below.\n272\t    livestock = np.where(\n273\t        feed, JOB_FEED,\n274\t        np.where(animal_harvest, JOB_HARVEST,\n275\t                 np.where(care, JOB_CARE,\n276\t                          np.where(collect, JOB_COLLECT,\n277\t                                   np.where(place, JOB_PLACE,\n278\t                                            np.where(build, JOB_BUILD, JOB_NONE))))),\n279\t    )\n280\t\n281\t    job = np.where(\n282\t        view.kind == WEED, JOB_DIG,\n283\t        np.where(harvest, JOB_HARVEST,\n284\t                 np.where(water, JOB_WATER,\n285\t                          np.where(plant, JOB_PLANT, JOB_NONE))),\n286\t    )\n287\t    job = job * farmed\n288\t    job = np.where(livestock != JOB_NONE, livestock, job)\n289\t    return job, farmed | (livestock != JOB_NONE)\n290\t\n291\t\n292\tdef _owner(farmed, n_live):\n293\t    \"\"\"Split the farmed tiles into one contiguous block per live unit.\n294\t\n295\t    Contiguous in serpentine order, so a block is a compact strip of the farm and a unit is not\n296\t    asked to cross the board between jobs.\n297\t    \"\"\"\n298\t    rank = np.cumsum(farmed, axis=1) - 1\n299\t    total = np.maximum(farmed.sum(axis=1), 1)\n300\t    owner = (rank * n_live[:, None]) // total[:, None]\n301\t    return np.minimum(owner, (n_live - 1)[:, None])\n302\t\n303\t\n304\tdef _step_toward(pos, target, kind):\n305\t    \"\"\"One legal move from `pos` toward `target`; the longer axis first, then the other.\n306\t\n307\t    `pos` and `target` are ``[B, U]`` serpentine positions -- every unit in the batch is routed in\n308\t    one pass, because doing it per unit made the interpreter, not the simulator, the bottleneck.\n309\t    \"\"\"\n310\t    dx = (OX[target].astype(np.int16) - OX[pos]).astype(np.int16)\n311\t    dy = (OY[target].astype(np.int16) - OY[pos]).astype(np.int16)\n312\t\n313\t    horiz = np.where(dx > 0, 2, 3).astype(np.int8)       # E, W\n314\t    vert = np.where(dy > 0, 1, 0).astype(np.int8)        # S, N\n315\t    first = np.where(\n316\t        (np.abs(dx) >= np.abs(dy)) & (dx != 0), horiz, np.where(dy != 0, vert, horiz)\n317\t    )\n318\t    second = np.where(first == horiz, vert, horiz)\n319\t    first = np.where((dx == 0) & (dy == 0), -1, first)\n320\t    second = np.where((dx == 0) | (dy == 0), -1, second)\n321\t\n322\t    def legal(direction):\n323\t        nb = NEIGH[pos, np.maximum(direction, 0)]\n324\t        blocked = np.take_along_axis(kind, nb.astype(np.int64), axis=1) == LOCKED\n325\t        return (direction >= 0) & (nb >= 0) & ~blocked\n326\t\n327\t    direction = np.where(legal(first), first, np.where(legal(second), second, -1))\n328\t    return np.where(direction >= 0, MOVE_OPS[np.maximum(direction, 0)], O.NOOP)\n329\t\n330\t\n331\tdef decide(view, plan):\n332\t    \"\"\"Choose every unit's op and the turn's market orders for a whole batch.\"\"\"\n333\t    batch = view.n_units.shape[0]\n334\t    games = np.arange(batch)\n335\t    day, hour = view.day, view.hour\n336\t    units = np.arange(MAX_EXEC_UNITS)\n337\t\n338\t    job, farmed = tile_jobs(view, plan)\n339\t    n_live = np.clip(view.n_units, 1, MAX_EXEC_UNITS)\n340\t    owner = _owner(farmed, n_live)\n341\t    live = units[None, :] < view.n_units[:, None]                      # [B, U]\n342\t\n343\t    # Nearest outstanding job inside each unit's own block, for every unit at once.\n344\t    pos = view.unit_ord\n345\t    mine = (owner[:, None, :] == units[None, :, None]) & (job > JOB_NONE)[:, None, :]\n346\t    mine &= live[:, :, None]\n347\t    # **Carry-gated jobs.** FEED needs the unit to hold wheat (`jax_masks.py:96`) and PLACE needs the\n348\t    # animal in hand, so a unit that is not equipped must not claim the job -- otherwise it walks\n349\t    # there, cannot act, and the tile stays unserved while the animal starves.\n350\t    can_feed = (view.carried_wheat > 0)[:, :, None]\n351\t    can_place = (view.carried_animal >= 0)[:, :, None]\n352\t    is_feed = (job == JOB_FEED)[:, None, :]\n353\t    is_place = (job == JOB_PLACE)[:, None, :]\n354\t    mine &= ~(is_feed & ~can_feed)\n355\t    mine &= ~(is_place & ~can_place)\n356\t\n357\t    cost = np.where(mine, DIST[pos], _BIG)                             # [B, U, TILES]\n358\t    target = cost.argmin(axis=2)\n359\t    found = np.take_along_axis(cost, target[:, :, None], axis=2)[:, :, 0] < _BIG\n360\t\n361\t    target_job = np.take_along_axis(job, target, axis=1)\n362\t    at_target = pos == target\n363\t    unit_op = np.where(at_target, JOB_TO_OP[target_job], _step_toward(pos, target, view.kind))\n364\t    unit_crop = np.maximum(np.take_along_axis(plan.crop_of.astype(np.int64), target, axis=1), 0)\n365\t    unit_op = np.where(found, unit_op, O.NOOP)\n366\t\n367\t    # BUILD is two different ops depending on which structure the plan asked for, so it cannot be a\n368\t    # single entry in `JOB_TO_OP`.\n369\t    wants = np.take_along_axis(plan.structure_of.astype(np.int64), target, axis=1)\n370\t    building = found & at_target & (target_job == JOB_BUILD)\n371\t    unit_op = np.where(building & (wants == COOP_GENE_KIND), O.BUILD_COOP, unit_op)\n372\t\n373\t    # PLACE carries the animal's item index; the reference reads which animal from the unit's hand.\n374\t    unit_item = np.zeros_like(unit_op)\n375\t    unit_qty = np.zeros_like(unit_op)\n376\t    placing = found & at_target & (target_job == JOB_PLACE)\n377\t    unit_item = np.where(placing, ANIMAL_ITEM_BASE + np.maximum(view.carried_animal, 0), unit_item)\n378\t\n379\t    # ---- the dawn PICKUP window ----------------------------------------------------------------\n380\t    #\n381\t    # Every unit respawns on the shed at dawn (`ops.SHED_TILES`), so hour 0 is a free PICKUP with no\n382\t    # walking. Fetching feed mid-day is what turned `jax_frontier` into a 70.5% PICKUP loop, so it is\n383\t    # only ever done here. Units are claimed lowest-index first: animals to place, then feed wheat.\n384\t    at_shed = np.isin(pos, SHED_ORDS) & live\n385\t    has_animal_tiles = view.animal >= 0\n386\t    n_pens_free = (((view.kind == COOP) | (view.kind == PASTURE)).sum(axis=1)\n387\t                   - has_animal_tiles.sum(axis=1))\n388\t    shed_stock = view.shed_animals.sum(axis=1)\n389\t    want_collect = np.minimum(np.maximum(n_pens_free, 0), shed_stock)          # [B]\n390\t    herd_size = (view.animal >= 0).sum(axis=1)\n391\t    want_wheat = np.where(herd_size > 0, plan.feed_wheat * np.maximum(herd_size, 1), 0)\n392\t\n393\t    rank = np.cumsum(at_shed, axis=1) - 1                                     # 0-based among at-shed\n394\t    # **NOT gated on being idle.** The first version required `~found`, so a unit only fetched feed if\n395\t    # it had no other job -- and at dawn every unit has watering or planting to do. Nobody ever picked\n396\t    # up wheat, `carrying_wheat` was never true, FEED could never be legal, and the herd starved: a\n397\t    # measured 14 pens built, 3 animals bought, **0 FEED**, $30 in the bank. Keeping an animal alive\n398\t    # outranks watering a plant, so the lowest-indexed units are claimed for it whatever else is going\n399\t    # on. `hungry` is what makes this bounded -- once the herd is fed the claim stops.\n400\t    hungry = (has_animal_tiles & ~view.fed_today).sum(axis=1)\n401\t    collect_animal = at_shed & (rank < want_collect[:, None]) & (view.carried_animal < 0)\n402\t    n_carriers = np.minimum(np.maximum(hungry, 0), 3)\n403\t    fetch_wheat = (at_shed & ~collect_animal\n404\t                   & (rank >= want_collect[:, None])\n405\t                   & (rank < (want_collect + n_carriers)[:, None])\n406\t                   & (view.shed_wheat[:, None] > 0) & (want_wheat[:, None] > 0)\n407\t                   & (view.carried_wheat <= 0))\n408\t\n409\t    first_animal = np.argmax(view.shed_animals > 0, axis=1)\n410\t    unit_op = np.where(collect_animal, O.PICKUP, unit_op)\n411\t    unit_item = np.where(collect_animal, ANIMAL_ITEM_BASE + first_animal[:, None], unit_item)\n412\t    unit_qty = np.where(collect_animal, 1, unit_qty)\n413\t\n414\t    unit_op = np.where(fetch_wheat, O.PICKUP, unit_op)\n415\t    unit_item = np.where(fetch_wheat, WHEAT_ITEM, unit_item)\n416\t    unit_qty = np.where(fetch_wheat, np.minimum(want_wheat, 10)[:, None], unit_qty)\n417\t\n418\t    # Never ask to plant a crop the farm has no seed left for: the reference silently drops\n419\t    # *every* PLANT for that crop, which would waste the turn for several units at once. Units\n420\t    # claim seeds in index order, which is also the order the simulator spends them.\n421\t    seeds_left = view.seeds.copy()\n422\t    planting = found & at_target & (target_job == JOB_PLANT)\n423\t    for u in range(MAX_EXEC_UNITS):\n424\t        want = planting[:, u]\n425\t        if not want.any():\n426\t            continue\n427\t        crop = unit_crop[:, u]\n428\t        allowed = want & (seeds_left[games, crop] > 0)\n429\t        np.add.at(seeds_left, (games[allowed], crop[allowed]), -1)\n430\t        unit_op[:, u] = np.where(want & ~allowed, O.NOOP, unit_op[:, u])\n431\t\n432\t    # ---- market -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n433\t    block = min(day // BLOCK_DAYS, N_BLOCKS - 1)\n434\t    dumping = day >= plan.dump_day\n435\t    tick = (hour % np.maximum(plan.sell_every, 1)) == 0\n436\t\n437\t    price_ok = view.prices >= (plan.floor_frac * BASE_PRICE[None, :])\n438\t    qty = plan.sell_qty[:, block, :]\n439\t    qty = np.where(dumping[:, None], view.shed, np.where(price_ok, qty, 0))\n440\t    sell = np.where(tick[:, None], np.minimum(qty, view.shed), 0).astype(np.int64)\n441\t\n442\t    # One crop's seeds per turn, rotating, so the fixed 8-slot order list always fits.\n443\t    buy_seed = np.zeros((batch, N_CROPS), dtype=np.int64)\n444\t    c = hour % N_CROPS\n445\t    upcoming = (\n446\t        (view.kind == EMPTY)\n447\t        & farmed\n448\t        & (plan.crop_of == c)\n449\t        & (day <= plan.stop_plant[:, None])\n450\t        & (((plan.phase - day) % 5) <= 1)      # due today or tomorrow\n451\t    )\n452\t    need = np.maximum(upcoming.sum(axis=1) - view.seeds[:, c], 0)\n453\t    budget = np.maximum(view.money - plan.seed_reserve, 0.0)\n454\t    buy_seed[:, c] = np.minimum(need, np.floor(budget / SEED_COST[c])).astype(np.int64)\n455\t\n456\t    target_hands = plan.hires[games, min(day, plan.hires.shape[1] - 1)]\n457\t    hire = (view.n_units - 1) < target_hands\n458\t\n459\t    slot = np.minimum(view.n_land, plan.land_day.shape[1] - 1)\n460\t    buy_land = (view.n_land < plan.land_day.shape[1]) & (day >= plan.land_day[games, slot])\n461\t\n462\t    # ---- livestock orders ------------------------------------------------------------------------\n463\t    #\n464\t    # **An animal is only ordered against a standing empty pen.** This single condition is the fix for\n465\t    # `jax_frontier`, which bought its herd on day 0 against pens it never built and wrote off ~$1,400.\n466\t    # The shed count is subtracted too, or a slow placement queue would keep re-ordering.\n467\t    buy_animal = np.zeros((batch, N_ANIMALS), dtype=np.int64)\n468\t    housing = np.maximum(n_pens_free - shed_stock, 0)\n469\t    stocking = day >= plan.stock_day\n470\t    for a in range(N_ANIMALS):\n471\t        placed = (view.animal == a).sum(axis=1) + view.shed_animals[:, a]\n472\t        room = np.minimum(np.maximum(plan.herd[:, a] - placed, 0), housing)\n473\t        afford = np.floor(np.maximum(view.money, 0.0) / ANIMAL_COST[a]).astype(np.int64)\n474\t        take = np.where(stocking, np.minimum(room, afford), 0)\n475\t        buy_animal[:, a] = take\n476\t        housing = np.maximum(housing - take, 0)          # one pen per animal, first species first\n477\t\n478\t    # Wheat is *bought*, not grown, when it is feed: the leaders buy 967 units a season for 14\n479\t    # animals. `feed_wheat` is units per animal, so the order tops the shed up to that.\n480\t    buy_product = np.zeros((batch, N_PRODUCTS), dtype=np.int64)\n481\t    short = np.maximum(want_wheat - view.shed_wheat, 0)\n482\t    wheat_budget = np.floor(np.maximum(view.money - plan.seed_reserve, 0.0) / WHEAT_PRICE_CAP)\n483\t    buy_product[:, WHEAT_ITEM] = np.minimum(short, wheat_budget.astype(np.int64))\n484\t\n485\t    return Decision(\n486\t        unit_op=unit_op,\n487\t        unit_crop=unit_crop,\n488\t        sell=sell,\n489\t        buy_seed=buy_seed,\n490\t        hire=hire,\n491\t        buy_land=buy_land,\n492\t        unit_item=unit_item,\n493\t        unit_qty=unit_qty,\n494\t        buy_animal=buy_animal,\n495\t        buy_product=buy_product,\n496\t    )\n497\t"}]},"uuid":"db4f4667-8adf-415d-bd6f-86200bec887a","timestamp":"2026-08-04T09:47:01.214Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"de3ffd52-0657-4eb9-a151-42e80162c6c6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture","sessionId":"6e91a562-2c58-4e1d-8c6f-dd43634ac84a","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"humming-hopping-marshmallow"}

{"parentUuid":"ebf71408-162c-4b9a-a063-63a267dd25bb","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5bffb09b-9cfa-457a-b68e-4ca7752df84e","agentId":"af0fe4f4b572e87a8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017fkjjV8JdAhGdF2nd4P9pp","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Cross-entropy method over farm plans.\n2\t\n3\tWhy CEM rather than beam search or a GA\n4\t---------------------------------------\n5\t\n6\t**Beam search was rejected on the shape of the problem.** A beam over per-day decisions needs a\n7\tper-day score to prune on, and this game has none worth having: the whole point of the measured\n8\teconomics is that money banked early is money *not* invested, so a day-12 leader is routinely a\n9\tday-30 loser. Anything that prunes on partial return prunes exactly the strategies -- late-maturing\n10\tcrops, deferred selling, a slow capital ramp -- that the economics say are the prize. Pruning on the\n11\ttrue objective would need a rollout per node, at which point it is a population method with worse\n12\tsample efficiency.\n13\t\n14\t**CEM rather than a GA** because the genome is a set of independent categorical choices with no\n15\tmeaningful locality between genes, so crossover has no structure to exploit, while a factored\n16\tcategorical distribution has one very useful property: the fitted distribution is itself the\n17\tanswer. After the search, ``p(tile 37 = MELON)`` is a direct statement about that tile, with the\n18\tstrength of the evidence attached. That is what makes the result readable rather than merely a\n19\twinning genome, and readable is what the brief actually asks for.\n20\t\n21\tThe elite update is the standard one, smoothed and floored:\n22\t\n23\t    p <- (1 - alpha) * p + alpha * (elite frequency),  then clip to `floor` and renormalise\n24\t\n25\tThe floor matters more here than usual. With 277 genes and ~40 elites, a category can vanish from\n26\tthe elite set by chance in one generation and never come back; a 1% floor keeps every option alive\n27\tfor the whole run.\n28\t\n29\tTwo methodological guards, both taken from `docs/experiment_log.md` section 3, where the heuristic\n30\ttuner learned them expensively:\n31\t\n32\t- **Unlock schedules are resampled every generation.** A fixed set of schedules is a fixed set of\n33\t  seeds, and a fixed set of seeds reported +40% where held-out seeds said +12.8%.\n34\t- **The reported winner is re-scored on a fresh draw** before it is believed, because taking the\n35\t  best of N noisy estimates biases every comparison upward.\n36\t\"\"\"\n37\t\n38\timport numpy as np\n39\t\n40\tfrom kagg.search import plan as P\n41\tfrom kagg.search import rollout\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tdef _rank(win_rate, delta):\n45\t    \"\"\"Indices worst-to-best. Win rate first, money delta only to break its many ties.\n46\t\n47\t    Selecting on money is the documented way to pick the wrong agent in this project: `hold_days`\n48\t    14 banked the most and won the least. Delta appears here purely because a handful of games\n49\t    gives a very coarse win rate, and something has to order the plans that all lose 0-6.\n50\t    \"\"\"\n51\t    return np.lexsort((delta, win_rate))\n52\t\n53\t\n54\tclass Result:\n55\t    def __init__(self):\n56\t        self.history = []\n57\t        self.best_genome = None\n58\t        self.best_score = (-1.0, -np.inf)\n59\t        self.probs = None\n60\t\n61\t\n62\tdef search(opposition, generations=25, pop=192, elite_frac=0.2, alpha=0.55, floor=0.01,\n63\t           schedules=2, weed_chance=0.005, seed=0, log=print, start_probs=None):\n64\t    \"\"\"Run CEM against a fixed set of opponent plans. `opposition` is ``[M, N_GENES]``.\n65\t\n66\t    `start_probs` is the initial categorical distribution; `None` means uniform. Pass\n67\t    `P.probs_around(P.leaders_genome())` to search **around a plan we have measured working** rather\n68\t    than from scratch -- the leaders' allocation is known (`docs/episode_teardown_2026-08-03.md`) and\n69\t    starting uniform throws that away.\n70\t    \"\"\"\n71\t    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\n72\t    probs = P.uniform_probs() if start_probs is None else np.array(start_probs, dtype=float)\n73\t    opponents = P.decode(opposition)\n74\t    n_elite = max(4, int(pop * elite_frac))\n75\t    result = Result()\n76\t\n77\t    for gen in range(generations):\n78\t        genomes = P.sample(probs, pop, rng)\n79\t        if result.best_genome is not None:\n80\t            genomes[0] = result.best_genome          # elitism, so a good plan is never lost\n81\t\n82\t        draw = [rollout.sample_unlock_schedule(rng) for _ in range(schedules)]\n83\t        win, delta = rollout.evaluate(\n84\t            P.decode(genomes), opponents, draw,\n85\t            weed_chance=weed_chance, seed=int(rng.integers(1 << 30)),\n86\t        )\n87\t\n88\t        order = _rank(win, delta)\n89\t        elite = genomes[order[-n_elite:]]\n90\t        top = order[-1]\n91\t\n92\t        counts = np.zeros_like(probs)\n93\t        for gene in range(P.N_GENES):\n94\t            counts[gene, : P.N_CATS[gene]] = np.bincount(\n95\t                elite[:, gene], minlength=int(P.N_CATS[gene])\n96\t            )[: P.N_CATS[gene]]\n97\t        empirical = counts / counts.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n98\t        probs = (1 - alpha) * probs + alpha * empirical\n99\t        probs = np.where(probs > 0, np.maximum(probs, floor), 0.0)\n100\t        probs /= probs.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n101\t\n102\t        score = (float(win[top]), float(delta[top]))\n103\t        if score > result.best_score:\n104\t            result.best_score = score\n105\t            result.best_genome = genomes[top].copy()\n106\t        result.history.append({\n107\t            \"generation\": gen,\n108\t            \"best_win\": float(win[top]),\n109\t            \"best_delta\": float(delta[top]),\n110\t            \"mean_win\": float(win.mean()),\n111\t            \"elite_win\": float(win[order[-n_elite:]].mean()),\n112\t        })\n113\t        log(f\"gen {gen:3d} | best win {win[top]:.2f} delta {delta[top]:+9.0f} \"\n114\t            f\"| elite win {win[order[-n_elite:]].mean():.2f} | pop mean {win.mean():.2f}\")\n115\t\n116\t    result.probs = probs\n117\t    return result\n118\t\n119\t\n120\tdef confirm(genomes, opposition, schedules=6, weed_chance=0.005, seed=99):\n121\t    \"\"\"Re-score candidates on a fresh draw of unlock schedules -- the winner's-curse check.\"\"\"\n122\t    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\n123\t    draw = [rollout.sample_unlock_schedule(rng) for _ in range(schedules)]\n124\t    return rollout.evaluate(\n125\t        P.decode(genomes), P.decode(opposition), draw, weed_chance=weed_chance, seed=seed\n126\t    )\n127\t\n128\t\n129\tdef modal_genome(probs):\n130\t    \"\"\"The most likely plan under the fitted distribution.\"\"\"\n131\t    return probs.argmax(axis=1)\n132\t"}]},"uuid":"c83eb730-2f0b-41cc-b812-eabafe763740","timestamp":"2026-08-04T09:47:02.022Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ebf71408-162c-4b9a-a063-63a267dd25bb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture","sessionId":"6e91a562-2c58-4e1d-8c6f-dd43634ac84a","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"humming-hopping-marshmallow"}
{"parentUuid":"c83eb730-2f0b-41cc-b812-eabafe763740","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"af0fe4f4b572e87a8","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CdhVihTa1PoiNscnPdweZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Wa36BNfqGXwcdv3iKxQfQg","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture/kagg/search/rollout.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2789,"cache_read_input_tokens":100712,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2789,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdhVif5TG7LNSeRMrKHuM","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"332aa0c3-a9d9-4e13-a055-e78d1fa67dfc","timestamp":"2026-08-04T09:47:02.355Z","effort":"high","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture","sessionId":"6e91a562-2c58-4e1d-8c6f-dd43634ac84a","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"humming-hopping-marshmallow"}
{"parentUuid":"332aa0c3-a9d9-4e13-a055-e78d1fa67dfc","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5bffb09b-9cfa-457a-b68e-4ca7752df84e","agentId":"af0fe4f4b572e87a8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Wa36BNfqGXwcdv3iKxQfQg","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Playing plans against plans on the batched simulator.\n2\t\n3\tThe batch dimension is the population. One call to :func:`play` advances every candidate, every\n4\topponent and every seating of a generation in lockstep, so a 200-plan generation costs one pass of\n5\t719 simulator steps rather than 200 episodes.\n6\t\n7\tEverything here is *simulator* measurement. It is used to rank candidates during the search and for\n8\tnothing else -- the number that decides whether the search worked comes from `kagg.arena.evaluate`\n9\tin the real environment, because a plan that only wins in the simulator has proved nothing.\n10\t\"\"\"\n11\t\n12\timport numpy as np\n13\t\n14\tfrom kagg.search import executor as X\n15\tfrom kagg.search.plan import N_CROPS\n16\tfrom kagg.sim.game import Actions, Game\n17\tfrom kagg.sim.market import price\n18\tfrom kagg.sim.town import SHOPS, SHOP_INDEX\n19\t\n20\tSHOP_UNLOCK_INTERVAL = 3\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tdef sample_unlock_schedule(rng):\n24\t    \"\"\"A plausible shop unlock order: one random remaining shop every third day.\n25\t\n26\t    Mirrors `_end_of_day`, which unlocks on days divisible by `townShopUnlockInterval`. The order\n27\t    is the only meaningful seed-to-seed variation the simulator has, so it stands in for the\n28\t    reference environment's seed.\n29\t    \"\"\"\n30\t    order = list(rng.permutation(len(SHOPS)))\n31\t    return {SHOP_UNLOCK_INTERVAL * (k + 1): int(shop) for k, shop in enumerate(order)}\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tdef build_view(game, player, day, hour):\n35\t    \"\"\"Read one player's state out of a batched :class:`~kagg.sim.game.Game`.\"\"\"\n36\t    tiles, farm = game.tiles[player], game.farms[player]\n37\t    ordering = X.TILE_OF_ORD\n38\t    pos = farm.unit_pos[:, : X.MAX_EXEC_UNITS, :]\n39\t    return X.View(\n40\t        day=day,\n41\t        hour=hour,\n42\t        kind=tiles.kind[:, ordering],\n43\t        crop=tiles.crop[:, ordering],\n44\t        planted_day=tiles.planted_day[:, ordering],\n45\t        watered=tiles.watered_today[:, ordering],\n46\t        yield_units=tiles.yield_units[:, ordering],\n47\t        unit_ord=X.ORD_OF_TILE[pos[:, :, 1] * X.BOARD + pos[:, :, 0]].astype(np.int64),\n48\t        n_units=farm.n_units,\n49\t        money=farm.money,\n50\t        seeds=farm.seeds,\n51\t        shed=farm.shed[:, :N_CROPS],\n52\t        prices=price(game.inventory)[:, :N_CROPS],\n53\t        n_land=farm.n_land,\n54\t        # Livestock. Without these the animal genes are inert and CEM searches the crop-only\n55\t        # subspace -- which is what it did until 2026-08-04, topping out at $42,087 against\n56\t        # leaders banking $100k+ with 34.9% of revenue from milk and wool.\n57\t        animal=tiles.animal[:, ordering],\n58\t        fed_today=tiles.fed_today[:, ordering],\n59\t        cared_today=tiles.cared_today[:, ordering],\n60\t        consecutive_unfed=tiles.consecutive_unfed[:, ordering],\n61\t        fertilizer_available=tiles.fertilizer_available[:, ordering],\n62\t        shed_animals=farm.shed[:, X.ANIMAL_ITEM_BASE:],\n63\t        shed_wheat=farm.shed[:, X.WHEAT_ITEM],\n64\t        carried_wheat=farm.carried[:, : X.MAX_EXEC_UNITS, X.WHEAT_ITEM],\n65\t        carried_animal=_carried_animal(farm.carried[:, : X.MAX_EXEC_UNITS, :]),\n66\t    )\n67\t\n68\t\n69\tdef _carried_animal(carried):\n70\t    \"\"\"[B, U] animal id a unit is holding, or -1. `PLACE` needs to name the animal in hand.\"\"\"\n71\t    animals = carried[:, :, X.ANIMAL_ITEM_BASE:]\n72\t    holding = animals.sum(axis=2) > 0\n73\t    return np.where(holding, animals.argmax(axis=2), -1).astype(np.int64)\n74\t\n75\t\n76\tdef _write(actions, player, decision):\n77\t    actions.unit_op[:, player, : X.MAX_EXEC_UNITS] = decision.unit_op\n78\t    actions.unit_crop[:, player, : X.MAX_EXEC_UNITS] = decision.unit_crop\n79\t    actions.sell[:, player, :N_CROPS] = decision.sell\n80\t    actions.buy_seed[:, player, :] = decision.buy_seed\n81\t    actions.hire[:, player] = decision.hire\n82\t    actions.buy_land[:, player] = decision.buy_land\n83\t    # `Actions` has carried these four slots all along; the executor never filled them.\n84\t    actions.unit_item[:, player, : X.MAX_EXEC_UNITS] = decision.unit_item\n85\t    actions.unit_qty[:, player, : X.MAX_EXEC_UNITS] = decision.unit_qty\n86\t    actions.buy_animal[:, player, :] = decision.buy_animal\n87\t    actions.buy_product[:, player, :] = decision.buy_product\n88\t\n89\t\n90\tdef play(seat_plans, unlock_schedule, weed_chance=0.005, rng=None, steps=None):\n91\t    \"\"\"Play a full season. `seat_plans` is a pair of Plans, each already sized to the batch.\n92\t\n93\t    Returns final money as ``[B, 2]``.\n94\t    \"\"\"\n95\t    batch = seat_plans[0].size\n96\t    game = Game(batch, weed_chance=weed_chance, rng=rng, unlock_schedule=unlock_schedule)\n97\t    actions = Actions(batch)\n98\t    total = steps if steps is not None else 719\n99\t\n100\t    for step in range(total):\n101\t        day, hour = step // 24, step % 24\n102\t        for player in (0, 1):\n103\t            view = build_view(game, player, day, hour)\n104\t            _write(actions, player, X.decide(view, seat_plans[player]))\n105\t        if game.step(actions):\n106\t            break\n107\t    return game.money()\n108\t\n109\t\n110\tdef evaluate(candidates, opponents, schedules, weed_chance=0.005, seed=0):\n111\t    \"\"\"Score every candidate against every opponent, both seatings, on every unlock schedule.\n112\t\n113\t    Returns ``(win_rate[N], mean_delta[N])`` -- win rate is the selection criterion because the\n114\t    ladder is Elo and margin does not count; the money delta is carried only to break the many\n115\t    ties a 0-1 signal produces over a handful of games.\n116\t    \"\"\"\n117\t    n = candidates.size\n118\t    m = opponents.size\n119\t    wins = np.zeros(n)\n120\t    delta = np.zeros(n)\n121\t    games = 0\n122\t\n123\t    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\n124\t    for schedule in schedules:\n125\t        for swapped in (False, True):\n126\t            # Each candidate meets every opponent: candidate index varies slowest.\n127\t            cand = candidates.repeat(m)\n128\t            opp = opponents.take(np.tile(np.arange(m), n))\n129\t            pair = (opp, cand) if swapped else (cand, opp)\n130\t            money = play(pair, schedule, weed_chance=weed_chance, rng=rng)\n131\t            mine = money[:, 1] if swapped else money[:, 0]\n132\t            theirs = money[:, 0] if swapped else money[:, 1]\n133\t            wins += (mine > theirs).reshape(n, m).sum(axis=1)\n134\t            delta += (mine - theirs).reshape(n, m).sum(axis=1)\n135\t            games += m\n136\t\n137\t    return wins / games, delta / games\n138\t\n139\t\n140\tdef shop_name(index):\n141\t    return SHOPS[index]\n142\t\n143\t\n144\tdef shop_index(name):\n145\t    return SHOP_INDEX[name]\n146\t"}]},"uuid":"175c576a-9f9f-4801-92cb-669bf77fb498","timestamp":"2026-08-04T09:47:02.363Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"332aa0c3-a9d9-4e13-a055-e78d1fa67dfc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture","sessionId":"6e91a562-2c58-4e1d-8c6f-dd43634ac84a","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"humming-hopping-marshmallow"}

{"parentUuid":"493ac519-52c5-4e51-b5fb-db40f8b433de","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5bffb09b-9cfa-457a-b68e-4ca7752df84e","agentId":"af0fe4f4b572e87a8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EV8FZWbh2G9q6cnqofWC2n","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Read a winning seat's plan -- chains bound to squares -- out of a replay, as a genome.\n2\t\n3\t`plan.leaders_genome()` states the gap this closes:\n4\t\n5\t    \"The *spatial* layout is ours, not theirs -- the counts are measured but which tile holds what is\n6\t     not extracted yet, so pastures go nearest the shed and the crops fill outward in serpentine\n7\t     order. CEM will move them.\"\n8\t\n9\t`crop_of` is a per-tile gene, so which square holds which chain is exactly what the genome encodes\n10\tand exactly what was being guessed. The episode observations carry the whole `tiles` grid at every\n11\tstep, so nothing needs reconstructing from the action tape.\n12\t\n13\tIt also corrects three counts that `docs/episode_teardown_2026-08-03.md` took from three episodes.\n14\tMining all 312 seats (`scripts/mine_build_orders.py`) and reading one $132,245 seat square by square\n15\t(`scripts/extract_winner_plan.py`) gives:\n16\t\n17\t    pastures      28, not 14\n18\t    land parcels  1 (day 10), not 2 (days 7 and 10)\n19\t    dump day      ~20, not 26 -- and day 20 is exactly where TOWN_CENTER_DEMAND_SCHEDULE steps to 4x\n20\t\n21\t**This is a starting distribution, not an answer.** `plan.probs_around` keeps mass on every\n22\talternative so CEM can leave it; `tests/test_replay_plan.py` pins that no gene is pinned.\n23\t\"\"\"\n24\t\n25\timport collections\n26\timport json\n27\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n28\t\n29\timport numpy as np\n30\t\n31\tfrom . import plan as P\n32\t\n33\tTURNS_PER_DAY = 24\n34\t# `crop_of` is the reference's `tiles[y][x]` flattened row-major, which is how `executor.py` indexes\n35\t# it. Pinned by `tests/test_replay_plan.py` via the round-trip rather than assumed here.\n36\tdef tile_index(x, y):\n37\t    return y * P.BOARD + x\n38\t\n39\t\n40\t@dataclass\n41\tclass Layout:\n42\t    \"\"\"One seat's committed square roles plus its season parameters, all measured.\"\"\"\n43\t\n44\t    money: float = 0.0\n45\t    pasture: tuple = ()\n46\t    coop: tuple = ()\n47\t    crops: dict = field(default_factory=dict)          # (x, y) -> crop name\n48\t    animal_counts: dict = field(default_factory=dict)  # species -> live count at the end\n49\t    land_days: tuple = ()\n50\t    crew_by_day: tuple = ()\n51\t    animals_by_day: tuple = ()\n52\t    wheat_total: int = 0\n53\t    dump_day: int = 20\n54\t\n55\t    @property\n56\t    def n_pasture(self):\n57\t        return len(self.pasture)\n58\t\n59\t    @property\n60\t    def n_coop(self):\n61\t        return len(self.coop)\n62\t\n63\t    @property\n64\t    def crop_counts(self):\n65\t        return dict(collections.Counter(self.crops.values()))\n66\t\n67\t\n68\tdef _role(tile):\n69\t    if tile is None or isinstance(tile, str):\n70\t        return None, None\n71\t    if \"animal\" in tile:\n72\t        return \"ANIMAL\", tile[\"animal\"]\n73\t    kind = tile.get(\"kind\")\n74\t    if kind == \"PLANT\":\n75\t        return \"PLANT\", tile.get(\"crop\")\n76\t    if kind in (\"PASTURE\", \"COOP\"):\n77\t        return kind, None\n78\t    return None, None\n79\t\n80\t\n81\tdef layout_from_replay(path, seat=1, hold_share=0.70):\n82\t    \"\"\"Extract the square roles and season parameters for one seat.\n83\t\n84\t    A square's role is the *first* non-empty thing it held, because that is the commitment the plan\n85\t    made; an animal placed on a pasture later does not change which chain owns the square. Species is\n86\t    read from the live animals at the end instead, since `herd` is a per-species target.\n87\t\n88\t    `dump_day` is the earliest day from which `hold_share` of all sell volume still lies ahead --\n89\t    i.e. when the seat stops trickling and starts emptying the shed.\n90\t    \"\"\"\n91\t    ep = json.loads(open(path).read())\n92\t    steps = ep[\"steps\"]\n93\t    money = float(ep[\"rewards\"][seat])\n94\t\n95\t    role_of, crops = {}, {}\n96\t    land_days, crew, live = [], {}, {}\n97\t    wheat = 0\n98\t    sells = collections.Counter()\n99\t    animal_counts = {}\n100\t\n101\t    for t, step in enumerate(steps):\n102\t        day = t // TURNS_PER_DAY\n103\t        obs = step[seat].get(\"observation\") or step[0].get(\"observation\")\n104\t        farms = (obs or {}).get(\"farms\")\n105\t        if farms and seat < len(farms):\n106\t            farm = farms[seat]\n107\t            crew[day] = 1 + len(farm.get(\"hands\") or [])\n108\t            species = collections.Counter()\n109\t            n_live = 0\n110\t            for y, row in enumerate(farm[\"tiles\"]):\n111\t                for x, tile in enumerate(row):\n112\t                    role, subject = _role(tile)\n113\t                    if role is None:\n114\t                        continue\n115\t                    if role == \"ANIMAL\":\n116\t                        species[subject] += 1\n117\t                        n_live += 1\n118\t                    key = (x, y)\n119\t                    if key not in role_of:\n120\t                        # An animal implies the pasture/coop that must already be under it.\n121\t                        role_of[key] = \"PASTURE\" if role == \"ANIMAL\" else role\n122\t                        if role == \"PLANT\":\n123\t                            crops[key] = subject\n124\t                    elif role == \"PLANT\" and key not in crops:\n125\t                        crops[key] = subject\n126\t            live[day] = n_live\n127\t            if species:\n128\t                animal_counts = dict(species)\n129\t        action = step[seat].get(\"action\")\n130\t        if isinstance(action, dict):\n131\t            for order in action.get(\"market\") or []:\n132\t                if not order:\n133\t                    continue\n134\t                if order[0] in (\"BUY_LAND\", \"BUID_LAND\"):\n135\t                    land_days.append(day)\n136\t                elif order[0] == \"BUY_PRODUCT\" and len(order) >= 3 and order[1] == \"WHEAT\":\n137\t                    wheat += int(order[2])\n138\t                elif order[0] == \"SELL\" and len(order) >= 3:\n139\t                    # **WHEAT is excluded from the dump-day estimate.** It is bought as feed and\n140\t                    # resold as arbitrage, so it trades all season: 15,247 units for this seat with\n141\t                    # only 47.9% of them late, against 98-99% for MILK, WOOL and FERTILIZER. Folding\n142\t                    # it in dragged the estimated onset from day 20 to day 22 and buried the signal.\n143\t                    # `dump_day` governs holding produce for the demand window, which is what those\n144\t                    # three do.\n145\t                    if order[1] != \"WHEAT\":\n146\t                        sells[day] += int(order[2])\n147\t\n148\t    total = sum(sells.values()) or 1\n149\t    dump = 20\n150\t    for d in range(P.SEASON_DAYS):\n151\t        if sum(n for k, n in sells.items() if k >= d) / total < hold_share:\n152\t            dump = max(d - 1, 0)\n153\t            break\n154\t\n155\t    return Layout(\n156\t        money=money,\n157\t        pasture=tuple(sorted(k for k, v in role_of.items() if v == \"PASTURE\")),\n158\t        coop=tuple(sorted(k for k, v in role_of.items() if v == \"COOP\")),\n159\t        crops={k: v for k, v in crops.items() if role_of.get(k) == \"PLANT\" and v},\n160\t        animal_counts=animal_counts,\n161\t        land_days=tuple(sorted(set(land_days))),\n162\t        crew_by_day=tuple(crew.get(d, 0) for d in range(P.SEASON_DAYS)),\n163\t        animals_by_day=tuple(live.get(d, 0) for d in range(P.SEASON_DAYS)),\n164\t        wheat_total=wheat,\n165\t        dump_day=dump,\n166\t    )\n167\t\n168\t\n169\tdef _nearest(table, value):\n170\t    return int(np.argmin(np.abs(np.asarray(table, dtype=np.float64) - float(value))))\n171\t\n172\t\n173\tdef genome_from_layout(layout):\n174\t    \"\"\"Encode a `Layout` as a categorical genome, following `plan.leaders_genome`'s conventions.\"\"\"\n175\t    g = np.zeros(P.N_GENES, dtype=np.int64)\n176\t\n177\t    def put(name, value):\n178\t        a, b = P.gene_slice(name)\n179\t        g[a:b] = value\n180\t\n181\t    # ---- the squares: this is the part that was previously guessed --------\n182\t    use = np.zeros(P.TILES, dtype=np.int64)            # 0 == fallow\n183\t    for (x, y) in layout.pasture:\n184\t        use[tile_index(x, y)] = P.CROP_GENE_PASTURE\n185\t    for (x, y) in layout.coop:\n186\t        use[tile_index(x, y)] = P.CROP_GENE_COOP\n187\t    for (x, y), name in layout.crops.items():\n188\t        use[tile_index(x, y)] = P.CROP_NAMES.index(name) + 1\n189\t    a, b = P.gene_slice(\"crop_of\")\n190\t    g[a:b] = use\n191\t\n192\t    # ---- season parameters ------------------------------------------------\n193\t    put(\"phase\", 0)\n194\t    put(\"stop_plant\", _nearest(P.STOP_PLANT_DAYS, 14))\n195\t    put(\"harvest_par\", 2)                              # cut at max_yield_day\n196\t    put(\"sell_every\", _nearest(P.SELL_EVERY, 2))\n197\t    put(\"floor_frac\", 1)                               # 0.3x base\n198\t    put(\"seed_reserve\", 1)                             # keep $200 for feed\n199\t    put(\"sell_qty\", 3)                                 # 10/tick; `dump_day` does the heavy lifting\n200\t    put(\"dump_day\", _nearest(P.DUMP_DAYS, layout.dump_day))\n201\t    put(\"fert_policy\", P.FERT_APPLY_THEN_SELL)         # 243 collects per top seat, ~26 fertilises\n202\t\n203\t    # Crew is re-hired every dawn, so headcount is a genuinely daily decision.\n204\t    a, b = P.gene_slice(\"hires\")\n205\t    g[a:b] = [_nearest(P.HIRE_LEVELS, n) for n in layout.crew_by_day]\n206\t\n207\t    # Animals from day 0 if the seat had any on day 0.\n208\t    put(\"stock_day\", _nearest(P.STOCK_DAYS, 0 if layout.animals_by_day[0] else 4))\n209\t    # Wheat held per animal per day. `feed_wheat` is a stock target, not a season total.\n210\t    peak = max(max(layout.animals_by_day), 1)\n211\t    put(\"feed_wheat\", _nearest(P.FEED_WHEAT, layout.wheat_total / peak / P.SEASON_DAYS))\n212\t\n213\t    # Herd targets per species, from the live census. Clipped to the ladder, and never below the\n214\t    # pens available -- a herd target under the pen count leaves pens standing empty.\n215\t    a, _ = P.gene_slice(\"herd\")\n216\t    pens = max(layout.n_pasture + layout.n_coop, 1)\n217\t    counts = layout.animal_counts or {}\n218\t    for i, name in enumerate(P.ANIMAL_NAMES):\n219\t        want = counts.get(name, 0)\n220\t        g[a + i] = _nearest(P.HERD_LEVELS, min(want, pens))\n221\t\n222\t    # Land: one gene per parcel, in LAND_ORDER sequence, NEVER for parcels never bought.\n223\t    a, b = P.gene_slice(\"land_day\")\n224\t    g[a:b] = len(P.LAND_DAYS) - 1                      # NEVER\n225\t    for i, day in enumerate(layout.land_days[:3]):\n226\t        g[a + i] = _nearest(P.LAND_DAYS, day)\n227\t    return g\n228\t\n229\t\n230\tdef genome_from_replay(path, seat=1):\n231\t    return genome_from_layout(layout_from_replay(path, seat=seat))\n232\t"}]},"uuid":"89cfd8f9-b364-4b61-b673-6b932397ba14","timestamp":"2026-08-04T09:47:02.698Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"493ac519-52c5-4e51-b5fb-db40f8b433de","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture","sessionId":"6e91a562-2c58-4e1d-8c6f-dd43634ac84a","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"humming-hopping-marshmallow"}

{"parentUuid":"a8887228-86c2-4edc-a2b0-a59d6739dbf2","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5bffb09b-9cfa-457a-b68e-4ca7752df84e","agentId":"af0fe4f4b572e87a8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01565YvmR1Mqw7rsrEqSq7G4","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"A winning seat's *spatial* layout, encoded as a genome. Genome level, not action level.\n2\t\n3\t`plan.leaders_genome()` is honest about the gap it leaves:\n4\t\n5\t    \"The *spatial* layout is ours, not theirs -- the counts are measured but which tile holds what\n6\t     is not extracted yet, so pastures go nearest the shed and the crops fill outward in serpentine\n7\t     order. CEM will move them.\"\n8\t\n9\t`crop_of` is a per-tile gene, so which square holds what is exactly the thing the genome encodes and\n10\texactly the thing we were guessing. The episode observations carry the full `tiles` grid at every\n11\tstep, so it does not need guessing.\n12\t\n13\tIt also corrects three counts. `leaders_genome()` came from `docs/episode_teardown_2026-08-03.md`,\n14\tthree episodes: 14 pastures, dump day 26, two land parcels. Mining all 312 seats\n15\t(`scripts/mine_build_orders.py`) and reading one $132,245 seat square by square\n16\t(`scripts/extract_winner_plan.py`) gives 28 pastures, ~98% of sells on day 20+, and one parcel.\n17\t\n18\tThis tests the *genome*: that the layout survives encode -> decode. Whether it scores well is a\n19\tseparate question for `rollout.evaluate`, and whether CEM can improve on it is the point of having\n20\tit at all -- it is a starting distribution, not an answer.\n21\t\"\"\"\n22\t\n23\timport numpy as np\n24\timport pytest\n25\t\n26\tfrom kagg.search import plan as P\n27\tfrom kagg.search import replay_plan as RP\n28\t\n29\tEPISODE = \"data/89113699.json\"\n30\tSEAT = 1\n31\t\n32\t\n33\t@pytest.fixture(scope=\"module\")\n34\tdef layout():\n35\t    return RP.layout_from_replay(EPISODE, seat=SEAT)\n36\t\n37\t\n38\tdef test_the_replay_yields_the_measured_square_counts(layout):\n39\t    \"\"\"21 crop tiles (12 MELON, 9 CARROT) and 28 pasture tiles, per `data/logs/winner_plan.log`.\"\"\"\n40\t    assert layout.n_pasture == 28\n41\t    assert layout.n_coop == 0\n42\t    assert sum(layout.crop_counts.values()) == 21\n43\t    assert layout.crop_counts[\"MELON\"] == 12\n44\t    assert layout.crop_counts[\"CARROT\"] == 9\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tdef test_only_two_unlocked_quadrants_are_used(layout):\n48\t    \"\"\"One land parcel: NW + NE. Every committed square must lie in y < 5.\"\"\"\n49\t    for (x, y) in list(layout.pasture) + list(layout.crops):\n50\t        assert y < 5, f\"({x},{y}) is in a quadrant this seat never unlocked\"\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef test_the_genome_round_trips_the_layout(layout):\n54\t    \"\"\"The whole point: `crop_of` is per tile, so the layout must survive encode -> decode intact.\"\"\"\n55\t    g = RP.genome_from_layout(layout)\n56\t    assert g.shape == (P.N_GENES,)\n57\t    decoded = P.decode(g[None])\n58\t\n59\t    use = np.asarray(decoded.structure_of[0])\n60\t    crop = np.asarray(decoded.crop_of[0])\n61\t    for (x, y) in layout.pasture:\n62\t        assert use[y * P.BOARD + x] == P.PASTURE, f\"pasture at ({x},{y}) lost in decode\"\n63\t    for (x, y), name in layout.crops.items():\n64\t        i = y * P.BOARD + x\n65\t        assert use[i] == P.NO_STRUCTURE\n66\t        assert crop[i] == P.CROP_NAMES.index(name), f\"crop at ({x},{y}) decoded wrong\"\n67\t    assert int((use == P.PASTURE).sum()) == 28\n68\t    assert int((crop >= 0).sum()) == 21\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tdef test_it_corrects_the_three_counts_leaders_genome_guessed(layout):\n72\t    \"\"\"Different from `leaders_genome()` in the three places the measurement disagrees.\n73\t\n74\t    Not a style preference: 14 vs 28 pastures, day 26 vs day 20 dumping, and two vs one land parcel\n75\t    are each a measured correction, and each is worth testing so a future edit cannot quietly undo it.\n76\t    \"\"\"\n77\t    g = RP.genome_from_layout(layout)\n78\t    old = P.leaders_genome()\n79\t\n80\t    lo, hi = P.gene_slice(\"crop_of\")\n81\t    n_new = int((g[lo:hi] == P.CROP_GENE_PASTURE).sum())\n82\t    n_old = int((old[lo:hi] == P.CROP_GENE_PASTURE).sum())\n83\t    assert n_new == 28 and n_old == 14\n84\t\n85\t    lo, _ = P.gene_slice(\"dump_day\")\n86\t    assert P.DUMP_DAYS[g[lo]] == 20, \"the measured seats dump from day 20, when town demand hits 4x\"\n87\t\n88\t    lo, hi = P.gene_slice(\"land_day\")\n89\t    bought = int((P.LAND_DAYS[g[lo:hi]] != P.NEVER).sum())\n90\t    assert bought == 1, \"this seat bought exactly one parcel, on day 10\"\n91\t\n92\t\n93\tdef test_the_seed_is_a_distribution_not_an_answer(layout):\n94\t    \"\"\"`probs_around` must leave real mass on alternatives, or the seed caps the ceiling.\n95\t\n96\t    `CLAUDE.md`: a prior that is a shape is fine, a prior that is a policy is not. The test is that\n97\t    every gene keeps a route to every other category.\n98\t    \"\"\"\n99\t    g = RP.genome_from_layout(layout)\n100\t    probs = P.probs_around(g)\n101\t    assert probs.shape[0] == P.N_GENES\n102\t    for i in range(P.N_GENES):\n103\t        k = P.N_CATS[i]\n104\t        row = probs[i, :k]\n105\t        assert row.min() > 0.0, f\"gene {i} cannot reach one of its categories\"\n106\t        assert row.max() < 1.0, f\"gene {i} is pinned to the seed\"\n107\t"}]},"uuid":"e0aaede5-a72f-4dbe-ad8a-898086700c57","timestamp":"2026-08-04T09:47:03.044Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a8887228-86c2-4edc-a2b0-a59d6739dbf2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture","sessionId":"6e91a562-2c58-4e1d-8c6f-dd43634ac84a","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"humming-hopping-marshmallow"}

The expensive parts of a plan search -- *when* to buy each land\n5\tparcel, *how many* animals and of which kind, *how much* crew, *when* to sell -- are all recorded.\n6\tSearching for them from a crop-only seed is paying compute to rediscover what is already on disk.\n7\t\n8\tThis extracts, per seat, only the **plan-level** decisions -- the genome, not the action tape:\n9\t\n10\t- the day each `BUY_LAND` fired, and how many parcels were bought at all\n11\t- the animal portfolio: which animals, bought on which day\n12\t- `HIRE` orders per day, i.e. crew size, which the dawn reset makes a daily decision\n13\t- `BUY_PRODUCT` wheat, the feed supply that the wheat-into-animal-product conversion runs on\n14\t- `SELL` volume per product per day, to see the real cadence against our naive sell-everything-daily\n15\t- the op census that tells us whether a seat ran animals at all (FEED / CARE / COLLECT_FERTILIZER)\n16\t\n17\tIt doubles as the coverage test for the routine library: any pattern here that\n18\t`kagg/search/routines.py` cannot express at *any* parameters is a measured gap, not an opinion.\n19\t\n20\t    PYTHONPATH=. python3 -u scripts/mine_build_orders.py --top 12 2>&1 | tee data/logs/mine_build_orders.log\n21\t\"\"\"\n22\t\n23\timport argparse\n24\timport collections\n25\timport glob\n26\timport json\n27\timport pathlib\n28\t\n29\tTURNS_PER_DAY = 24\n30\tANIMALS = (\"GOOSE\", \"COW\", \"SHEEP\")\n31\tUNIT_OPS = (\"FEED\", \"CARE\", \"COLLECT_FERTILIZER\", \"HARVEST\", \"PLANT\", \"WATER\",\n32\t            \"BUILD_PASTURE\", \"BUILD_COOP\", \"PLACE\", \"PICKUP\", \"DROP\", \"FERTILIZE\", \"DIG\", \"PASS\")\n33\t\n34\t\n35\tdef seats(path):\n36\t    \"\"\"Yield `(seat, final_money, per-seat plan facts)` for one episode file.\"\"\"\n37\t    with open(path) as fh:\n38\t        ep = json.load(fh)\n39\t    rewards = ep.get(\"rewards\") or []\n40\t    steps = ep.get(\"steps\") or []\n41\t    for seat in range(len(rewards)):\n42\t        money = rewards[seat]\n43\t        if money is None:\n44\t            continue\n45\t        land, animals, hires, wheat = [], [], collections.Counter(), collections.Counter()\n46\t        sells = collections.defaultdict(collections.Counter)\n47\t        ops = collections.Counter()\n48\t        for t, step in enumerate(steps):\n49\t            if seat >= len(step):\n50\t                continue\n51\t            action = step[seat].get(\"action\")\n52\t            if not isinstance(action, dict):\n53\t                continue\n54\t            day = t // TURNS_PER_DAY\n55\t            for order in action.get(\"market\") or []:\n56\t                if not order:\n57\t                    continue\n58\t                op = order[0]\n59\t                if op in (\"BUY_LAND\", \"BUID_LAND\"):\n60\t                    land.append(day)\n61\t                elif op == \"HIRE\":\n62\t                    hires[day] += 1\n63\t                elif op == \"BUY_ANIMAL\" and len(order) >= 2:\n64\t                    animals.append((day, order[1]))\n65\t                elif op == \"BUY_PRODUCT\" and len(order) >= 3 and order[1] == \"WHEAT\":\n66\t                    wheat[day] += int(order[2])\n67\t                elif op == \"SELL\" and len(order) >= 3:\n68\t                    sells[order[1]][day] += int(order[2])\n69\t            units = [action.get(\"farmer\")] + list(action.get(\"hands\") or [])\n70\t            for u in units:\n71\t                if u:\n72\t                    ops[u[0]] += 1\n73\t        yield seat, money, {\n74\t            \"land_days\": land, \"animals\": animals, \"hires\": hires,\n75\t            \"wheat\": wheat, \"sells\": sells, \"ops\": ops,\n76\t        }\n77\t\n78\t\n79\tdef main():\n80\t    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n81\t    ap.add_argument(\"--top\", type=int, default=12)\n82\t    ap.add_argument(\"--glob\", default=\"data/*.json\")\n83\t    a = ap.parse_args()\n84\t\n85\t    rows = []\n86\t    for path in sorted(glob.glob(a.glob)):\n87\t        try:\n88\t            for seat, money, facts in seats(path):\n89\t                rows.append((money, pathlib.Path(path).stem, seat, facts))\n90\t        except (json.JSONDecodeError, KeyError, TypeError):\n91\t            continue\n92\t    rows.sort(key=lambda r: -r[0])\n93\t    print(f\"{len(rows)} seats across {len(set(r[1] for r in rows))} episodes\\n\")\n94\t\n95\t    print(\"=\" * 108)\n96\t    print(f\"{'money':>9} {'episode':>10} {'st':>2} | {'land days':>14} {'animals':>26} \"\n97\t          f\"{'hire/day':>9} {'wheat':>7} {'FEED':>6}\")\n98\t    print(\"=\" * 108)\n99\t    for money, ep, seat, f in rows[:a.top]:\n100\t        ld = \",\".join(str(d) for d in sorted(set(f[\"land_days\"]))) or \"-\"\n101\t        an = collections.Counter(name for _d, name in f[\"animals\"])\n102\t        an_s = \" \".join(f\"{n}x{name[:2]}@d{min(d for d, m in f['animals'] if m == name)}\"\n103\t                        for name, n in an.items()) or \"-\"\n104\t        hires = sum(f[\"hires\"].values())\n105\t        print(f\"{money:>9,.0f} {ep:>10} {seat:>2} | {ld:>14} {an_s:>26} \"\n106\t              f\"{hires/30:>9.1f} {sum(f['wheat'].values()):>7} {f['ops']['FEED']:>6}\")\n107\t\n108\t    # ---- aggregate: what do the winners do that we do not ----------------\n109\t    top = rows[:a.top]\n110\t    bot = rows[-a.top:]\n111\t    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 108)\n112\t    print(f\"{'':22} {'TOP ' + str(a.top):>14} {'BOTTOM ' + str(a.top):>14}\")\n113\t    print(\"=\" * 108)\n114\t\n115\t    def agg(group, fn):\n116\t        return sum(fn(f) for _m, _e, _s, f in group) / max(len(group), 1)\n117\t\n118\t    metrics = [\n119\t        (\"final money\", lambda f: 0),          # filled below from the money column\n120\t        (\"land parcels bought\", lambda f: len(set(f[\"land_days\"]))),\n121\t        (\"first land purchase day\", lambda f: min(set(f[\"land_days\"])) if f[\"land_days\"] else 30),\n122\t        (\"animals bought\", lambda f: len(f[\"animals\"])),\n123\t        (\"hires per day\", lambda f: sum(f[\"hires\"].values()) / 30),\n124\t        (\"wheat bought\", lambda f: sum(f[\"wheat\"].values())),\n125\t        (\"FEED ops\", lambda f: f[\"ops\"][\"FEED\"]),\n126\t        (\"CARE ops\", lambda f: f[\"ops\"][\"CARE\"]),\n127\t        (\"COLLECT_FERTILIZER ops\", lambda f: f[\"ops\"][\"COLLECT_FERTILIZER\"]),\n128\t        (\"FERTILIZE ops\", lambda f: f[\"ops\"][\"FERTILIZE\"]),\n129\t        (\"PLANT ops\", lambda f: f[\"ops\"][\"PLANT\"]),\n130\t        (\"HARVEST ops\", lambda f: f[\"ops\"][\"HARVEST\"]),\n131\t        (\"PASS ops\", lambda f: f[\"ops\"][\"PASS\"]),\n132\t    ]\n133\t    print(f\"{'final money':22} {sum(m for m,_,_,_ in top)/len(top):>14,.0f} \"\n134\t          f\"{sum(m for m,_,_,_ in bot)/len(bot):>14,.0f}\")\n135\t    for name, fn in metrics[1:]:\n136\t        print(f\"{name:22} {agg(top, fn):>14.1f} {agg(bot, fn):>14.1f}\")\n137\t\n138\t    # ---- sell cadence: is it daily, or concentrated late? ----------------\n139\t    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 108)\n140\t    print(\"SELL volume by day-decile, top seats (does the town's day-10 / day-20 demand step show?)\")\n141\t    print(\"=\" * 108)\n142\t    for money, ep, seat, f in top[:6]:\n143\t        by_day = collections.Counter()\n144\t        for _product, days in f[\"sells\"].items():\n145\t            for d, n in days.items():\n146\t                by_day[d] += n\n147\t        total = sum(by_day.values()) or 1\n148\t        thirds = [sum(n for d, n in by_day.items() if lo <= d < hi) / total\n149\t                  for lo, hi in ((0, 10), (10, 20), (20, 30))]\n150\t        bar = \"  \".join(f\"d{lo}-{hi}: {p:5.1%}\" for (lo, hi), p in\n151\t                        zip(((0, 10), (10, 20), (20, 30)), thirds))\n152\t        print(f\"{money:>9,.0f} {ep:>10} {seat:>2} | total {total:>5} units | {bar}\")\n153\t\n154\t\n155\tif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n156\t    raise SystemExit(main())\n157\t"}]},"uuid":"ded90ef1-6f09-4ba9-8eea-3292306071a7","timestamp":"2026-08-04T09:47:03.772Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"868b6be4-c460-43db-b186-881d7390ae0c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture","sessionId":"6e91a562-2c58-4e1d-8c6f-dd43634ac84a","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"humming-hopping-marshmallow"}

{"parentUuid":"b9541225-6a8b-4708-a031-48ed4e37bfe3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5bffb09b-9cfa-457a-b68e-4ca7752df84e","agentId":"af0fe4f4b572e87a8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Pxs6YTwpqmJ6a9kMHKCPMP","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# What a $145k agent actually does — measured from real episode replays\n2\t\n3\tFirst teardown of real leaderboard episodes in this project.\n4\tEverything here is counted from the replay JSON, not inferred from a notebook or a forum post.\n5\t\n6\tSource: `kaggle/kaggriculture-episodes-index` -> `kaggriculture-episodes-2026-08-02`, four episodes at\n7\t~27 MB each.\n8\tScript: `scratchpad/analyse_eps.py`, output in `data/logs/episode_analysis.log`.\n9\t\n10\t**Alex said the leaders were more diverse and I treated it as unverified.\n11\tThe data says he was right, and the margin is not small.**\n12\t\n13\t---\n14\t\n15\t## The headline: they are ~50/50, not specialists\n16\t\n17\t`senkin13`, episode 89541819, final reward **$145,034**, gross sales **$220,542**:\n18\t\n19\t| product | revenue | share | units |\n20\t|---|---:|---:|---:|\n21\t| MILK | $58,505 | 26.5% | 235 |\n22\t| STRAWBERRY | $42,942 | 19.5% | 309 |\n23\t| WOOL | $40,112 | 18.2% | 190 |\n24\t| WHEAT | $38,632 | 17.5% | 855 |\n25\t| MELON | $24,489 | 11.1% | 123 |\n26\t| FERTILIZER | $14,698 | 6.7% | 271 |\n27\t| EGG | $769 | 0.3% | 12 |\n28\t| CARROT | $395 | 0.2% | 10 |\n29\t\n30\t**Animal products 45.1% · crops 48.3% · fertiliser 6.7%.**\n31\t\n32\tThree crops carrying real weight, two animal species, and a fertiliser side-business.\n33\tThis is not the \"83% from milk and wool\" single-episode figure in `CLAUDE.md` -- that was one agent in\n34\tone episode and reads as an animal specialist.\n35\tA genuinely mixed economy is a different target.\n36\t\n37\t## Four things that contradict what we have been building\n38\t\n39\t### 1. Melon is not the meta crop\n40\t\n41\tStrawberry out-earns melon nearly 2:1 ($42,942 against $24,489) on 309 units against 123.\n42\tSeeds bought: `STRAWBERRY 44, WHEAT 34, MELON 21`.\n43\t\n44\tOur policy is **93% melon** (`crop MELON:0.93`), and `jax_heuristic`'s `MELON_TILES` is the constant we\n45\twere about to raise.\n46\t`docs/public_meta_2026-08-03.md` already said \"melon is the wrong crop\" from notebook reading; this is\n47\tthe measured confirmation.\n48\t\n49\t### 2. Wheat is an input, not a crop to specialise in\n50\t\n51\tThey **buy 1,055 units of wheat for $47,594** and **sell 855 units for $38,632**.\n52\tWheat is bought to FEED animals -- `jax_masks.py:96` requires `carrying_wheat` -- and the surplus is\n53\ttraded.\n54\tNet cash on wheat is negative; the return arrives as milk and wool.\n55\t\n56\tA cash-only reward view would score that wheat purchase as a $47k loss, which is exactly the objection\n57\tthat produced `kagg/rl/net_worth.py`.\n58\t\n59\t### 3. Fertiliser is a revenue stream, not an expense\n60\t\n61\t271 units sold for **$14,698**, sourced from `COLLECT_FERTILIZER` -- no action cost, one per animal per\n62\tday (`jax_masks.py:98`, gated on `has_animal`).\n63\tFree money, and only available if you own animals.\n64\t\n65\tOurs **buys** fertiliser on 19.3% of turns, sells on 24.5%, and applies it **zero** times in 8 full\n66\tseasons (`FERTILIZE: raw 0`). We pay the spread for what they harvest free.\n67\t\n68\t### 4. Cows and sheep. No geese.\n69\t\n70\t`COW 8, SHEEP 6, GOOSE 0`. `BUILD_PASTURE 14, BUILD_COOP 0`.\n71\tAlso **304 hires** and 2 land purchases.\n72\t\n73\tGoose is the cheapest animal ($300) and the one our own toy and agents reach for first.\n74\tThe measured meta skips it entirely.\n75\t\n76\t## The op census, theirs against ours\n77\t\n78\t| op | them | us |\n79\t|---|---:|---:|\n80\t| FEED | 4.45% (324) | **0.00%** |\n81\t| CARE | 4.27% (311) | **0.01%** |\n82\t| COLLECT_FERTILIZER | 4.38% (319) | **0.00%** |\n83\t| FERTILIZE | 1.24% (90) | **0.00%** |\n84\t| WATER | 10.9% | 19.0% |\n85\t| PASS | 7.0% | 5.6% |\n86\t\n87\tEvery one of the four ops carrying their animal economy is at literal zero in ours, and we water\n88\troughly twice as much.\n89\t`CLAUDE.md` already measured WATER at **+0.100** correlation with money against FEED **+0.452** and\n90\tCARE **+0.441** over 312 seats; this is the same finding from the other direction.\n91\t\n92\t## Two caveats, stated because they change what the numbers mean\n93\t\n94\t**This is a mirror match.**\n95\t`senkin13` and `syouya tobita` submitted **identical action dicts on 536 of 720 turns** -- verified, not\n96\tassumed. They are near-identical forks of one strategy. Where they diverge is only the market list, and\n97\tseat 0 gets orders filled that seat 1 does not, which is the seat-0 market priority our own sim models.\n98\t`CLAUDE.md`: *\"A mirror match is not a best response.\"* So this is one popular strategy well executed,\n99\tnot the field's answer to it.\n100\t\n101\t**These are not the top five.**\n102\tThe 2026-08-03 leaderboard reads `Dmitry Larko 2942.8, li jiaqi77 2933.3, manual player 2911.7,\n103\tIY 2869.2, Juste Me`. Neither name here appears in it.\n104\tWhat is measured above is a strong mid-field agent at ~$145k. The leaders may differ again, and the\n105\tepisodes for those names still need pulling.\n106\t\n107\t## Rating inflation, from the index manifest\n108\t\n109\t| date | episodes | top avg score | median avg score |\n110\t|---|---:|---:|---:|\n111\t| 2026-07-30 | 864 | 1,152.4 | 669.8 |\n112\t| 2026-07-31 | 928 | 1,427.0 | 1,175.3 |\n113\t| 2026-08-01 | 829 | 1,580.6 | 1,348.2 |\n114\t| 2026-08-02 | 793 | **2,627.2** | **2,319.2** |\n115\t\n116\tThe **median** competitor went 670 -> 2,319 in four days, and the top-to-median gap narrowed from 483\n117\tto 308 points.\n118\tThe field is converging upward fast, so any target read off a notebook from a few days ago is stale --\n119\tincluding the $90k-$123k range in `public_meta_2026-08-03.md`.\n120\t\n121\t## What follows\n122\t\n123\t1. **Pull episodes for the top-five names.** This teardown measured whoever was in the first listing\n124\t   page. The leaders are the actual reference.\n125\t2. **Strawberry, not melon.** Re-measure `MELON_TILES` against a strawberry allocation, with weeds on\n126\t   (the old constant sweep is void -- see `jax_frontier.py`).\n127\t3. **The animal economy is worth ~45% of a $220k gross and we score 0.00% on all four of its ops.**\n128\t   Ours is not refusing a marginal option; it has never seen the option work. No scripted agent we own\n129\t   beats `farm`'s $28,168 using animals, so there is nothing in the pool to learn it from.\n130\t4. **COLLECT_FERTILIZER is free money nobody in our pool takes**, already noted in\n131\t   `jax_frontier.py`'s docstring and still unbuilt.\n132\t\n133\tRelated: [[ideas]], [[public_meta_2026-08-03]], [[fable_review_animal_chain_2026-08-03]].\n134\t"}]},"uuid":"23639821-7586-488e-b366-10008a2f6608","timestamp":"2026-08-04T09:47:04.144Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b9541225-6a8b-4708-a031-48ed4e37bfe3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alexchilton/DataspellProjects/kaggriculture","sessionId":"6e91a562-2c58-4e1d-8c6f-dd43634ac84a","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"humming-hopping-marshmallow"}
